```python
import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from vid2sub.subs import translate_shape as ts
from vid2sub.subs.model import Cue

IDS = [f"s{i}" for i in range(100)]
SOURCE = {cid: Cue(id=cid, start_ms=i * 2000, end_ms=i * 2000 + 1500, text=f"text {i}")
          for i, cid in enumerate(IDS)}


def test_batches_cover_every_id_once_with_read_only_context_on_both_sides():
    batches = ts.plan_batches(IDS, size=40, context=5)
    assert [len(b.ids) for b in batches] == [40, 40, 20]
    assert batches[0].before == () and batches[0].after == tuple(IDS[40:45])
    assert batches[1].before == tuple(IDS[35:40])


def test_selected_ids_batch_in_contiguous_runs():
    batches = ts.plan_batches(IDS, ["s3", "s4", "s9"], size=40)
    assert [b.ids for b in batches] == [("s3", "s4"), ("s9",)]


def test_splitting_a_batch_halves_it():
    batch = ts.plan_batches(IDS[:10], size=40)[0]
    halves = ts.split_batch(batch, IDS[:10])
    assert [len(h.ids) for h in halves] == [5, 5]


def test_the_batch_schema_admits_only_this_batchs_ids():
    model = ts.batch_model(["a", "b"])
    model.model_validate({"items": [{"src": ["a", "b"], "text": "AB"}]})
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        model.model_validate({"items": [{"src": ["z"], "text": "?"}]})
    schema = model.model_json_schema()
    assert "a" in str(schema) and "b" in str(schema)


def test_an_item_may_merge_adjacent_cues_but_must_cover_all_in_order():
    ok = [ts.Item(("s0", "s1"), "Both"), ts.Item(("s2",), "Third")]
    assert ts.validate_partition(ok, ["s0", "s1", "s2"]) == ok
    for bad in ([ts.Item(("s1",), "x"), ts.Item(("s0",), "y"), ts.Item(("s2",), "z")],
                [ts.Item(("s0",), "x"), ts.Item(("s2",), "z")],
                [ts.Item(("s0", "s1", "s2"), "  ")]):
        with pytest.raises(ts.PartitionError):
            ts.validate_partition(bad, ["s0", "s1", "s2"])


def test_assembled_translation_spans_its_source_cues_and_records_their_hash():
    taken: set[str] = set()
    out = ts.assemble([ts.Item(("s0", "s1"), "One long English line")], SOURCE, "en", taken)
    assert (out[0].start_ms, out[0].end_ms) == (0, 3500)
    assert out[0].src == ("s0", "s1")
    assert out[0].src_sha == ts.src_sha(["text 0", "text 1"])
    assert out[0].id in taken


def test_limits_follow_reading_speed_and_line_width():
    limits = ts.item_limits([SOURCE["s0"]], "en")
    assert limits["s0"] == 30  # 20 cps for 1.5 s; two lines of 42 would allow 84


def test_a_translation_goes_stale_when_its_source_changes_or_disappears():
    taken: set[str] = set()
    trans = ts.assemble([ts.Item(("s0",), "Zero"), ts.Item(("s1",), "One")], SOURCE, "en", taken)
    changed = dict(SOURCE)
    changed["s0"] = SOURCE["s0"].model_copy(update={"text": "edited"})
    del changed["s1"]
    assert ts.stale_ids(trans, changed) == {trans[0].id, trans[1].id}
    assert ts.stale_ids(trans, SOURCE) == set()
    assert ts.untranslated(IDS[:3], trans) == ["s2"]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/subs/translate_shape.py`:
```python
"""The shape of translation: batches, per-batch schemas, partitions and staleness (spec §13).

Pure, so the CLI, the editor (M2) and Pages (M4) share it. A batch is a run of source cues that
are contiguous in the track, with read-only context on both sides. The model answers with items,
each covering one or more adjacent source cues, because Japanese→English often reorders across
cues. The schema is built per batch with the batch's ids as a Literal, so llama.cpp's grammar can
only produce real ids; the partition check then makes sure every id is covered once, in order.
A translation records its source cues' ids and a hash of their text, so a source edit marks it
stale.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import math
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, create_model

from vid2sub.subs import langs, naming
from vid2sub.subs.model import Cue, new_cue_id
from vid2sub.subs.rules import rules_for, width_chars
from vid2sub.subs.text import canonical, strip_line_ends, wrap


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Batch:
    ids: tuple[str, ...]
    before: tuple[str, ...]
    after: tuple[str, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Item:
    src: tuple[str, ...]
    text: str


class PartitionError(ValueError):
    pass


def plan_batches(all_ids: Sequence[str], selected: Sequence[str] | None = None, *,
                 size: int = 40, context: int = 5) -> list[Batch]:
    order = {cid: i for i, cid in enumerate(all_ids)}
    wanted = None if selected is None else set(selected)
    chosen = [cid for cid in all_ids if wanted is None or cid in wanted]
    runs: list[list[str]] = []
    cur: list[str] = []
    for cid in chosen:
        if cur and order[cid] != order[cur[-1]] + 1:
            runs.append(cur)
            cur = []
        cur.append(cid)
    if cur:
        runs.append(cur)
    batches = []
    for run in runs:
        for k in range(0, len(run), size):
            part = run[k:k + size]
            first, last = order[part[0]], order[part[-1]]
            batches.append(Batch(tuple(part), tuple(all_ids[max(0, first - context):first]),
                                 tuple(all_ids[last + 1:last + 1 + context])))
    return batches


def split_batch(batch: Batch, all_ids: Sequence[str], *, context: int = 5) -> list[Batch]:
    if len(batch.ids) <= 1:
        return [batch]
    mid = math.ceil(len(batch.ids) / 2)
    return (plan_batches(all_ids, batch.ids[:mid], size=mid, context=context)
            + plan_batches(all_ids, batch.ids[mid:], size=mid, context=context))


def batch_model(ids: Sequence[str]) -> type[BaseModel]:
    src = Literal[tuple(ids)]  # type: ignore[valid-type]
    item = create_model("TranslationItem", src=(list[src], Field(min_length=1)),
                        text=(str, ...))
    return create_model("TranslationBatch", items=(list[item], Field(min_length=1)))


def items_from(result: BaseModel) -> list[Item]:
    return [Item(tuple(it.src), it.text) for it in result.items]  # type: ignore[attr-defined]


def validate_partition(items: Sequence[Item], ids: Sequence[str]) -> list[Item]:
    flat = [cid for it in items for cid in it.src]
    if flat != list(ids):
        raise PartitionError("the items don't cover the batch's cues exactly once, in order")
    if any(not canonical(it.text) for it in items):
        raise PartitionError("a translation item is empty")
    return list(items)


def _target(target_track: str):
    info = naming.track(target_track)
    cjk = info.lang in langs.LANGS and langs.get(info.lang).cjk
    return info, rules_for(info.lang), cjk


def item_limits(cues: Sequence[Cue], target_track: str) -> dict[str, int]:
    _, rules, cjk = _target(target_track)
    by_width = rules.max_lines * width_chars(rules, cjk)
    return {c.id: max(1, int(min(by_width, rules.cps * (c.end_ms - c.start_ms) / 1000)))
            for c in cues}


def src_sha(texts: Sequence[str]) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256("\x1f".join(texts).encode()).hexdigest()[:12]


def assemble(items: Sequence[Item], source: Mapping[str, Cue], target_track: str,
             taken: set[str]) -> list[Cue]:
    info, rules, _ = _target(target_track)
    out = []
    for it in items:
        srcs = [source[cid] for cid in it.src]
        text = canonical(it.text)
        lines = wrap(text, rules.max_width, rules.max_lines) or [text.replace("\n", " ")]
        if info.lang in ("zh", "yue"):
            lines = strip_line_ends(lines)
        out.append(Cue(id=new_cue_id(taken), start_ms=srcs[0].start_ms,
                       end_ms=srcs[-1].end_ms, text="\n".join(lines), src=it.src,
                       src_sha=src_sha([c.text for c in srcs])))
    return out


def stale_ids(translation: Sequence[Cue], source: Mapping[str, Cue]) -> set[str]:
    stale = set()
    for c in translation:
        if not c.src or any(cid not in source for cid in c.src):
            stale.add(c.id)
        elif c.src_sha != src_sha([source[cid].text for cid in c.src]):
            stale.add(c.id)
    return stale


def untranslated(source_ids: Sequence[str], translation: Sequence[Cue]) -> list[str]:
    covered = {cid for c in translation for cid in (c.src or ())}
    return [cid for cid in source_ids if cid not in covered]
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Word, Segment` (Task 4); `is_cjk, NO_LINE_END` (Task 3); `langs` (Task 2).
- Produces: `vid2sub.parse.ParsedChunk(segments: tuple[Segment, ...], language: str | None,
  language_probs: dict[str, float])`; `vid2sub.parse.ParseError`;
  `words.ms(seconds) -> int | None`;
  `words.tokens_to_words(tokens: Sequence[tuple[str, int|None, int|None, float|None]], *,
  start_ms=0) -> list[Word]`;
  `words.fill_untimed(raw, start_ms=0) -> list[Word]`;
  `words.attach_punctuation(text, units: Sequence[tuple[str, int|None, int|None]], *,
  start_ms=0) -> list[Word] | None`;
  `whispercpp.parse_whispercpp(data: Any) -> ParsedChunk` (chunk-relative times).

whisper.cpp v1.9.4's `verbose_json` (from `examples/server/server.cpp`): top level `task`,
`language` (a full name, "japanese"), `duration`, `text`, `segments`, and, unless disabled,
`detected_language`, `detected_language_probability`, `language_probabilities` (ISO codes →
probability). Each segment: `id`, `text`, `start`/`end` (seconds), `tokens`, `words`,
`temperature`, `avg_logprob`, `no_speech_prob`. Each word is a BPE token merged only to complete
a UTF-8 character: `word`, `start`, `end` (seconds), `t_dtw` (raw 10 ms units, −1 without DTW),
`probability`.

```python
from vid2sub.parse.words import attach_punctuation, fill_untimed, ms, tokens_to_words


def test_seconds_become_milliseconds_and_junk_becomes_none():
    assert ms(1.2345) == 1234 or ms(1.2345) == 1235
    assert ms(None) is None and ms(-1) is None and ms("x") is None


def test_english_tokens_merge_into_words_on_the_leading_space():
    words = tokens_to_words([(" Hel", 0, 200, 0.9), ("lo", 200, 400, 0.5), (",", 400, 420, 0.8),
                             (" world", 500, 900, 0.95)])
    assert [w.text for w in words] == [" Hello,", " world"]
    assert (words[0].start_ms, words[0].end_ms, words[0].prob) == (0, 420, 0.5)


def test_cjk_tokens_stay_separate_words_and_latin_inside_cjk_keeps_its_space():
    words = tokens_to_words([("我", 0, 100, None), ("哋", 100, 200, None), (" shop", 200, 300, None),
                             ("ping", 300, 400, None), ("啦", 400, 500, None)])
    assert [w.text for w in words] == ["我", "哋", " shopping", "啦"]


def test_untimed_words_sit_at_the_previous_end_and_are_marked():
    words = fill_untimed([["a", 100, 200, None], ["b", None, None, None], ["c", 300, 250, None]],
                         start_ms=50)

        (100, 200, True), (200, 200, False), (200, 200, False)]


def test_punctuation_and_spaces_from_the_text_attach_to_aligned_units():
    words = attach_punctuation("你好，世界。", [("你", 0, 1), ("好", 1, 2), ("世", 2, 3), ("界", 3, 4)])
    assert [w.text for w in words] == ["你", "好，", "世", "界。"]
    words = attach_punctuation(" Hello, world.", [("hello", 0, 5), ("world", 6, 9)])
    assert [w.text for w in words] == [" Hello,", " world."]
    words = attach_punctuation("他说：“你好”", [("他", 0, 1), ("说", 1, 2), ("你", 2, 3), ("好", 3, 4)])
    assert [w.text for w in words] == ["他", "说：", "“你", "好”"]


def test_a_unit_missing_from_the_text_gives_none():
    assert attach_punctuation("abc", [("zzz", 0, 1)]) is None
```

```python
import pytest

from vid2sub.parse import ParseError
from vid2sub.parse.whispercpp import parse_whispercpp


def word(text, start, end, dtw=-1, p=0.9):
    return {"word": text, "start": start, "end": end, "t_dtw": dtw, "probability": p}


EN = {"task": "transcribe", "language": "english", "duration": 3.0, "text": " Hello, world.",
      "segments": [{"id": 0, "text": " Hello, world.", "start": 0.0, "end": 2.0,
                    "avg_logprob": -0.2, "no_speech_prob": 0.01,
                    "words": [word(" Hel", 0.0, 0.2), word("lo", 0.2, 0.4), word(",", 0.4, 0.42),
                              word(" world", 0.5, 0.9), word(".", 0.9, 0.95)]}],
      "language_probabilities": {"en": 0.98, "de": 0.01}}


def test_a_verbose_json_response_becomes_segments_of_words_in_milliseconds():
    parsed = parse_whispercpp(EN)
    assert parsed.language == "en"
    assert parsed.language_probs["en"] == 0.98
    seg = parsed.segments[0]
    assert [w.text for w in seg.words] == [" Hello,", " world."]
    assert seg.text == " Hello, world." and seg.end_ms == 2000
    assert seg.avg_logprob == -0.2


def test_dtw_times_are_used_when_the_server_sends_them():
    data = {"language": "japanese", "segments": [{"text": "こんにちは", "start": 0.0, "end": 1.0,
            "words": [word("こん", 0.0, 0.5, dtw=12), word("にちは", 0.5, 1.0, dtw=55)]}]}
    seg = parse_whispercpp(data).segments[0]
    assert [(w.start_ms, w.end_ms) for w in seg.words] == [(120, 550), (550, 1000)]


def test_a_broken_utf8_token_makes_the_segment_unaligned():
    data = {"language": "chinese", "segments": [{"text": "你好", "start": 0.0, "end": 1.0,
            "words": [word("�", 0.0, 0.5), word("好", 0.5, 1.0)]}]}
    seg = parse_whispercpp(data).segments[0]
    assert seg.words == () and seg.text == "你好"


def test_cantonese_is_recognised_by_its_full_name():
    assert parse_whispercpp({"language": "cantonese", "segments": []}).language == "yue"


def test_something_that_isnt_verbose_json_is_refused():
    with pytest.raises(ParseError):
        parse_whispercpp({"text": "plain"})
```

`src/vid2sub/parse/__init__.py`:
```python
"""Engine responses → vid2sub's one word shape (spec §9.2). Pure: no I/O, runs in Pyodide later.

Every engine's response becomes a ParsedChunk: segments of words in Whisper's convention (a
leading space marks a word boundary; joining a segment's words gives its text), with times in
milliseconds relative to the chunk.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from vid2sub.subs.model import Segment


class ParseError(ValueError):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ParsedChunk:
    segments: tuple[Segment, ...]
    language: str | None
    language_probs: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
```

`src/vid2sub/parse/words.py`:
```python
"""Helpers shared by the parsers: tokens into words, untimed words, and punctuation (spec §9.2).

whisper.cpp's "words" are BPE tokens, so they're merged into words on the leading space; a CJK
token is a word of its own. Aligners (Qwen's, OpenAI's word list) return units without the
text's spaces and punctuation, so `attach_punctuation` maps them back: closing punctuation joins
the word before it, a space and any opening punctuation join the word after. A word with no time
sits at the previous word's end and is marked untimed (recordings' rule).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from typing import Any

from vid2sub.subs.model import Word
from vid2sub.subs.text import NO_LINE_END, is_cjk

RawToken = tuple[str, int | None, int | None, float | None]


def ms(seconds: Any) -> int | None:
    if seconds is None:
        return None
    try:
        value = float(seconds)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    return None if value < 0 else round(value * 1000)


def fill_untimed(raw: Sequence[Sequence[Any]], start_ms: int = 0) -> list[Word]:
    words: list[Word] = []
    prev_end = start_ms
    for text, start, end, prob in raw:
        if start is None or end is None or end < start:
            words.append(Word(text=text, start_ms=prev_end, end_ms=prev_end, prob=prob,
                              timed=False))
        else:
            words.append(Word(text=text, start_ms=start, end_ms=end, prob=prob))
            prev_end = end
    return words


def tokens_to_words(tokens: Sequence[RawToken], *, start_ms: int = 0) -> list[Word]:
    merged: list[list[Any]] = []
    for text, start, end, prob in tokens:
        if not text or not text.strip():
            continue
        head = text.lstrip()
        last = merged[-1][0].rstrip() if merged else ""
        starts_word = (not merged or text[0].isspace() or is_cjk(head[0])
                       or (bool(last) and is_cjk(last[-1])))
        if starts_word:
            merged.append([text, start, end, prob])
            continue
        current = merged[-1]
        current[0] += text
        if end is not None:
            current[2] = end
        if prob is not None:
            current[3] = prob if current[3] is None else min(current[3], prob)
    return fill_untimed(merged, start_ms)


def attach_punctuation(text: str, units: Sequence[tuple[str, int | None, int | None]], *,
                       start_ms: int = 0) -> list[Word] | None:
    lowered = text.lower()
    haystack = lowered if len(lowered) == len(text) else text
    raw: list[list[Any]] = []
    pos = 0
    for utext, start, end in units:
        needle = utext.strip()
        if not needle:
            continue
        found = haystack.find(needle.lower() if haystack is lowered else needle, pos)
        if found < 0:
            return None
        gap = text[pos:found]
        lead = ""
        if gap:
            space = next((i for i, ch in enumerate(gap) if ch.isspace()), None)
            if space is not None:
                closing, lead = gap[:space], " " + gap[space:].strip()
            else:
                k = len(gap)
                while k > 0 and gap[k - 1] in NO_LINE_END:
                    k -= 1
                closing, lead = gap[:k], gap[k:]
            if closing:
                if raw:
                    raw[-1][0] += closing
                else:
                    lead = closing + lead
        raw.append([lead + text[found:found + len(needle)], start, end, None])
        pos = found + len(needle)
    if not raw:
        return None
    raw[-1][0] += text[pos:].rstrip()
    return fill_untimed(raw, start_ms)
```

`src/vid2sub/parse/whispercpp.py`:
```python
"""whisper.cpp v1.9.4 server `verbose_json` → a ParsedChunk (spec §9.2).

Its words are BPE tokens (merged here); its top-level `language` is a full name, while
`language_probabilities` is keyed by ISO code; `t_dtw` is in raw 10 ms units and, when the server
runs with DTW (local-ai item 5), is better than the token's own start/end, so it wins. A token
with U+FFFD (a CJK character split across tokens, whisper.cpp issue #399) makes the segment
unaligned rather than wrong.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from vid2sub.parse import ParseError, ParsedChunk
from vid2sub.parse.words import ms, tokens_to_words
from vid2sub.subs import langs
from vid2sub.subs.model import Segment


def parse_whispercpp(data: Any) -> ParsedChunk:
    if not isinstance(data, dict) or not isinstance(data.get("segments"), list):
        raise ParseError("not a whisper.cpp verbose_json response")
    probs = {str(k): float(v) for k, v in (data.get("language_probabilities") or {}).items()}
    language = langs.from_whisper_full(str(data.get("language") or ""))
    if language is None and probs:
        language = max(probs, key=probs.__getitem__)
    segments = []
    for seg in data["segments"]:
        s_start = ms(seg.get("start")) or 0
        s_end = ms(seg.get("end"))
        s_end = s_start if s_end is None or s_end < s_start else s_end
        raw = [w for w in seg.get("words") or [] if isinstance(w, dict)]
        dtw = [w.get("t_dtw") for w in raw]
        use_dtw = bool(raw) and all(isinstance(t, (int, float)) and t >= 0 for t in dtw)
        tokens = []
        for k, w in enumerate(raw):
            if use_dtw:
                start = round(dtw[k] * 10)
                end = round(dtw[k + 1] * 10) if k + 1 < len(raw) else s_end
            else:
                start, end = ms(w.get("start")), ms(w.get("end"))
            tokens.append((str(w.get("word", "")), start, end, w.get("probability")))
        words = tokens_to_words(tokens, start_ms=s_start)
        if any("�" in w.text for w in words):
            words = []
        text = "".join(w.text for w in words) if words else str(seg.get("text", ""))
        segments.append(Segment(text=text, start_ms=s_start, end_ms=s_end, words=tuple(words),
                                avg_logprob=seg.get("avg_logprob"),
                                no_speech_prob=seg.get("no_speech_prob"),
                                compression_ratio=seg.get("compression_ratio")))
    return ParsedChunk(tuple(segments), language, probs)
```

### Task 14: The OpenAI and Qwen parsers

**Files:**
- Create: `src/vid2sub/parse/openai.py`, `src/vid2sub/parse/qwen.py`
- Test: `tests/test_parse_openai_qwen.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ParsedChunk, ParseError` and `words.ms, words.attach_punctuation` (Task 13).
- Produces: `parse_openai(data: Any) -> ParsedChunk`;
  `parse_qwen(result: Mapping[str, Any], *, duration_ms: int) -> ParsedChunk`.

OpenAI's `whisper-1` `verbose_json` with `timestamp_granularities=["word","segment"]`: top-level
`language` (full name), `duration`, `text`, `segments` (`start`, `end`, `text`, `avg_logprob`,
`compression_ratio`, `no_speech_prob`, …) and top-level `words` (`word`, `start`, `end`, without
spaces or punctuation). `mlx-qwen3-asr`'s result: `text`, `language` (a name, "Chinese"),
`segments` = word-level units `{"text", "start", "end"}` in seconds.

```python
from vid2sub.parse import ParseError
from vid2sub.parse.openai import parse_openai
from vid2sub.parse.qwen import parse_qwen

OPENAI = {
    "language": "english", "duration": 4.0, "text": "Hello, world. Bye.",
    "segments": [{"start": 0.0, "end": 2.0, "text": " Hello, world.", "avg_logprob": -0.1,
                  "compression_ratio": 1.2, "no_speech_prob": 0.0},
                 {"start": 2.0, "end": 4.0, "text": " Bye.", "avg_logprob": -0.3,
                  "compression_ratio": 1.0, "no_speech_prob": 0.0}],
    "words": [{"word": "Hello", "start": 0.1, "end": 0.5}, {"word": "world", "start": 0.6, "end": 1.0},
              {"word": "Bye", "start": 2.2, "end": 2.6}],
}


def test_openai_words_are_assigned_to_segments_with_the_texts_punctuation():
    parsed = parse_openai(OPENAI)
    assert parsed.language == "en"
    assert [w.text for w in parsed.segments[0].words] == [" Hello,", " world."]
    assert [w.text for w in parsed.segments[1].words] == [" Bye."]
    assert parsed.segments[0].compression_ratio == 1.2


def test_an_openai_response_without_segments_is_refused():
    with pytest.raises(ParseError):
        parse_openai({"text": "hi"})


def test_qwen_units_get_the_texts_punctuation_and_the_language_code():
    parsed = parse_qwen({"text": "你好，世界。", "language": "Chinese",
                         "segments": [{"text": "你", "start": 0.0, "end": 0.2},
                                      {"text": "好", "start": 0.2, "end": 0.4},
                                      {"text": "世", "start": 0.6, "end": 0.8},
                                      {"text": "界", "start": 0.8, "end": 1.0}]}, duration_ms=1500)
    assert parsed.language == "zh"
    seg = parsed.segments[0]
    assert [w.text for w in seg.words] == ["你", "好，", "世", "界。"]
    assert (seg.start_ms, seg.end_ms) == (0, 1000)


def test_qwen_text_that_doesnt_match_its_units_becomes_one_unaligned_segment():
    parsed = parse_qwen({"text": "abc", "language": "English",
                         "segments": [{"text": "zzz", "start": 0, "end": 1}]}, duration_ms=1500)
    assert parsed.segments[0].words == () and parsed.segments[0].end_ms == 1500


def test_empty_qwen_output_gives_no_segments():
    assert parse_qwen({"text": "", "language": "English", "segments": []},
                      duration_ms=1000).segments == ()
```

Expected: FAIL (`ModuleNotFoundError`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/parse/openai.py`:
```python
"""OpenAI `whisper-1` `verbose_json` → a ParsedChunk (spec §9.2).

OpenAI returns words at the top level, without spaces or punctuation, so each word is assigned
to the segment containing its midpoint and the segment's text supplies the punctuation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from vid2sub.parse import ParseError, ParsedChunk
from vid2sub.parse.words import attach_punctuation, ms
from vid2sub.subs import langs
from vid2sub.subs.model import Segment


def parse_openai(data: Any) -> ParsedChunk:
    if not isinstance(data, dict) or not isinstance(data.get("segments"), list):
        raise ParseError("not an OpenAI verbose_json response")
    raw_lang = str(data.get("language") or "")
    language = langs.from_whisper_full(raw_lang) or (raw_lang if raw_lang in langs.LANGS else None)
    words = [w for w in data.get("words") or [] if isinstance(w, dict)]
    segs = data["segments"]
    segments = []
    for i, seg in enumerate(segs):
        s_start = ms(seg.get("start")) or 0
        s_end = max(ms(seg.get("end")) or s_start, s_start)
        last = i == len(segs) - 1
        mine = []
        for w in words:
            ws, we = ms(w.get("start")), ms(w.get("end"))
            if ws is None or we is None:
                continue
            mid = (ws + we) // 2
            if s_start <= mid < s_end or (last and mid >= s_start):
                mine.append((str(w.get("word", "")), ws, we))
        text = str(seg.get("text", ""))
        aligned = attach_punctuation(text, mine, start_ms=s_start) if mine else None
        segments.append(Segment(
            text="".join(w.text for w in aligned) if aligned else text,
            start_ms=s_start, end_ms=s_end, words=tuple(aligned or ()),
            avg_logprob=seg.get("avg_logprob"), no_speech_prob=seg.get("no_speech_prob"),
            compression_ratio=seg.get("compression_ratio")))
    return ParsedChunk(tuple(segments), language, {language: 1.0} if language else {})
```

`src/vid2sub/parse/qwen.py`:
```python
"""mlx-qwen3-asr's result → a ParsedChunk (spec §9.2, §9.4).

Qwen3-ASR gives the text; its forced aligner gives word or character units with times. Whether
the units carry punctuation isn't documented (spec §29), so the text's punctuation is attached to
them either way. If the units can't be found in the text, the chunk becomes one unaligned segment
rather than wrong timings.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any

from vid2sub.parse import ParsedChunk
from vid2sub.parse.words import attach_punctuation, ms
from vid2sub.subs import langs
from vid2sub.subs.model import Segment


def parse_qwen(result: Mapping[str, Any], *, duration_ms: int) -> ParsedChunk:
    text = str(result.get("text") or "")
    language = langs.from_qwen(str(result.get("language") or ""))
    probs = {language: 1.0} if language else {}
    units = [(str(u.get("text", "")), ms(u.get("start")), ms(u.get("end")))
             for u in result.get("segments") or [] if isinstance(u, Mapping)]
    words = attach_punctuation(text, units) if units and text.strip() else None
    if words:
        seg = Segment(text="".join(w.text for w in words), start_ms=words[0].start_ms,
                      end_ms=max(w.end_ms for w in words), words=tuple(words))
    elif text.strip():
        seg = Segment(text=text, start_ms=0, end_ms=duration_ms)
    else:
        return ParsedChunk((), language, probs)
    return ParsedChunk((seg,), language, probs)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/catalog.py`:
```python
"""The model catalog and its routing (spec §11).

Data, not code: each model family's languages, strengths, weak spots, where it runs and how to
install it. The UI's pickers and the README's model table come from it. `vid2sub.toml`'s
`[models.*]` entries point at catalog ids. Routing is an ordered preference per source language:
the first model available now wins, and among equals the Spark's (an endpoint) wins over the
Mac's (in-process). The Spark's translation default is Gemma 4, resident with two slots; the only
Qwen chat model there is Dan's coder (council, 2026-10-14). Pure.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

from vid2sub.subs import langs


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CatalogEntry:
    id: str
    task: Literal["transcribe", "translate"]
    title: str
    good_for: str
    weak: str
    runs_on: str
    install: str
    languages: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    max_chunk_s: float = 300.0
    supports_prompt: bool = True
    family: str = "whisper"
    cloud: bool = False
    default_model: str | None = None


CATALOG: dict[str, CatalogEntry] = {e.id: e for e in (
    CatalogEntry(
        "qwen3-asr-1.7b", "transcribe", "Qwen3-ASR 1.7B + ForcedAligner 0.6B",
        good_for="Mandarin and Cantonese by a wide margin (Qwen's numbers: WenetSpeech-Yue long "
                 "8.85 vs Whisper large-v3's 46.64); strong English; Hong Kong and Guangdong "
                 "accents; vocabulary context",
        weak="Japanese is supported but has no published benchmark",
        runs_on="Mac (mlx-qwen3-asr); the Spark once local-ai serves it",
        install="vid2sub[mac]", languages=("zh", "yue", "en", "ja", "ko"), max_chunk_s=180,
        family="qwen3-asr", default_model="Qwen/Qwen3-ASR-1.7B"),
    CatalogEntry(
        "qwen3-asr-0.6b", "transcribe", "Qwen3-ASR 0.6B + ForcedAligner 0.6B",
        good_for="the same languages as the 1.7B, lighter and faster",
        weak="less accurate than the 1.7B", runs_on="Mac (mlx-qwen3-asr)",
        install="vid2sub[mac]", languages=("zh", "yue", "en", "ja", "ko"), max_chunk_s=180,
        family="qwen3-asr", default_model="Qwen/Qwen3-ASR-0.6B"),
    CatalogEntry(
        "whisper-large-v3", "transcribe", "Whisper large-v3",
        good_for="Japanese (until compared with Qwen3-ASR), 99 languages, vocabulary prompt",
        weak="slower than turbo; poor on Cantonese",
        runs_on="Windows/Linux (faster-whisper, M3); the Spark if local-ai adds it",
        install="a whisper.cpp server, or vid2sub[whisper] from M3",
        languages=("ja", "en", "zh", "ko", "yue")),
    CatalogEntry(
        "whisper-large-v3-turbo", "transcribe", "Whisper large-v3-turbo",
        good_for="English → English captions, quick drafts",
        weak="weaker on Cantonese and tonal languages; no translate task",
        runs_on="the Spark (always loaded); Windows/Linux from M3",
        install="the Spark's endpoint, or vid2sub[whisper] from M3",
        languages=("en", "ja", "zh", "ko")),
    CatalogEntry(
        "openai-whisper-1", "transcribe", "OpenAI whisper-1",
        good_for="a cloud fallback in any language",
        weak="25 MB per request (vid2sub's chunks fit); the audio leaves your machine",
        runs_on="OpenAI", install="an OpenAI key", languages=("en", "ja", "zh", "ko"),
        cloud=True),
    CatalogEntry(
        "gemma-4-26b-a4b", "translate", "Gemma 4 26B-A4B",
        good_for="Japanese → English, general translation; always loaded on the Spark, two slots",
        weak="", runs_on="the Spark", install="the Spark's endpoint", family="llm"),
    CatalogEntry(
        "qwen3.8-27b", "translate", "Qwen3.8 27B",
        good_for="Mandarin and Cantonese → English",
        weak="on the Spark it's Dan's coder: loads on demand, one slot, 41 GiB",
        runs_on="the Spark", install="the Spark's endpoint", family="llm"),
    CatalogEntry(
        "local-llm", "translate", "A local chat model",
        good_for="translation on your own machine (LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM)",
        weak="quality depends on the model you load", runs_on="your machine",
        install="any OpenAI-compatible server", family="llm"),
    CatalogEntry(
        "openai-gpt", "translate", "OpenAI GPT",
        good_for="a cloud fallback for translation", weak="the text leaves your machine",
        runs_on="OpenAI", install="an OpenAI key", family="llm", cloud=True),
)}

TRANSCRIBE_ROUTES: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    "yue": ("qwen3-asr-1.7b", "qwen3-asr-0.6b", "whisper-large-v3", "whisper-large-v3-turbo",
            "openai-whisper-1"),
    "zh": ("qwen3-asr-1.7b", "qwen3-asr-0.6b", "whisper-large-v3", "whisper-large-v3-turbo",
           "openai-whisper-1"),
    "ja": ("whisper-large-v3", "qwen3-asr-1.7b", "whisper-large-v3-turbo", "qwen3-asr-0.6b",
           "openai-whisper-1"),
    "en": ("whisper-large-v3-turbo", "qwen3-asr-1.7b", "whisper-large-v3", "qwen3-asr-0.6b",
           "openai-whisper-1"),
    "ko": ("whisper-large-v3", "qwen3-asr-1.7b", "whisper-large-v3-turbo", "openai-whisper-1"),
}
TRANSLATE_ROUTE: tuple[str, ...] = ("gemma-4-26b-a4b", "local-llm", "qwen3.8-27b", "openai-gpt")
WEAK: frozenset[tuple[str, str]] = frozenset({
    ("yue", "whisper-large-v3"), ("yue", "whisper-large-v3-turbo"),
    ("zh", "whisper-large-v3-turbo"),
})


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Candidate:
    name: str
    catalog: str | None
    kind: Literal["endpoint", "in-process"]
    available: bool
    reason: str = ""
    cloud: bool = False


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Suggestion:
    model: str | None
    catalog: str | None
    warning: str = ""
    considered: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = ()


def suggest(lang: str, candidates: Sequence[Candidate], task: str, *,
            allow_cloud: bool) -> Suggestion:
    route = (TRANSCRIBE_ROUTES.get(lang, TRANSCRIBE_ROUTES["en"]) if task == "transcribe"
             else TRANSLATE_ROUTE)
    considered: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    for cid in route:
        matches = sorted((c for c in candidates if c.catalog == cid),
                         key=lambda c: 0 if c.kind == "endpoint" else 1)
        for c in matches:
            if not c.available:
                considered.append((c.name, c.reason))
                continue
            if c.cloud and not allow_cloud:
                considered.append((c.name, "a cloud model: needs --allow-cloud"))
                continue
            warning = ""
            if (lang, cid) in WEAK and lang in langs.LANGS:
                warning = f"{CATALOG[cid].title} is weak on {langs.get(lang).name}"
            return Suggestion(c.name, cid, warning, tuple(considered))
    return Suggestion(None, None, "no usable model", tuple(considered))


def readme_table() -> str:
    rows = ["| Model | Task | Good for | Weak spots | Runs on |", "|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    for e in CATALOG.values():
        rows.append(f"| {e.title} | {e.task} | {e.good_for} | {e.weak or '—'} | {e.runs_on} |")
    return "\n".join(rows) + "\n"
```

```python
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from vid2sub import security as s


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name, expected", [
    ("a/b: c?", "a_b_ c_"),
    ("CON", "_CON"),
    ("nul.txt", "_nul.txt"),
    ("ends with dots...", "ends with dots"),
    ("tab\there‮evil", "tabhereevil"),
    ("", "untitled"),
])
def test_safe_component_makes_names_every_os_accepts(name, expected):
    assert s.safe_component(name) == expected


def test_safe_component_caps_the_length_in_bytes():
    name = s.safe_component("字" * 300)
    assert len(name.encode()) <= 200 and name.startswith("字")


def test_safe_write_creates_a_file_and_leaves_no_temporary_files(tmp_path):
    target = tmp_path / "a.srt"
    s.safe_write(target, b"hello", overwrite=False)
    assert target.read_bytes() == b"hello"
    assert [p.name for p in tmp_path.iterdir()] == ["a.srt"]


def test_safe_write_refuses_to_overwrite_unless_told(tmp_path):
    target = tmp_path / "a.srt"
    target.write_bytes(b"old")
    with pytest.raises(s.WriteRefused, match="already exists"):
        s.safe_write(target, b"new", overwrite=False)
    s.safe_write(target, b"new", overwrite=True)
    assert target.read_bytes() == b"new"


@pytest.mark.skipif(sys.platform == "win32", reason="symlinks need privileges on Windows")
def test_safe_write_never_follows_a_planted_symlink(tmp_path):
    secret = tmp_path / "zshrc"
    secret.write_bytes(b"precious")
    link = tmp_path / "Show.en.srt"
    link.symlink_to(secret)
    with pytest.raises(s.WriteRefused, match="isn't a regular file"):
        s.safe_write(link, b"evil", overwrite=True)
    assert secret.read_bytes() == b"precious"


def test_safe_write_cleans_up_when_the_replace_fails(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    def boom(src, dst):
        raise OSError("disk on fire")
    monkeypatch.setattr(s.os, "replace", boom)
    with pytest.raises(OSError):
        s.safe_write(tmp_path / "a.srt", b"x", overwrite=False)
    assert list(tmp_path.iterdir()) == []


def test_redact_removes_every_secret_value():
    assert s.redact("auth sk-abcdef123 failed for sk-abcdef123", ["sk-abcdef123", ""]) == \
        "auth [redacted] failed for [redacted]"


def test_child_processes_never_inherit_keys():
    env = s.child_env({"PATH": "/bin", "HOME": "/h", "OPENAI_API_KEY": "x", "SPARK_API_KEY": "y"})
    assert env == {"PATH": "/bin", "HOME": "/h"}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("url, ok", [
    ("https://api.openai.com/v1", True),
    ("http://127.0.0.1:9100/v1", True),
    ("http://localhost:9100/v1", True),

    ("http://100.101.102.103:9100/v1", True),
    ("http://192.168.1.50:9100/v1", False),
    ("http://evil.example/v1", False),
])
def test_keys_travel_over_plain_http_only_to_this_machine_or_the_tailnet(url, ok):
    assert s.key_transport_ok(url) is ok


def test_relative_inputs_resolve_against_the_working_directory(tmp_path):
    assert s.resolve_input("ep1.mkv", cwd=tmp_path) == tmp_path / "ep1.mkv"
    assert s.resolve_input(str(tmp_path / "x.mkv")) == tmp_path / "x.mkv"


@pytest.mark.skipif(os.name != "nt", reason="drive-relative paths exist only on Windows")
def test_a_drive_relative_windows_path_is_refused():
    with pytest.raises(s.InputRefused):
        s.resolve_input("C:episode.mkv")
```

Expected: FAIL (`ImportError`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/security.py`:
```python
"""Security helpers every other module uses (spec §6.5–§6.8).

`safe_component` builds every name vid2sub writes, so a YouTube title or a hostile file name can't
smuggle in a path separator, a Windows reserved name or a bidi trick. `safe_write` is the only way
vid2sub writes a file: a temporary file created exclusively without following links, fsynced,
then atomically replaced; a target that isn't a regular file (a planted symlink) is refused, and
Windows' sharing violations (Plex or antivirus holding the file) are retried. Child processes get
an allowlisted environment, so they never inherit keys. Keys travel over plain http only to this
machine or a Tailscale address (spec §5.2).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ipaddress
import os
import re
import secrets
import stat
import time
import unicodedata
from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

_RESERVED = {"CON", "PRN", "AUX", "NUL", *(f"COM{i}" for i in range(1, 10)),
             *(f"LPT{i}" for i in range(1, 10))}
_BAD = set('<>:"/\\|?*')
_ENV_KEEP = ("PATH", "HOME", "USERPROFILE", "TMP", "TEMP", "TMPDIR", "LANG", "LC_ALL",
             "LC_CTYPE", "SYSTEMROOT", "WINDIR", "COMSPEC", "PATHEXT", "XDG_CACHE_HOME",
             "DENO_DIR", "SSL_CERT_FILE", "HTTP_PROXY", "HTTPS_PROXY", "NO_PROXY",
             "http_proxy", "https_proxy", "no_proxy")
_TAILNET = ipaddress.ip_network("100.64.0.0/10")


class WriteRefused(Exception):
    pass


class InputRefused(ValueError):
    pass


def safe_component(name: str, *, max_bytes: int = 200) -> str:
    name = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", name)
    kept = []
    for ch in name:
        if unicodedata.category(ch) in ("Cc", "Cf", "Cs", "Co", "Cn"):
            continue  # control characters, bidi and other format marks, surrogates
        kept.append("_" if ch in _BAD else ch)
    out = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", "".join(kept)).strip().rstrip(". ")
    if not out:
        return "untitled"
    if out.split(".")[0].upper() in _RESERVED:
        out = "_" + out
    while len(out.encode("utf-8")) > max_bytes:
        out = out[:-1]
    return out.rstrip(". ") or "untitled"


def _replace(src: Path, dst: Path, attempts: int = 6) -> None:
    for i in range(attempts):
        try:
            os.replace(src, dst)
            return
        except PermissionError:
            if os.name != "nt" or i == attempts - 1:
                raise WriteRefused(f"{dst.name} is open in another program (Plex or antivirus?);"
                                   " try again") from None
            time.sleep(0.2 * 2 ** i)


def safe_write(path: Path, data: bytes, *, overwrite: bool) -> None:
    path = Path(path)
    try:
        st = os.lstat(path)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        st = None
    if st is not None:
        if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode):
            raise WriteRefused(f"{path.name} exists and isn't a regular file")
        if not overwrite:
            raise WriteRefused(f"{path.name} already exists")
    tmp = path.parent / f".{path.name}.{secrets.token_hex(6)}.tmp"
    flags = (os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
             | getattr(os, "O_BINARY", 0))
    fd = os.open(tmp, flags, 0o644)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as f:
            f.write(data)
            f.flush()
            os.fsync(f.fileno())
        _replace(tmp, path)
    except BaseException:
        tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise


def redact(text: str, secret_values: Iterable[str]) -> str:
    for value in sorted({v for v in secret_values if v and len(v) >= 6}, key=len, reverse=True):
        text = text.replace(value, "[redacted]")
    return text


def child_env(environ: Mapping[str, str] = os.environ) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {k: environ[k] for k in _ENV_KEEP if k in environ}


def host_of(url: str) -> str:
    return (urlsplit(url).hostname or "").lower()


def is_loopback(host: str) -> bool:
    if host == "localhost":
        return True
    try:
        return ipaddress.ip_address(host).is_loopback
    except ValueError:
        return False


def is_tailnet(host: str) -> bool:
    if host.endswith(".ts.net"):
        return True
    try:
        return ipaddress.ip_address(host) in _TAILNET
    except ValueError:
        return False


def key_transport_ok(url: str) -> bool:
    scheme, host = urlsplit(url).scheme, host_of(url)
    return scheme == "https" or (scheme == "http" and (is_loopback(host) or is_tailnet(host)))


def resolve_input(value: str, *, cwd: Path | None = None) -> Path:
    """CLI inputs may be relative (plan decision 9); drive-relative Windows paths are refused."""
    p = Path(value).expanduser()
    if os.name == "nt" and p.drive and not p.root:
        raise InputRefused("a drive-relative path like C:file is ambiguous; give the full path")
    return p if p.is_absolute() else (cwd or Path.cwd()) / p
```

`src/vid2sub/example.toml` is the template, packaged so `vid2sub config init` works from an

```toml
# vid2sub settings (spec §5). vid2sub never writes this file, and it never holds a key: only
# the names of environment variables that hold them (key_env). Copy it to
# ~/.config/vid2sub/vid2sub.toml (macOS, Linux) or %APPDATA%\vid2sub\vid2sub.toml (Windows).

[server]
port = 8765
open_browser = true

[privacy]
# A model marked `local = true` must live on one of these hosts.
local_hosts = ["127.0.0.1", "localhost", "::1"]

# The DGX Spark through local-ai's SSH tunnel (`make tunnel`).
[endpoints.spark]
base_url = "http://127.0.0.1:9100/v1"
key_env = "SPARK_API_KEY"

[endpoints.openai]
base_url = "https://api.openai.com/v1"
key_env = "OPENAI_API_KEY"
allow_typed_key = true

[models.spark-turbo]
catalog = "whisper-large-v3-turbo"
endpoint = "spark"
model = "whisper-large-v3-turbo"
parser = "whisper.cpp"
local = true

[models.spark-gemma]
catalog = "gemma-4-26b-a4b"
endpoint = "spark"
model = "gemma-4-26b-a4b"
task = "translate"
local = true
request_extra = { chat_template_kwargs = { enable_thinking = false } }

# Qwen3-ASR on an Apple Silicon Mac: install vid2sub[mac].
[models.mac-qwen]
catalog = "qwen3-asr-1.7b"
engine = "mlx-qwen3-asr"

# Cloud models are used only when a run passes --allow-cloud.
[models.openai-whisper]
catalog = "openai-whisper-1"
endpoint = "openai"
model = "whisper-1"
parser = "openai"

[models.openai-gpt]
catalog = "openai-gpt"
endpoint = "openai"
model = "gpt-5-mini"
task = "translate"

[defaults]
targets = ["en"]
formats = ["srt"]
# youtube_dir = "~/Movies/vid2sub"
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/config.py`:
```python
"""`vid2sub.toml`: endpoints, models, privacy and defaults (spec §5).

The user writes it; vid2sub never does, and it refuses key values: only the names of environment
variables that hold keys (council, 2026-10-14: a file that might hold a key must not be named to
get past Dan's `.env` hooks). Unknown keys are refused at load, naming the key (an audio-router
lesson). A model is local only if it runs in-process, or says `local = true` and its endpoint's
host is in `privacy.local_hosts` (spec §7.2: llama-swap or Ollama can pass a request to a hosted
model on localhost, so the flag is per model). Keys travel over plain http only to this machine
or the tailnet.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import tomllib
from collections.abc import Iterator, Mapping
from importlib.resources import files
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, ValidationError

from vid2sub.catalog import CATALOG
from vid2sub.security import host_of, key_transport_ok
from vid2sub.subs import naming

ENV_NAME = re.compile(r"^[A-Z_][A-Z0-9_]*$")
SECRET_PREFIXES = ("sk-", "sk_", "hf_", "ghp_", "gho_", "github_pat_", "xoxb-", "xoxp-",
                   "AKIA", "-----BEGIN")


class ConfigError(ValueError):
    pass


class _M(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")


class Server(_M):
    port: int = Field(8765, ge=1, le=65535)
    open_browser: bool = True


class Privacy(_M):
    local_hosts: list[str] = Field(default_factory=lambda: ["127.0.0.1", "localhost", "::1"])


class Endpoint(_M):
    base_url: str
    key_env: str | None = None
    allow_typed_key: bool = False


class ModelEntry(_M):
    catalog: str | None = None
    endpoint: str | None = None
    engine: Literal["mlx-qwen3-asr"] | None = None
    model: str | None = None
    parser: Literal["whisper.cpp", "openai"] | None = None
    task: Literal["transcribe", "translate"] = "transcribe"
    local: bool = False
    concurrency: int = Field(1, ge=1, le=8)
    request_extra: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    api: Literal["completions", "responses"] | None = None


class Defaults(_M):
    targets: list[str] = Field(default_factory=lambda: ["en"])
    formats: list[Literal["srt", "ass"]] = Field(default_factory=lambda: ["srt"])
    youtube_dir: str | None = None
    cache_max_gb: float = Field(20, gt=0)


class Config(_M):
    server: Server = Field(default_factory=Server)
    privacy: Privacy = Field(default_factory=Privacy)
    endpoints: dict[str, Endpoint] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    models: dict[str, ModelEntry] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    defaults: Defaults = Field(default_factory=Defaults)


def _strings(obj: Any, where: str = "") -> Iterator[tuple[str, str]]:
    if isinstance(obj, str):
        yield where, obj
    elif isinstance(obj, dict):
        for k, v in obj.items():
            yield from _strings(v, f"{where}.{k}" if where else str(k))
    elif isinstance(obj, list):
        for i, v in enumerate(obj):
            yield from _strings(v, f"{where}[{i}]")


def _local_hosts(config: Config) -> set[str]:
    return {h.lower() for h in config.privacy.local_hosts}


def _validate(config: Config) -> Config:
    for name, ep in config.endpoints.items():
        if urlsplit(ep.base_url).scheme not in ("http", "https"):
            raise ConfigError(f"endpoints.{name}.base_url must start with http:// or https://")
        if ep.key_env is not None and not ENV_NAME.fullmatch(ep.key_env):
            raise ConfigError(f"endpoints.{name}.key_env must be an environment variable name "
                              "like SPARK_API_KEY, never a key")
        if ep.key_env and not key_transport_ok(ep.base_url):
            raise ConfigError(f"endpoints.{name}: a key goes over plain http only to this "
                              "machine or a Tailscale address; use https")
    for name, m in config.models.items():
        where = f"models.{name}"
        if (m.endpoint is None) == (m.engine is None):
            raise ConfigError(f"{where} needs exactly one of `endpoint` or `engine`")
        if m.catalog is not None:
            if m.catalog not in CATALOG:
                raise ConfigError(f"{where}.catalog: unknown catalog id {m.catalog!r}")
            if CATALOG[m.catalog].task != m.task:
                raise ConfigError(f"{where}: {m.catalog} is a {CATALOG[m.catalog].task} model, "
                                  f"but task is {m.task}")
        if m.engine is not None:
            if m.task != "transcribe":
                raise ConfigError(f"{where}: in-process engines only transcribe")
            continue
        if m.endpoint not in config.endpoints:
            raise ConfigError(f"{where}.endpoint: no endpoint named {m.endpoint!r}")
        if not m.model:
            raise ConfigError(f"{where}.model: the name the server knows the model by")
        if m.task == "transcribe" and m.parser is None:
            raise ConfigError(f"{where}.parser: whisper.cpp or openai")
        if m.local and host_of(config.endpoints[m.endpoint].base_url) not in _local_hosts(config):
            raise ConfigError(f"{where}.local: its endpoint's host isn't in privacy.local_hosts")
    for target in config.defaults.targets:
        try:
            naming.target_track(target)
        except naming.UnknownTrack as err:
            raise ConfigError(f"defaults.targets: {err}") from None
    return config


def is_local(entry: ModelEntry, config: Config) -> bool:
    if entry.engine is not None:
        return True
    ep = config.endpoints.get(entry.endpoint or "")
    return bool(entry.local and ep and host_of(ep.base_url) in _local_hosts(config))


def config_path(explicit: str | None = None,
                environ: Mapping[str, str] = os.environ) -> Path:
    if explicit:
        return Path(explicit).expanduser()
    if environ.get("VID2SUB_CONFIG"):
        return Path(environ["VID2SUB_CONFIG"]).expanduser()
    if os.name == "nt":
        return Path(environ.get("APPDATA", Path.home())) / "vid2sub" / "vid2sub.toml"
    base = environ.get("XDG_CONFIG_HOME") or str(Path.home() / ".config")
    return Path(base) / "vid2sub" / "vid2sub.toml"


def load_config(path: Path) -> Config:
    if not path.exists():
        return Config()
    try:
        data = tomllib.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except tomllib.TOMLDecodeError as err:
        raise ConfigError(f"{path.name}: {err}") from None
    for where, value in _strings(data):
        if value.startswith(SECRET_PREFIXES):
            raise ConfigError(f"{where} looks like a key; vid2sub.toml holds only the names of "
                              "environment variables (key_env)")
    try:
        config = Config.model_validate(data)
    except ValidationError as err:
        first = err.errors()[0]
        loc = ".".join(str(p) for p in first["loc"])
        raise ConfigError(f"{loc}: {first['msg']}") from None
    return _validate(config)


STARTER = files("vid2sub").joinpath("example.toml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
```

```python
    assert (ROOT / "src" / "vid2sub" / "example.toml").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == c.STARTER
```

```python
import os
import stat
import sys

import pytest

from vid2sub.media import tools as t


def make_exe(directory, name):
    path = directory / (name + (".exe" if os.name == "nt" else ""))
    path.write_bytes(b"#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n")
    path.chmod(path.stat().st_mode | stat.S_IEXEC)
    return path


def test_which_finds_absolute_path_entries_and_ignores_relative_ones(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    bin_dir = tmp_path / "bin"
    bin_dir.mkdir()
    exe = make_exe(bin_dir, "faketool")
    monkeypatch.chdir(bin_dir)
    assert t.which("faketool", path=os.pathsep.join([".", "bin"])) is None
    assert t.which("faketool", path=str(bin_dir)) == str(exe)


def test_run_tool_refuses_a_relative_executable(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="absolute"):
        t.run_tool(["ffmpeg", "-version"], cwd=tmp_path, timeout=5)


def test_child_processes_see_no_keys(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("OPENAI_API_KEY", "should-not-leak")
    proc = t.run_tool([sys.executable, "-c",
                       "import os; print('OPENAI_API_KEY' in os.environ)"], cwd=tmp_path,
                      timeout=30)
    assert proc.stdout.strip() == b"False"


def test_a_failing_tool_raises_with_its_stderr(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(t.ToolFailed) as err:
        t.run_tool([sys.executable, "-c", "import sys; sys.stderr.write('bad'); sys.exit(3)"],
                   cwd=tmp_path, timeout=30)
    assert err.value.returncode == 3 and "bad" in err.value.stderr


def test_a_missing_tool_says_how_to_install_it():
    tools = t.Tools(ffmpeg=None, ffprobe=None, deno=None)
    with pytest.raises(t.ToolMissing) as err:
        tools.need("deno")
    assert "deno" in err.value.hint


@pytest.mark.media
def test_a_playlist_disguised_as_mp4_is_refused_before_it_can_fetch_anything(tmp_path):
    evil = tmp_path / "evil.mp4"
    evil.write_text("#EXTM3U\n#EXT-X-VERSION:3\n#EXTINF:1.0,\nhttp://127.0.0.1:9/seg.ts\n"
                    "#EXT-X-ENDLIST\n")
    tools = t.Tools.find()
    with pytest.raises(t.ToolFailed):
        t.run_tool([tools.need("ffprobe"), "-v", "error", *t.input_args(evil), "-show_format"],
                   cwd=tmp_path, timeout=30)


@pytest.mark.media
def test_ffmpeg_reports_a_version():
    assert t.version(t.Tools.find().need("ffmpeg")) >= (4,)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/media/__init__.py`:
```python
"""Media: probing, identity, decoding, voice detection, chunking and YouTube (spec §8)."""
```

`src/vid2sub/media/tools.py`:
```python
"""Finding and running ffmpeg, ffprobe and deno (spec §6.7, §4.2).

Every child process runs through `run_tool`: an absolute executable (never found in the current
directory, which Windows would otherwise search first), an argument list with no shell, `cwd` set
to the job's scratch folder, an allowlisted environment so it never inherits a key, and a
timeout. ffmpeg and ffprobe read input only through the `file` protocol and a demuxer allowlist,
so a playlist disguised as `.mp4` can neither fetch URLs nor read other files. Homebrew's folders
are a fallback because launchd's PATH doesn't include them (an audio-router lesson).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import subprocess
from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from vid2sub.security import child_env

FALLBACK_DIRS = ("/opt/homebrew/bin", "/usr/local/bin", "/usr/bin")
FORMAT_WHITELIST = ("matroska,webm,mov,mp4,m4a,3gp,3g2,mj2,avi,mpegts,flv,ogg,wav,flac,mp3,aac,"
                    "asf,aiff,w64,caf")
_HINTS = {
    "ffmpeg": "install ffmpeg 6 or later (macOS: brew install ffmpeg; Windows: winget install "
              "Gyan.FFmpeg; Linux: your package manager)",
    "ffprobe": "ffprobe comes with ffmpeg: install ffmpeg 6 or later",
    "deno": "install deno 2.3 or later (https://deno.com): yt-dlp needs it for YouTube",
}


class ToolMissing(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, name: str):
        self.name = name
        self.hint = _HINTS.get(name, f"install {name}")
        super().__init__(f"{name} isn't installed: {self.hint}")


class ToolFailed(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, tool: str, returncode: int, stderr: str):
        self.tool, self.returncode, self.stderr = tool, returncode, stderr
        super().__init__(f"{tool} failed (exit {returncode})")


def which(name: str, path: str | None = None) -> str | None:
    search = os.environ.get("PATH", "") if path is None else path
    dirs = [d for d in search.split(os.pathsep) if d and os.path.isabs(d)]
    if os.name != "nt":
        dirs += [d for d in FALLBACK_DIRS if d not in dirs]
    exts = os.environ.get("PATHEXT", ".EXE;.BAT;.CMD").split(";") if os.name == "nt" else [""]
    for d in dirs:
        for ext in exts:
            candidate = os.path.join(d, name + ext)
            if os.path.isfile(candidate) and os.access(candidate, os.X_OK):
                return candidate
    return None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Tools:
    ffmpeg: str | None
    ffprobe: str | None
    deno: str | None

    @classmethod
    def find(cls) -> Tools:
        return cls(which("ffmpeg"), which("ffprobe"), which("deno"))

    def need(self, name: str) -> str:
        exe = getattr(self, name)
        if not exe:
            raise ToolMissing(name)
        return exe


def run_tool(args: Sequence[str], *, cwd: Path, timeout: float, stdin: bytes | None = None,
             check: bool = True) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[bytes]:
    if not os.path.isabs(args[0]):
        raise ValueError("run_tool needs an absolute executable path")
    name = Path(args[0]).name
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(list(args), cwd=cwd, env=child_env(), input=stdin,
                              capture_output=True, timeout=timeout, shell=False)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        raise ToolFailed(name, -1, f"timed out after {timeout:.0f} s") from None
    if check and proc.returncode != 0:
        raise ToolFailed(name, proc.returncode,
                         proc.stderr[-4000:].decode("utf-8", errors="replace"))
    return proc


def input_args(path: Path) -> list[str]:
    return ["-protocol_whitelist", "file", "-format_whitelist", FORMAT_WHITELIST,
            "-i", f"file:{path}"]


def version(exe: str) -> tuple[int, ...] | None:
    """ffmpeg answers `-version`; deno answers `--version`."""
    for flag in ("-version", "--version"):
        try:
            proc = subprocess.run([exe, flag], capture_output=True, timeout=30, env=child_env())
        except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
            return None
        if proc.returncode == 0:
            first = proc.stdout.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines()[:1]
            match = re.search(r"(\d+)\.(\d+)(?:\.(\d+))?", first[0]) if first else None
            return tuple(int(g) for g in match.groups() if g is not None) if match else None
    return None
```

- Produces (tests): `helpers.make_clip(directory, name="clip.mkv", *, seconds=4.0,

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the clip helper**

Add to the imports at the top of `tests/helpers.py` (keep every import at the top; ruff's
E402 rejects imports further down):
```python
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
```

and append:
```python
def make_clip(directory: Path, name: str = "clip.mkv", *, seconds: float = 4.0,
              audio_delay: float = 0.0, audio_langs=("jpn",), default_audio: int = 0,
              title: str | None = None, speech: Path | None = None) -> Path:
    """A small video made with ffmpeg's lavfi sources: a test pattern, one sine-wave audio track
    per language (or `speech` for the first), an optional audio delay and title."""
    ffmpeg = shutil.which("ffmpeg")
    assert ffmpeg, "ffmpeg is needed for media tests"
    args = [ffmpeg, "-nostdin", "-v", "error", "-y",
            "-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"testsrc=size=160x120:rate=10:duration={seconds}"]
    for k, _ in enumerate(audio_langs):
        if audio_delay:
            args += ["-itsoffset", str(audio_delay)]
        if speech is not None and k == 0:
            args += ["-i", str(speech)]
        else:
            args += ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"sine=frequency={440 + 110 * k}:duration={seconds}"]
    args += ["-map", "0:v"]
    for k in range(len(audio_langs)):
        args += ["-map", f"{k + 1}:a"]
    args += ["-c:v", "mpeg4", "-c:a", "aac"]
    for k, lang in enumerate(audio_langs):
        args += [f"-metadata:s:a:{k}", f"language={lang}",
                 f"-disposition:a:{k}", "default" if k == default_audio else "0"]
    if title:
        args += ["-metadata", f"title={title}"]
    out = directory / name
    subprocess.run(args + [str(out)], check=True)
    return out
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/media/probe.py`:
```python
"""Probing a video and picking its audio track (spec §8.2, §8.4, §8.5).

ffprobe gives the duration, the video codec and every audio stream with its language tag,
default flag and start time. Cover art (an attached picture) isn't the video. If the chosen audio
starts later than the earliest stream (an MKV audio delay, an MPEG-TS start time), the difference
is the timeline offset added to every word, so cues match what the player shows.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from vid2sub.media.tools import Tools, input_args, run_tool
from vid2sub.subs import langs


class ProbeError(RuntimeError):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AudioTrack:
    index: int  # among audio streams: ffmpeg's a:N
    stream_index: int
    codec: str
    channels: int | None
    language: str | None
    tag: str | None
    default: bool
    start_ms: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Probe:
    duration_ms: int
    container: str
    video_codec: str | None
    audio: tuple[AudioTrack, ...]
    min_start_ms: int


def _ms(value: Any) -> int | None:
    try:
        return round(float(value) * 1000)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None


def parse_probe(data: dict) -> Probe:
    fmt = data.get("format") or {}
    duration = _ms(fmt.get("duration"))
    starts: list[int] = []
    video: str | None = None
    audio: list[AudioTrack] = []
    for s in data.get("streams") or []:
        kind = s.get("codec_type")
        disposition = s.get("disposition") or {}
        start = _ms(s.get("start_time"))
        if kind == "video" and not disposition.get("attached_pic"):
            video = video or s.get("codec_name")
            if start is not None:
                starts.append(start)
        elif kind == "audio":
            tag = (s.get("tags") or {}).get("language")
            audio.append(AudioTrack(
                index=len(audio), stream_index=int(s["index"]),
                codec=str(s.get("codec_name", "")), channels=s.get("channels"),
                language=langs.from_media_tag(tag), tag=tag,
                default=bool(disposition.get("default")), start_ms=start or 0))
            if start is not None:
                starts.append(start)
    if duration is None:
        raise ProbeError("couldn't read this file's duration")
    if not audio:
        raise ProbeError("this file has no audio track")
    return Probe(duration, str(fmt.get("format_name", "")), video, tuple(audio),
                 min(starts) if starts else 0)


def probe(path: Path, tools: Tools, *, scratch: Path) -> Probe:
    proc = run_tool([tools.need("ffprobe"), "-v", "error", *input_args(path), "-show_format",
                     "-show_streams", "-of", "json"], cwd=scratch, timeout=120)
    try:
        data = json.loads(proc.stdout)
    except ValueError:
        raise ProbeError("ffprobe's answer wasn't JSON") from None
    return parse_probe(data)


def pick_track(p: Probe, lang: str | None, override: int | str | None = None) -> AudioTrack:
    if isinstance(override, int):
        if not 0 <= override < len(p.audio):
            raise ProbeError(f"there's no audio track {override}; this file has {len(p.audio)}")
        return p.audio[override]
    wanted = override if isinstance(override, str) else lang
    if wanted:
        for track in p.audio:
            if track.language == wanted:
                return track
        if isinstance(override, str):
            raise ProbeError(f"no audio track is tagged {override}")
    return next((t for t in p.audio if t.default), p.audio[0])


def timeline_offset_ms(p: Probe, track: AudioTrack) -> int:
    return max(0, track.start_ms - p.min_start_ms)
```

```python
from vid2sub.media.identity import MIB, full_sha256, identify, quick_sha256


def write(path, data):
    path.write_bytes(data)
    return path


def test_the_quick_hash_reads_only_the_ends_and_the_size(tmp_path):
    data = bytearray(b"a" * (5 * MIB))
    a = write(tmp_path / "a.mkv", bytes(data))
    data[2 * MIB] = ord("b")  # the middle: invisible to the quick hash
    b = write(tmp_path / "b.mkv", bytes(data))
    assert quick_sha256(a) == quick_sha256(b)
    assert full_sha256(a) != full_sha256(b)
    data[-1] = ord("c")  # the last MiB: visible
    c = write(tmp_path / "c.mkv", bytes(data))
    assert quick_sha256(c) != quick_sha256(b)


def test_a_small_file_is_hashed_whole_and_its_size_counts(tmp_path):
    x = write(tmp_path / "x", b"hello")
    y = write(tmp_path / "y", b"hello\x00")
    assert quick_sha256(x) != quick_sha256(y)
    assert identify(x).size == 5
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/media/identity.py`:
```python
"""A video's identity: size plus a quick hash of its ends (spec §8.3).

Hashing a multi-GB file over SMB on every open is impractical, so a video is known by its size
and a SHA-256 of its size, first MiB and last MiB. The full SHA-256 is computed by streaming
during a job and appended to the sidecar.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

MIB = 1 << 20


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VideoIdentity:
    size: int
    quick_sha256: str


def quick_sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    size = path.stat().st_size
    h = hashlib.sha256(size.to_bytes(8, "big"))
    with path.open("rb") as f:
        h.update(f.read(MIB))
        if size > MIB:
            f.seek(max(MIB, size - MIB))
            h.update(f.read(MIB))
    return h.hexdigest()


def full_sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as f:
        while chunk := f.read(MIB):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def identify(path: Path) -> VideoIdentity:
    return VideoIdentity(size=path.stat().st_size, quick_sha256=quick_sha256(path))
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/media/decode.py`:
```python
"""Decode once to 16 kHz mono, and encode chunks as metadata-free FLAC (spec §8.5, §7.3).

Every stage reads the same 16 kHz mono PCM WAV (an audio-router lesson: one waveform, one
timeline), written to the job's scratch folder and deleted with it. Chunks are encoded from raw
PCM with `-map_metadata -1 -fflags +bitexact`: ffmpeg otherwise copies an MKV's title into the
FLAC, and the Spark's whisper-server logs ffmpeg's report, metadata tags included (council,
2026-10-14).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import wave
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from vid2sub.media.probe import AudioTrack
from vid2sub.media.tools import Tools, input_args, run_tool

SAMPLE_RATE = 16_000
SAMPLES_PER_MS = SAMPLE_RATE // 1000


def decode_to_wav(path: Path, track: AudioTrack, out: Path, tools: Tools, *, scratch: Path,
                  duration_ms: int) -> None:
    timeout = max(120.0, duration_ms / 1000 * 0.5)
    run_tool([tools.need("ffmpeg"), "-nostdin", "-v", "error", *input_args(path),
              "-map", f"0:{track.stream_index}", "-ac", "1", "-ar", str(SAMPLE_RATE),
              "-c:a", "pcm_s16le", "-map_metadata", "-1", "-fflags", "+bitexact", "-y",
              str(out)], cwd=scratch, timeout=timeout)


def read_wav(path: Path) -> np.ndarray:
    with wave.open(str(path), "rb") as w:
        if (w.getnchannels(), w.getframerate(), w.getsampwidth()) != (1, SAMPLE_RATE, 2):
            raise ValueError("expected 16 kHz mono 16-bit PCM")
        frames = w.readframes(w.getnframes())
    return np.frombuffer(frames, dtype="<i2").copy()


def to_float(samples: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    return samples.astype(np.float32) / 32768.0


def encode_flac(samples: np.ndarray, tools: Tools, *, scratch: Path) -> bytes:
    proc = run_tool([tools.need("ffmpeg"), "-v", "error", "-f", "s16le", "-ar", str(SAMPLE_RATE),
                     "-ac", "1", "-protocol_whitelist", "pipe", "-i", "pipe:0",
                     "-map_metadata", "-1", "-fflags", "+bitexact", "-c:a", "flac", "-f", "flac",
                     "pipe:1"], cwd=scratch, timeout=120,
                    stdin=samples.astype("<i2").tobytes())
    return proc.stdout
```

```bash
  https://raw.githubusercontent.com/SYSTRAN/faster-whisper/v1.2.1/LICENSE
SCRATCH=$(mktemp -d)
curl -fsSL -o "$SCRATCH/vad.py" \
  https://raw.githubusercontent.com/SYSTRAN/faster-whisper/v1.2.1/faster_whisper/vad.py
uv run python -c "import onnxruntime as o; s=o.InferenceSession('src/vid2sub/media/_vendor/silero_vad_v6.onnx'); print([i.name for i in s.get_inputs()])"
```

The last command must print the model's input names (faster-whisper's export takes `input`, `h`,
`c`). If it fails with a protobuf error, the download is a Git LFS pointer: fetch the file from
the faster-whisper 1.2.1 wheel on PyPI instead (`faster_whisper/assets/silero_vad_v6.onnx` inside
the wheel) and check again.

Create `src/vid2sub/media/_vendor/silero.py` by copying, from `$SCRATCH/vad.py`, the `VadOptions`

and with `get_vad_model` replaced as shown (faster-whisper's version builds the path from its own

```python
SPEECH = Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures" / "speech" / "jfk-14s.wav"


def speech_with_gaps(directory: Path) -> Path:
    """3 s silence, 8 s speech, 4 s silence, 6 s speech, 2 s silence (23 s, 16 kHz mono)."""
    with wave.open(str(SPEECH), "rb") as w:
        speech = np.frombuffer(w.readframes(w.getnframes()), dtype="<i2")
    rate = 16_000
    silence = lambda s: np.zeros(int(s * rate), dtype="<i2")  # noqa: E731
    audio = np.concatenate([silence(3), speech[: 8 * rate], silence(4),
                            speech[8 * rate: 14 * rate], silence(2)])
    out = directory / "gaps.wav"
    with wave.open(str(out), "wb") as w:
        w.setnchannels(1)
        w.setsampwidth(2)
        w.setframerate(rate)
        w.writeframes(audio.tobytes())
    return out
```

```python
def overlaps(region, window):
    return region[0] < window[1] and window[0] < region[1]

                      for r in regions if overlaps(r, window))
        assert covered > 0.5 * (window[1] - window[0] - 1000)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/media/vad.py`:
```python
"""Where the speech is (spec §8.6).

Silero VAD v6 on onnxruntime, vendored from faster-whisper (torch-free). ffmpeg's silencedetect
is energy-only and fails under music; the silero-vad package needs torch (council, 2026-10-14).
Regions are in milliseconds on the decoded audio's timeline.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from vid2sub.media._vendor.silero import VadOptions, get_speech_timestamps

VAD_ID = "silero-v6@faster-whisper-1.2.1"


def speech_regions(samples: np.ndarray, *, threshold: float = 0.5, min_speech_ms: int = 250,
                   min_silence_ms: int = 500, pad_ms: int = 200) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
    if len(samples) == 0:
        return []
    options = VadOptions(threshold=threshold, min_speech_duration_ms=min_speech_ms,
                         min_silence_duration_ms=min_silence_ms, speech_pad_ms=pad_ms)
    stamps = get_speech_timestamps(samples.astype(np.float32), options, sampling_rate=16_000)
    return [(round(s["start"] / 16), round(s["end"] / 16)) for s in stamps]
```

  `plan_chunks(speech, duration_ms, max_chunk_s, *, pad_ms=500, max_gap_ms=10_000) -> list[ChunkPlan]`;
  `chunk_samples(samples, plan, speech, *, fill_gap_ms=2000, keep_ms=200) -> np.ndarray`;
  `place(plan, segments) -> list[Segment]` (chunk-relative → global, keeping what this chunk owns).

```python
def test_short_audio_is_one_chunk_that_owns_the_whole_timeline():

    assert len(plans) == 1
    p = plans[0]

    plans = plan_chunks(speech, 600_000, 180)

    assert out[16 * 6000] == 0  # 8 s of non-speech, silenced

                  words=(Word(text=" a", start_ms=0, end_ms=100),
                         Word(text=" b", start_ms=1500, end_ms=1600)))

    owned = [w for s in placed for w in s.words]
    assert all(p.owns((w.start_ms + w.end_ms) // 2) for w in owned)

@settings(max_examples=80, deadline=None)

                max_size=30),
       st.lists(st.integers(0, 999_999), min_size=1, max_size=200),
       st.sampled_from([180, 300]))
def test_every_moment_is_owned_by_exactly_one_chunk(gaps_and_lengths, mids, max_chunk_s):
    t, speech = 0, []
    for gap, length in gaps_and_lengths:
        t += gap
        speech.append((t, t + length))
        t += length
    duration = t + 1000
    plans = plan_chunks(speech, duration, max_chunk_s)
    assert all(p.end_ms - p.start_ms <= max_chunk_s * 1000 for p in plans)
    for mid in (m % duration for m in mids):
        assert sum(p.owns(mid) for p in plans) == 1
```

Expected: FAIL (`ModuleNotFoundError`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/media/chunk.py`:
```python
"""Chunking the audio for transcription, and deciding which chunk owns each word (spec §8.7).

Speech regions are grouped into chunks no longer than the engine allows (300 s for Whisper, 180
for Qwen's aligner), with 0.5 s of padding either side, and cut in the middle of a silence; a
silence over 10 s always starts a new chunk, so long music isn't sent at all. The chunks' cores
tile the whole timeline (the first starts at 0, the last runs to the end), and a word belongs to
the chunk whose core contains its midpoint, so a word on a boundary is kept exactly once. Inside
a chunk, non-speech longer than 2 s is replaced with silence, which stops Whisper inventing lines
over music.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass

import numpy as np

from vid2sub.media.decode import SAMPLES_PER_MS
from vid2sub.subs.model import Segment, shift_segment


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ChunkPlan:
    index: int
    start_ms: int  # the first sample sent, padding included
    end_ms: int
    core_start_ms: int
    core_end_ms: int
    last: bool

    def owns(self, mid_ms: int) -> bool:
        return self.core_start_ms <= mid_ms and (mid_ms < self.core_end_ms or self.last)

    def plan_hash(self) -> str:
        return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(asdict(self), sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()[:12]


def _merge(speech: Sequence[tuple[int, int]], duration_ms: int) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
    regions = sorted((max(0, s), min(duration_ms, e)) for s, e in speech
                     if min(duration_ms, e) > max(0, s))
    merged: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
    for s, e in regions:
        if merged and s <= merged[-1][1]:
            merged[-1] = (merged[-1][0], max(merged[-1][1], e))
        else:
            merged.append((s, e))
    return merged


def plan_chunks(speech: Sequence[tuple[int, int]], duration_ms: int, max_chunk_s: float, *,
                pad_ms: int = 500, max_gap_ms: int = 10_000) -> list[ChunkPlan]:
    limit = int(max_chunk_s * 1000) - 2 * pad_ms
    if limit <= 0:
        raise ValueError("max_chunk_s is too small for the padding")
    merged = _merge(speech, duration_ms)
    if not merged:
        return []
    pieces: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
    for s, e in merged:
        while e - s > limit:
            pieces.append((s, s + limit))
            s += limit
        pieces.append((s, e))
    groups: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
    g_start, g_end = pieces[0]
    for s, e in pieces[1:]:
        if e - g_start <= limit and s - g_end <= max_gap_ms:
            g_end = e
            continue
        groups.append((g_start, g_end))
        g_start, g_end = s, e
    groups.append((g_start, g_end))
    plans = []
    for i, (gs, ge) in enumerate(groups):
        core_start = 0 if i == 0 else (groups[i - 1][1] + gs) // 2
        core_end = duration_ms if i == len(groups) - 1 else (ge + groups[i + 1][0]) // 2
        plans.append(ChunkPlan(i, max(0, gs - pad_ms), min(duration_ms, ge + pad_ms),
                               core_start, core_end, last=i == len(groups) - 1))
    return plans


def chunk_samples(samples: np.ndarray, plan: ChunkPlan, speech: Sequence[tuple[int, int]], *,
                  fill_gap_ms: int = 2000, keep_ms: int = 200) -> np.ndarray:
    out = samples[plan.start_ms * SAMPLES_PER_MS: plan.end_ms * SAMPLES_PER_MS].copy()
    spans = sorted((max(plan.start_ms, s - keep_ms), min(plan.end_ms, e + keep_ms))
                   for s, e in speech if e > plan.start_ms and s < plan.end_ms)
    t = plan.start_ms
    for s, e in spans + [(plan.end_ms, plan.end_ms)]:
        if s - t > fill_gap_ms:
            out[(t - plan.start_ms) * SAMPLES_PER_MS:(s - plan.start_ms) * SAMPLES_PER_MS] = 0
        t = max(t, e)
    return out


def place(plan: ChunkPlan, segments: Sequence[Segment]) -> list[Segment]:
    out = []
    for seg in segments:
        g = shift_segment(seg, plan.start_ms)
        if g.words:
            kept = tuple(w for w in g.words if plan.owns((w.start_ms + w.end_ms) // 2))
            if kept:
                out.append(g.model_copy(update={
                    "words": kept, "text": "".join(w.text for w in kept),
                    "start_ms": kept[0].start_ms, "end_ms": max(w.end_ms for w in kept)}))
        elif plan.owns((g.start_ms + g.end_ms) // 2):
            out.append(g)
    return out
```

```python
import json

import pytest

from vid2sub.media import youtube as yt
from vid2sub.media.tools import ToolFailed, Tools

TOOLS = Tools(ffmpeg="/usr/bin/ffmpeg", ffprobe="/usr/bin/ffprobe", deno="/usr/bin/deno")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("url", [
    "https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dQw4w9WgXcQ&t=42",
    "https://youtu.be/dQw4w9WgXcQ?si=abc",
    "https://m.youtube.com/watch?v=dQw4w9WgXcQ",
    "https://music.youtube.com/watch?v=dQw4w9WgXcQ&list=RD",
    "https://www.youtube.com/shorts/dQw4w9WgXcQ",
])
def test_youtube_links_give_their_video_id(url):
    assert yt.video_id(url) == "dQw4w9WgXcQ"


def test_an_id_starting_with_a_dash_is_fine():
    assert yt.video_id("https://youtu.be/-abcdefghij") == "-abcdefghij"

    "file:///etc/passwd", "https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=short",
])
def test_anything_but_one_youtube_video_is_refused(url):
```
