```python
def fake_runner(record, title="a/b: c?", ext="m4a"):
    def run(args, *, cwd, timeout, **_):
        record["args"] = args
        out = cwd / f"dQw4w9WgXcQ.{ext}"
        out.write_bytes(b"audio")
        line = json.dumps({"id": "dQw4w9WgXcQ", "title": title, "upload_date": "20091025",
                           "filepath": str(out)})
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(args, 0, stdout=line.encode() + b"\n", stderr=b"")
    return run


def test_a_download_runs_yt_dlp_locked_down_and_names_the_file_safely(tmp_path):
    record = {}
    scratch, out_dir = tmp_path / "scratch", tmp_path / "out"
    scratch.mkdir()
    got = yt.download("https://youtu.be/dQw4w9WgXcQ", out_dir=out_dir, scratch=scratch,
                      keep_video=False, tools=TOOLS, runner=fake_runner(record))
    args = record["args"]
    for flag in ("--ignore-config", "--no-plugin-dirs", "--no-playlist"):
        assert flag in args
    assert args[args.index("--use-extractors") + 1] == "youtube"
    assert args[-2:] == ["--", "https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dQw4w9WgXcQ"]
    assert args[args.index("-f") + 1] == "bestaudio/best"
    assert got.path == out_dir / "a_b_ c_ [dQw4w9WgXcQ].m4a"
    assert got.published == "20091025" and got.path.read_bytes() == b"audio"


def test_keep_video_asks_for_video_and_audio(tmp_path):
    record = {}
    (tmp_path / "s").mkdir()
    yt.download("https://youtu.be/dQw4w9WgXcQ", out_dir=tmp_path / "o", scratch=tmp_path / "s",
                keep_video=True, tools=TOOLS, runner=fake_runner(record, ext="mkv"))
    assert record["args"][record["args"].index("-f") + 1] == "bv*+ba/b"


def test_an_existing_download_is_reused(tmp_path):
    out_dir = tmp_path / "out"
    out_dir.mkdir()
    existing = out_dir / "Old title [dQw4w9WgXcQ].m4a"
    existing.write_bytes(b"x")
    got = yt.download("https://youtu.be/dQw4w9WgXcQ", out_dir=out_dir, scratch=tmp_path,
                      keep_video=False, tools=TOOLS,
                      runner=lambda *a, **k: pytest.fail("should not download"))
    assert got.path == existing and got.title == "Old title"


def test_a_bot_check_says_to_upgrade_yt_dlp(tmp_path):
    def blocked(args, **_):
        raise ToolFailed("python", 1, "ERROR: Sign in to confirm you're not a bot")
    (tmp_path / "s").mkdir()

        yt.download("https://youtu.be/dQw4w9WgXcQ", out_dir=tmp_path / "o",
                    scratch=tmp_path / "s", keep_video=False, tools=TOOLS, runner=blocked)
    assert err.value.kind == "bot_check" and "upgrade yt-dlp" in err.value.hint


def test_deno_is_required(tmp_path):

    with pytest.raises(ToolMissing):
        yt.download("https://youtu.be/dQw4w9WgXcQ", out_dir=tmp_path, scratch=tmp_path,
                    keep_video=False, tools=Tools(ffmpeg=None, ffprobe=None, deno=None))
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/media/youtube.py`:
```python
"""YouTube videos through yt-dlp, audio only unless asked (spec §8.1, §6.7).

yt-dlp runs as `python -m yt_dlp` from vid2sub's own environment, locked down: no config files
(which can set --exec), no plugins, the YouTube extractor only (so a URL can't reach the generic
extractor and the LAN), one video, output named by id inside the job's scratch folder, and the URL
after `--` (ids can start with `-`). The file is then moved into the YouTube folder under a safe
name, `<title> [<id>].<ext>`, and an existing download of the same id is reused. YouTube needs
deno (yt-dlp's only default JavaScript runtime). This is the first real YouTube source in Dan's
projects; recordings' Stage 5 can copy it (spec §26.2).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import shutil
import sys
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlsplit

from vid2sub.media.tools import ToolFailed, Tools, run_tool
from vid2sub.security import safe_component

YOUTUBE_HOSTS = {"youtube.com", "www.youtube.com", "m.youtube.com", "music.youtube.com",
                 "youtu.be"}
_ID = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{11}$")
UPGRADE_HINT = "upgrade yt-dlp: uv tool upgrade vid2sub --upgrade-package yt-dlp"


class NotYouTube(ValueError):
    pass


class YouTubeError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, kind: str, message: str, hint: str = ""):
        self.kind, self.hint = kind, hint
        super().__init__(message)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Fetched:
    path: Path
    video_id: str
    title: str
    published: str | None


def is_url(value: str) -> bool:
    return value.startswith(("http://", "https://"))


def video_id(url: str) -> str:
    parts = urlsplit(url)
    host = (parts.hostname or "").lower()
    if parts.scheme not in ("http", "https") or host not in YOUTUBE_HOSTS:
        raise NotYouTube("only YouTube links are supported")
    segments = [s for s in parts.path.split("/") if s]
    if host == "youtu.be":
        vid = segments[0] if segments else ""
    elif parts.path == "/watch":
        vid = (parse_qs(parts.query).get("v") or [""])[0]
    elif len(segments) >= 2 and segments[0] in ("shorts", "live", "embed"):
        vid = segments[1]
    else:
        vid = ""
    if not _ID.fullmatch(vid):
        raise NotYouTube("that link doesn't name one YouTube video")
    return vid


def canonical_url(vid: str) -> str:
    return f"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={vid}"


def find_existing(out_dir: Path, vid: str) -> Path | None:
    if not out_dir.is_dir():
        return None
    suffix = f"[{vid}]"
    return next((p for p in sorted(out_dir.iterdir()) if p.is_file() and p.stem.endswith(suffix)),
                None)


def download(url: str, *, out_dir: Path, scratch: Path, keep_video: bool, tools: Tools,
             runner: Callable = run_tool, timeout: float = 3600) -> Fetched:
    vid = video_id(url)
    existing = find_existing(out_dir, vid)
    if existing is not None:
        return Fetched(existing, vid, existing.stem.rsplit(" [", 1)[0], None)
    tools.need("deno")
    args = [sys.executable, "-m", "yt_dlp", "--ignore-config", "--no-plugin-dirs",
            "--use-extractors", "youtube", "--no-playlist", "--no-progress",
            "-f", "bv*+ba/b" if keep_video else "bestaudio/best",
            "-P", str(scratch), "-o", "%(id)s.%(ext)s",
            "--print", "after_move:%(.{id,title,upload_date,filepath})j"]
    if tools.ffmpeg:
        args += ["--ffmpeg-location", tools.ffmpeg]
    args += ["--", canonical_url(vid)]
    try:
        proc = runner(args, cwd=scratch, timeout=timeout)
    except ToolFailed as err:
        text = err.stderr.lower()
        if "not a bot" in text or "sign in" in text:
            raise YouTubeError("bot_check", "YouTube asked to confirm this isn't a bot",
                               UPGRADE_HINT) from None
        raise YouTubeError("failed", "yt-dlp couldn't download that video", UPGRADE_HINT) \
            from None
    info = json.loads(proc.stdout.decode("utf-8").strip().splitlines()[-1])
    src = Path(info["filepath"])
    if src.parent.resolve() != scratch.resolve():
        raise YouTubeError("failed", "yt-dlp wrote outside the scratch folder")
    title = str(info.get("title") or vid)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    dest = out_dir / f"{safe_component(title)} [{vid}]{src.suffix}"
    if dest.exists():
        raise YouTubeError("failed", f"{dest.name} appeared while downloading; try again")
    shutil.move(src, dest)
    return Fetched(dest, vid, title, info.get("upload_date"))
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces (`base.py`): `EngineError(kind, message, *, hint="", retry_after=None)` with kinds

- Produces (`clients.py`): `quiet_libraries()`; `RunPolicy(allow_cloud=False)`;

- Produces (tests): `fakes.FakeServer(responses=None, responder=None)` (context manager) with

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the fake server**

`tests/fakes.py`:
```python
"""Test doubles on real sockets, as recordings does: the code under test makes real HTTP calls."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import socket
import threading
from dataclasses import dataclass
from email import policy
from email.parser import BytesParser
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer


@dataclass
class Request:
    method: str
    path: str
    headers: dict[str, str]
    body: bytes

    def form(self) -> dict[str, tuple[str | None, bytes]]:
        ctype = self.headers["content-type"]
        message = BytesParser(policy=policy.HTTP).parsebytes(
            b"Content-Type: " + ctype.encode() + b"\r\n\r\n" + self.body)
        out = {}
        for part in message.iter_parts():
            name = part.get_param("name", header="content-disposition")
            out[name] = (part.get_filename(), part.get_payload(decode=True))
        return out

    def json(self):
        return json.loads(self.body)


class FakeServer:
    """Scripted responses `(status, json-or-bytes, headers)`, or a `responder(request)`."""

    def __init__(self, responses=None, responder=None):
        self.requests: list[Request] = []
        self.responses = list(responses or [])
        self.responder = responder
        self.httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), self._handler())
        threading.Thread(target=self.httpd.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()

    @property
    def url(self) -> str:
        return f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.httpd.server_address[1]}/v1"

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *exc):
        self.httpd.shutdown()
        self.httpd.server_close()

    def _handler(self):
        server = self

        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass

            def _respond(self):
                length = int(self.headers.get("content-length") or 0)
                req = Request(self.command, self.path,
                              {k.lower(): v for k, v in self.headers.items()},
                              self.rfile.read(length))
                server.requests.append(req)
                if server.responder is not None:
                    status, payload, headers = server.responder(req)
                elif server.responses:
                    status, payload, headers = server.responses.pop(0)
                else:
                    status, payload, headers = 200, {}, {}
                data = payload if isinstance(payload, bytes) else json.dumps(payload).encode()
                self.send_response(status)
                self.send_header("content-type", "application/json")
                for k, v in headers.items():
                    self.send_header(k, v)
                self.send_header("content-length", str(len(data)))
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(data)

            do_GET = do_POST = _respond

        return Handler


def closed_port_url() -> str:
    with socket.socket() as s:
        s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
        port = s.getsockname()[1]
    return f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/v1"
```

```python
import pytest

from vid2sub.config import Config, Endpoint, ModelEntry
from vid2sub.engines import clients
from vid2sub.engines.base import Chunk, EngineError, ModelStamp


def config(spark_url="http://127.0.0.1:9100/v1"):
    return Config(
        endpoints={"spark": Endpoint(base_url=spark_url, key_env="SPARK_API_KEY"),
                   "openai": Endpoint(base_url="https://api.openai.com/v1",
                                      key_env="OPENAI_API_KEY", allow_typed_key=True)},
        models={"turbo": ModelEntry(endpoint="spark", model="whisper-large-v3-turbo",
                                    parser="whisper.cpp", local=True),
                "w1": ModelEntry(endpoint="openai", model="whisper-1", parser="openai")})


def test_a_cloud_model_is_refused_unless_the_run_allows_it():
    cfg = config()
    with pytest.raises(EngineError) as err:
        clients.check_allowed("w1", cfg.models["w1"], cfg, clients.RunPolicy(allow_cloud=False))
    assert err.value.kind == "cloud_not_allowed" and "--allow-cloud" in err.value.hint
    clients.check_allowed("w1", cfg.models["w1"], cfg, clients.RunPolicy(allow_cloud=True))
    clients.check_allowed("turbo", cfg.models["turbo"], cfg, clients.RunPolicy())


def test_a_missing_key_names_its_variable_and_never_a_value():
    cfg = config()
    keys = clients.KeyRing(environ={})
    with pytest.raises(EngineError) as err:
        clients.transcription_client("turbo", cfg.models["turbo"], cfg, clients.RunPolicy(), keys)
    assert err.value.kind == "key_missing" and "SPARK_API_KEY" in str(err.value)


def test_a_typed_key_is_used_only_where_the_endpoint_allows_it():
    cfg = config()
    keys = clients.KeyRing(typed={"openai": "typed-key", "spark": "typed-spark"},
                           environ={"SPARK_API_KEY": "env-spark"})
    assert keys.key_for("openai", cfg.endpoints["openai"]) == "typed-key"
    assert keys.key_for("spark", cfg.endpoints["spark"]) == "env-spark"
    assert set(keys.secrets(cfg)) >= {"typed-key", "env-spark"}


def test_clients_use_the_configured_address_and_key_never_the_sdks_environment(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("OPENAI_BASE_URL", "https://evil.example/v1")
    monkeypatch.setenv("OPENAI_API_KEY", "env-openai")
    cfg = config()
    keys = clients.KeyRing(environ={"SPARK_API_KEY": "spark-secret"})
    client = clients.transcription_client("turbo", cfg.models["turbo"], cfg, clients.RunPolicy(),
                                          keys)
    assert str(client.base_url).startswith("http://127.0.0.1:9100/v1")
    assert client.api_key == "spark-secret"


def test_a_chunk_names_its_upload_and_encodes_once():
    import numpy as np
    calls = []

    def encode(samples):
        calls.append(1)
        return b"fLaC"

    chunk = Chunk(index=3, samples=np.zeros(16_000, dtype=np.int16), encode=encode)
    assert chunk.upload_name == "chunk-0003.flac" and chunk.duration_ms == 1000
    assert chunk.flac() == chunk.flac() == b"fLaC" and len(calls) == 1


def test_a_model_stamp_labels_the_model_and_revision():
    assert ModelStamp("qwen3-asr", "Qwen/Qwen3-ASR-1.7B", "0123456789abcdef").label == \
        "Qwen3-ASR-1.7B@0123456789ab"
```

`tests/test_one_client_factory.py`:
```python
"""Spec §6.4: one client factory. Only engines/clients.py may import openai, httpx or chatlas,
so every client gets an explicit address and key, no redirects, and the cloud check."""

import ast
from pathlib import Path

SRC = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "src" / "vid2sub"
NETWORK = {"openai", "httpx", "chatlas"}


def test_only_the_client_factory_imports_network_libraries():
    offenders = []
    for path in SRC.rglob("*.py"):
        if path == SRC / "engines" / "clients.py":
            continue
        for node in ast.walk(ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))):
            names = []
            if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
                names = [a.name for a in node.names]
            elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.module:
                names = [node.module]
            for name in names:
                if name.split(".")[0] in NETWORK:
                    offenders.append(f"{path.relative_to(SRC)}: {name}")
    assert not offenders, "network libraries imported outside engines/clients.py:\n" + \
        "\n".join(offenders)
```

`src/vid2sub/engines/__init__.py`:
```python
"""Transcription engines and the one client factory (spec §9, §6.4)."""
```

`src/vid2sub/engines/base.py`:
```python
"""The transcription engine protocol (spec §9.1).

Every engine takes one chunk and returns a ParsedChunk in vid2sub's word shape. Endpoints upload
the chunk as FLAC named `chunk-NNNN.flac` (spec §7.3); in-process engines take the samples. Errors
are EngineErrors with a kind and a hint that names the fix: a connection error or a Spark refusal
pauses the run so it can resume; a server error is retried; anything else fails the input.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Literal, Protocol

import numpy as np

from vid2sub.media.decode import SAMPLES_PER_MS, to_float
from vid2sub.parse import ParsedChunk

ErrorKind = Literal["connection", "auth", "too_large", "server", "refused", "bad_response",
                    "unavailable", "cloud_not_allowed", "key_missing"]


class EngineError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, kind: ErrorKind, message: str, *, hint: str = "",
                 retry_after: float | None = None):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.kind = kind
        self.hint = hint
        self.retry_after = retry_after


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ModelStamp:
    engine: str
    model: str
    revision: str = "unknown"

    @property
    def label(self) -> str:
        return f"{self.model.split('/')[-1]}@{self.revision[:12]}"


@dataclass
class Chunk:
    index: int
    samples: np.ndarray  # int16, 16 kHz mono
    encode: Callable[[np.ndarray], bytes]
    _flac: bytes | None = field(default=None, repr=False)

    def flac(self) -> bytes:
        if self._flac is None:
            self._flac = self.encode(self.samples)
        return self._flac

    @property
    def float32(self) -> np.ndarray:
        return to_float(self.samples)

    @property
    def duration_ms(self) -> int:
        return len(self.samples) // SAMPLES_PER_MS

    @property
    def upload_name(self) -> str:
        return f"chunk-{self.index:04d}.flac"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ChunkResult:
    parsed: ParsedChunk
    answered_model: str | None = None


class Engine(Protocol):
    name: str
    max_chunk_s: float
    supports_prompt: bool

    def transcribe(self, chunk: Chunk, *, language: str | None, prompt: str) -> ChunkResult: ...

    def detect_language(self, chunk: Chunk) -> dict[str, float]: ...

    def stamp(self) -> ModelStamp: ...

    def close(self) -> None: ...
```

`src/vid2sub/engines/clients.py`:
```python
"""The one place network clients are made and called (spec §6.4, §7.2; plan decision 11).

Only this module imports openai, httpx or chatlas (a test enforces it). Every client gets its
address and key passed explicitly, so the SDK never reads OPENAI_API_KEY or OPENAI_BASE_URL;
redirects are refused, so a 307 can't re-send audio or a key elsewhere; retries are vid2sub's own
(max_retries=0). A model that isn't local is used only when the run allows cloud models. Errors
become EngineErrors whose hints name the fix, with every key value redacted. A key goes only to
the endpoint it was declared with; a typed key only to an endpoint that allows one.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import httpx
import openai
from chatlas import ChatOpenAI, ChatOpenAICompletions
from pydantic import BaseModel

from vid2sub.config import Config, Endpoint, ModelEntry, is_local
from vid2sub.engines.base import EngineError
from vid2sub.security import host_of, redact

QUIET = ("openai", "httpx", "httpcore", "chatlas")
REFUSAL_CODES = {"loading", "no_fit", "held_by_brake", "gate_down"}  # local-ai Phase 3


def quiet_libraries() -> None:
    for name in QUIET:
        logging.getLogger(name).setLevel(logging.WARNING)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunPolicy:
    allow_cloud: bool = False


@dataclass
class KeyRing:
    typed: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    environ: Mapping[str, str] = field(default_factory=lambda: os.environ)

    def key_for(self, endpoint_name: str, endpoint: Endpoint) -> str | None:
        if endpoint.allow_typed_key and self.typed.get(endpoint_name):
            return self.typed[endpoint_name]
        if endpoint.key_env:
            return self.environ.get(endpoint.key_env) or None
        return None

    def secrets(self, config: Config) -> list[str]:
        values = list(self.typed.values())
        values += [self.environ.get(ep.key_env, "") for ep in config.endpoints.values()
                   if ep.key_env]
        return [v for v in values if v]


def check_allowed(name: str, entry: ModelEntry, config: Config, policy: RunPolicy) -> None:
    if not is_local(entry, config) and not policy.allow_cloud:
        raise EngineError("cloud_not_allowed",
                          f"{name} is a cloud model, and this run doesn't allow cloud models",
                          hint="add --allow-cloud to send audio or text to it")


def _key(entry: ModelEntry, config: Config, keys: KeyRing) -> str:
    ep = config.endpoints[entry.endpoint]
    key = keys.key_for(entry.endpoint, ep)
    if ep.key_env and not key:
        raise EngineError("key_missing",
                          f"{ep.key_env} isn't set in the environment vid2sub started from",
                          hint=f"export {ep.key_env} in your shell (on Dan's Mac, ~/.secrets), "
                               "then start vid2sub again from that shell")
    return key or "unused"


def _http(timeout: float) -> httpx.Client:
    return httpx.Client(follow_redirects=False, timeout=httpx.Timeout(timeout, connect=10.0))


def transcription_client(name: str, entry: ModelEntry, config: Config, policy: RunPolicy,
                         keys: KeyRing, *, timeout: float = 900) -> openai.OpenAI:
    check_allowed(name, entry, config, policy)
    ep = config.endpoints[entry.endpoint]
    return openai.OpenAI(api_key=_key(entry, config, keys), base_url=ep.base_url,
                         http_client=_http(timeout), max_retries=0)


def _json(response: Any) -> Any:
    try:
        return response.json()
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - any unreadable body is just "no JSON"
        return None


def _retry_after(response: Any) -> float | None:
    try:
        return float(response.headers.get("retry-after"))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None


def map_error(err: Exception, *, key_env: str | None, secrets: list[str]) -> EngineError:
    message = redact(str(err), secrets)
    if isinstance(err, openai.APITimeoutError):
        return EngineError("server", "the server took too long to answer",
                           hint="it may be busy; vid2sub retries twice")
    if isinstance(err, openai.APIConnectionError):
        return EngineError("connection", "couldn't reach the server",
                           hint="is it running? For the Spark: is `make tunnel` running?")
    if isinstance(err, openai.AuthenticationError):
        hint = (f"{key_env} is wrong, or isn't exported in the shell vid2sub started from; "
                "restart vid2sub from one that exports it") if key_env else \
            "this server needs a key: set key_env for its endpoint in vid2sub.toml"
        return EngineError("auth", "the server refused the key (401)", hint=hint)
    if isinstance(err, openai.APIStatusError):
        status = err.status_code
        body = _json(err.response)
        error = body.get("error") if isinstance(body, dict) else None
        code = error.get("code") if isinstance(error, dict) else None
        if code in REFUSAL_CODES:
            retry = error.get("retry_after_s")
            return EngineError("refused", f"the Spark refused the request: {code}",
                               hint="local-ai's `make status` says why",
                               retry_after=float(retry) if retry else None)
        if 300 <= status < 400:
            return EngineError("bad_response",
                               f"the server redirected ({status}); vid2sub never follows a "
                               "redirect with a key", hint="set base_url to the final address")
        if status == 413:
            return EngineError("too_large", "the chunk was too large for this server (413)",
                               hint="this server's upload limit is below vid2sub's chunk size")
        if status == 429:
            return EngineError("refused", "the server is busy (429)",
                               retry_after=_retry_after(err.response) or 30.0)
        if status >= 500:
            return EngineError("server", f"the server failed ({status})",
                               hint="vid2sub retries twice")
        return EngineError("bad_response", f"the server answered {status}: {message[:200]}")
    return EngineError("bad_response", message[:200])


def call_transcription(client: openai.OpenAI, *, key_env: str | None, secrets: list[str],
                       file: tuple[str, bytes, str], **params: Any
                       ) -> tuple[dict, Mapping[str, str]]:
    try:
        raw = client.audio.transcriptions.with_raw_response.create(file=file, **params)
    except openai.OpenAIError as err:
        raise map_error(err, key_env=key_env, secrets=secrets) from None
    response = raw.http_response
    try:
        data = response.json()
    except ValueError:
        raise EngineError("bad_response", "the server's answer wasn't JSON") from None
    return data, response.headers


def list_models(endpoint_name: str, endpoint: Endpoint, keys: KeyRing, *,
                timeout: float = 5.0) -> list[str]:
    key = keys.key_for(endpoint_name, endpoint)
    client = openai.OpenAI(api_key=key or "unused", base_url=endpoint.base_url,
                           http_client=_http(timeout), max_retries=0)
    try:
        return [m.id for m in client.models.list()]
    except openai.OpenAIError as err:
        raise map_error(err, key_env=endpoint.key_env, secrets=[key] if key else []) from None
    finally:
        client.close()


def chat(name: str, entry: ModelEntry, config: Config, policy: RunPolicy, keys: KeyRing, *,
         system_prompt: str, timeout: float = 300):
    check_allowed(name, entry, config, policy)
    ep = config.endpoints[entry.endpoint]
    api = entry.api or ("responses" if host_of(ep.base_url) == "api.openai.com"
                        else "completions")
    factory = ChatOpenAI if api == "responses" else ChatOpenAICompletions
    return factory(model=entry.model, base_url=ep.base_url, api_key=_key(entry, config, keys),
                   system_prompt=system_prompt,
                   kwargs={"http_client": _http(timeout), "max_retries": 0})


def call_structured(chat_obj: Any, prompt: str, data_model: type[BaseModel], *,
                    extra_body: dict | None, key_env: str | None,
                    secrets: list[str]) -> BaseModel:
    kwargs = {"extra_body": extra_body} if extra_body else None
    try:
        return chat_obj.chat_structured(prompt, data_model=data_model, echo="none",
                                        kwargs=kwargs)
    except openai.OpenAIError as err:
        raise map_error(err, key_env=key_env, secrets=secrets) from None
```

```python
import pytest
from fakes import FakeServer, closed_port_url

from vid2sub.config import Config, Endpoint, ModelEntry
from vid2sub.engines.base import Chunk, EngineError
from vid2sub.engines.clients import KeyRing, RunPolicy

SECRET = "spark-secret-123"
WHISPER = {"language": "japanese", "segments": [{"text": "こんにちは", "start": 0.0, "end": 1.0,
           "words": [{"word": "こん", "start": 0.0, "end": 0.5, "t_dtw": -1, "probability": 0.9},
                     {"word": "にちは", "start": 0.5, "end": 1.0, "t_dtw": -1,
                      "probability": 0.8}]}],
           "language_probabilities": {"ja": 0.97, "zh": 0.02}}


def engine(url, model="whisper-large-v3-turbo"):
    cfg = Config(endpoints={"spark": Endpoint(base_url=url, key_env="SPARK_API_KEY")},
                 models={"turbo": ModelEntry(catalog="whisper-large-v3-turbo", endpoint="spark",
                                             model=model, parser="whisper.cpp", local=True)})
    return EndpointEngine("turbo", cfg.models["turbo"], cfg, RunPolicy(),
                          KeyRing(environ={"SPARK_API_KEY": SECRET}))

                 encode=lambda s: b"fLaC-fake")

    with FakeServer([(200, WHISPER, {})]) as srv:
        result = engine(srv.url).transcribe(chunk(), language="ja", prompt="工藤新一.")
        req = srv.requests[0]
    assert req.path == "/v1/audio/transcriptions"
    assert req.headers["authorization"] == f"Bearer {SECRET}"
    form = req.form()
    assert form["file"] == ("chunk-0003.flac", b"fLaC-fake")
    assert form["model"][1] == b"whisper-large-v3-turbo"
    assert form["language"][1] == b"ja"
    assert form["prompt"][1] == "工藤新一.".encode()
    assert form["response_format"][1] == b"verbose_json"
    assert [w.text for w in result.parsed.segments[0].words] == ["こん", "にちは"]


def test_language_detection_returns_the_servers_probabilities():

        assert engine(srv.url).detect_language(chunk())["ja"] == 0.97
        assert "language" not in srv.requests[0].form()


def test_a_401_names_the_variable_to_export_and_never_shows_the_key():
    with FakeServer([(401, {"error": {"message": f"bad key {SECRET}"}}, {})]) as srv:
        with pytest.raises(EngineError) as err:

    assert err.value.kind == "auth" and "SPARK_API_KEY" in err.value.hint

@pytest.mark.parametrize("status, payload, headers, kind, retry", [
    (413, {"error": {"message": "too big"}}, {}, "too_large", None),

])
def test_server_answers_become_error_kinds(status, payload, headers, kind, retry):
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/engines/endpoint.py`:
```python
"""The endpoint engine: any OpenAI-shaped /v1/audio/transcriptions server (spec §9.3).

The Spark's whisper.cpp behind llama-swap, OpenAI, or a local whisper.cpp server. The entry's
`parser` picks the response shape. Requests ask for `verbose_json`, send the language and prompt
when given, and the chunk as `chunk-NNNN.flac`. If the server says which model answered
(local-ai Phase 3's `x-spark-model`), a different model from the one asked for fails the chunk:
vid2sub never accepts a substitute (spec §9.6, §17.4). The Spark reports no model revision
today, so stamps say `unknown`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from vid2sub.catalog import CATALOG
from vid2sub.config import Config, ModelEntry
from vid2sub.engines import clients
from vid2sub.engines.base import Chunk, ChunkResult, EngineError, ModelStamp
from vid2sub.parse import ParseError
from vid2sub.parse.openai import parse_openai
from vid2sub.parse.whispercpp import parse_whispercpp


class EndpointEngine:
    def __init__(self, name: str, entry: ModelEntry, config: Config,
                 policy: clients.RunPolicy, keys: clients.KeyRing):
        self.model_name = name
        self.entry = entry
        self.name = entry.parser or "openai"
        catalog = CATALOG.get(entry.catalog or "")
        self.max_chunk_s = catalog.max_chunk_s if catalog else 300.0
        self.supports_prompt = catalog.supports_prompt if catalog else True
        self._key_env = config.endpoints[entry.endpoint].key_env
        self._secrets = keys.secrets(config)
        self._client = clients.transcription_client(name, entry, config, policy, keys)

    def _post(self, chunk: Chunk, *, language: str | None, prompt: str) -> ChunkResult:
        params: dict = {"model": self.entry.model, "response_format": "verbose_json"}
        if language:
            params["language"] = language
        if prompt and self.supports_prompt:
            params["prompt"] = prompt
        if self.entry.parser == "openai":
            params["timestamp_granularities"] = ["word", "segment"]
        data, headers = clients.call_transcription(
            self._client, key_env=self._key_env, secrets=self._secrets,
            file=(chunk.upload_name, chunk.flac(), "audio/flac"), **params)
        answered = headers.get("x-spark-model")
        if answered and answered != self.entry.model:
            raise EngineError("bad_response",
                              f"asked for {self.entry.model} but {answered} answered",
                              hint="vid2sub never accepts a substitute model")
        try:
            parsed = (parse_whispercpp(data) if self.entry.parser == "whisper.cpp"
                      else parse_openai(data))
        except ParseError as err:
            raise EngineError("bad_response", str(err)) from None
        return ChunkResult(parsed, answered)

    def transcribe(self, chunk: Chunk, *, language: str | None, prompt: str) -> ChunkResult:
        return self._post(chunk, language=language, prompt=prompt)

    def detect_language(self, chunk: Chunk) -> dict[str, float]:
        parsed = self._post(chunk, language=None, prompt="").parsed
        if parsed.language_probs:
            return dict(parsed.language_probs)
        return {parsed.language: 1.0} if parsed.language else {}

    def stamp(self) -> ModelStamp:
        return ModelStamp(self.name, self.entry.model or "", "unknown")

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._client.close()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/engines/qwen_mlx.py`:
```python
"""Qwen3-ASR with its forced aligner, in-process on Apple Silicon (spec §9.4).

Through mlx-qwen3-asr (the `mac` extra, macOS 14+). The best model here for Mandarin and
Cantonese. Its aligner works in 180 s pieces, so chunks are at most 180 s. Qwen3-ASR takes a
vocabulary context (council correction, 2026-10-14), so the glossary goes in as `context`. The
import is lazy: mlx imports take tens of seconds (an audio-router lesson).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

from vid2sub.catalog import CATALOG
from vid2sub.config import ModelEntry
from vid2sub.engines.base import Chunk, ChunkResult, EngineError, ModelStamp
from vid2sub.parse.qwen import parse_qwen
from vid2sub.subs import langs

SAMPLE_RATE = 16_000


def hf_revision(repo: str) -> str:
    """The cached snapshot's hash, so a model update changes the version.

    Copied from audio-router's model_revision (stages/transcribe.py at 64612df)."""
    try:
        from huggingface_hub import snapshot_download

        path = Path(snapshot_download(repo, local_files_only=True))
        return path.name[:12] if path.name else "unknown"
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - an unstamped rendition beats a failed one
        return "unknown"


class QwenMLXEngine:
    name = "qwen3-asr"
    max_chunk_s = 180.0
    supports_prompt = True

    def __init__(self, name: str, entry: ModelEntry, *,
                 transcribe_fn: Callable | None = None):
        catalog = CATALOG.get(entry.catalog or "qwen3-asr-1.7b")
        self.model_name = name
        self.model_id = (entry.model or (catalog.default_model if catalog else None)
                         or "Qwen/Qwen3-ASR-1.7B")
        self._fn = transcribe_fn

    def _transcribe(self) -> Callable:
        if self._fn is None:
            try:
                from mlx_qwen3_asr import transcribe
            except ImportError:
                raise EngineError("unavailable", "mlx-qwen3-asr isn't installed",
                                  hint="on an Apple Silicon Mac (macOS 14+), install "
                                       "vid2sub[mac]") from None
            self._fn = transcribe
        return self._fn

    def transcribe(self, chunk: Chunk, *, language: str | None, prompt: str) -> ChunkResult:
        result = self._transcribe()(
            (chunk.float32, SAMPLE_RATE), model=self.model_id,
            language=langs.get(language).qwen if language else None,
            context=prompt or "", return_timestamps=True, verbose=False)
        data = {"text": getattr(result, "text", ""), "language": getattr(result, "language", ""),
                "segments": getattr(result, "segments", None) or []}
        return ChunkResult(parse_qwen(data, duration_ms=chunk.duration_ms))

    def detect_language(self, chunk: Chunk) -> dict[str, float]:
        result = self._transcribe()((chunk.float32, SAMPLE_RATE), model=self.model_id,
                                    language=None, return_timestamps=False, verbose=False)
        code = langs.from_qwen(getattr(result, "language", "") or "")
        return {code: 1.0} if code else {}

    def stamp(self) -> ModelStamp:
        return ModelStamp(self.name, self.model_id, hf_revision(self.model_id))

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._fn = None
```

`src/vid2sub/engines/registry.py`:
```python
"""Engines built from `vid2sub.toml` model entries (spec §5.1, §9)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from vid2sub.config import Config, ConfigError
from vid2sub.engines.base import Engine
from vid2sub.engines.clients import KeyRing, RunPolicy
from vid2sub.engines.endpoint import EndpointEngine
from vid2sub.engines.qwen_mlx import QwenMLXEngine


def make_engine(name: str, config: Config, policy: RunPolicy, keys: KeyRing) -> Engine:
    entry = config.models.get(name)
    if entry is None:
        raise ConfigError(f"no model named {name!r} in vid2sub.toml")
    if entry.task != "transcribe":
        raise ConfigError(f"{name} is a translation model, not a transcription one")
    if entry.engine == "mlx-qwen3-asr":
        return QwenMLXEngine(name, entry)
    return EndpointEngine(name, entry, config, policy, keys)
```

```markdown
# Tests

- `uv run pytest` runs everything except live tests. Tests marked `media` need ffmpeg and
  ffprobe; they skip without them unless `VID2SUB_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1` (CI sets it).
- Live tests (`uv run pytest -m live`) use real models and networks, opt-in:
  - `VID2SUB_LIVE_MLX=1` on an Apple Silicon Mac with `uv sync --extra mac`: Qwen3-ASR on the
    speech fixture (downloads the model on first run).
  - `VID2SUB_LIVE_SPARK=1` with local-ai's `make tunnel` running and `SPARK_API_KEY` exported:
    the Spark's whisper.cpp and Gemma 4.
  - YouTube: downloads one short public-domain video.
- Fixtures are synthetic or public domain (`tests/fixtures/CREDITS.md`).
```

```python
import sys

import pytest

from vid2sub import store
from vid2sub.media.identity import VideoIdentity
from vid2sub.security import WriteRefused
from vid2sub.subs.formats import to_srt
from vid2sub.subs.model import Cue
from vid2sub.subs.rendition import cues_body
from vid2sub.subs.sidecar import HeadRecord, SidecarError, make_rendition, parse_sidecar

CUES = [Cue(id="c1", start_ms=0, end_ms=1500, text="Hello"),
        Cue(id="c2", start_ms=2000, end_ms=3500, text="World")]


@pytest.fixture
def video(tmp_path):
    v = tmp_path / "Show - S01E02.mkv"
    v.write_bytes(b"video")
    return v


def header(video):
    return store.header_for(video, VideoIdentity(size=5, quick_sha256="ab"), 10_000)


def with_track(video, cues=CUES, track="en", role="source", by="auto"):
    body = cues_body(cues, language="en", script=None, engine="vid2sub", model=None,
                     version="v1", created_at=store.now(), inputs={}, meta={"role": role})
    rec = make_rendition([], track, body)
    side, _ = store.load(video)
    head = store.head_record(side or store.append(video, header(video), []), track, rec.id, by)
    return store.append(video, header(video), [rec, head])


def test_append_creates_the_sidecar_and_adds_records(video):
    side = with_track(video)
    text = store.sidecar_path(video).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert parse_sidecar(text)[0] == side
    assert store.source_track(side) == "en"


def test_a_sidecar_vid2sub_cant_read_is_never_overwritten(video):
    path = store.sidecar_path(video)
    path.write_text("not a sidecar\n")
    with pytest.raises(SidecarError):
        store.append(video, header(video), [])
    assert path.read_text() == "not a sidecar\n"


def test_a_concurrent_writer_is_merged_not_lost(video, monkeypatch):
    with_track(video)
    other = make_rendition([], "ja", {"kind": "cues"})
    real = store.serialize
    calls = []

    def racing(sidecar):
        if not calls:
            calls.append(1)
            path = store.sidecar_path(video)
            path.write_text(path.read_text() + real(sidecar.__class__(sidecar.header, (other,)))
                            .splitlines()[1] + "\n")
        return real(sidecar)

    monkeypatch.setattr(store, "serialize", racing)
    mine = make_rendition([], "en", {"kind": "cues", "n": 2})
    side = store.append(video, header(video), [mine])
    assert other.id in side.renditions() and mine.id in side.renditions()


def test_an_auto_version_never_replaces_a_persons(video):
    side = with_track(video, by="editor")
    assert store.head_record(side, "en", "r_new", "auto") is None
    assert store.head_record(side, "en", "r_new", "auto", replace_person=True) is not None
    assert store.head_record(side, "en", "r_new", "editor").replaces == side.head("en").rendition


def test_projecting_writes_lf_srt_and_records_its_hash_first(video):
    side = with_track(video)
    side, path, written = store.project(video, header(video), side, "en", "srt",
                                        overwrite_foreign=False)
    assert written and path.name == "Show - S01E02.en.srt"
    assert path.read_bytes() == to_srt(CUES)
    assert side.projections()[path.name].sha256 == store.sha256_hex(path.read_bytes())
    _, _, again = store.project(video, header(video), side, "en", "srt", overwrite_foreign=False)
    assert not again


def test_someone_elses_subtitle_file_is_never_overwritten(video):
    side = with_track(video)
    theirs = video.with_name("Show - S01E02.en.srt")
    theirs.write_text("1\n00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:01,000\nBazarr\n")
    with pytest.raises(store.ForeignFile):
        store.project(video, header(video), side, "en", "srt", overwrite_foreign=False)
    assert "Bazarr" in theirs.read_text()
    store.project(video, header(video), side, "en", "srt", overwrite_foreign=True)
    assert "Hello" in theirs.read_text()


@pytest.mark.skipif(sys.platform == "win32", reason="symlinks need privileges on Windows")
def test_a_symlink_where_the_subtitle_goes_is_refused(video, tmp_path):
    side = with_track(video)
    target = tmp_path / "elsewhere.txt"
    target.write_text("precious")
    video.with_name("Show - S01E02.en.srt").symlink_to(target)
    with pytest.raises(WriteRefused):
        store.project(video, header(video), side, "en", "srt", overwrite_foreign=True)
    assert target.read_text() == "precious"


def test_an_edit_made_in_another_editor_comes_back_as_an_external_version(video):
    side = with_track(video)
    side, path, _ = store.project(video, header(video), side, "en", "srt",
                                  overwrite_foreign=False)
    path.write_bytes(path.read_bytes().replace(b"World", b"Earth"))
    side, changed = store.check_external(video, header(video), side)
    assert changed == ["en"]
    head = side.head("en")
    assert isinstance(head, HeadRecord) and head.by == "external"
    rec, cues = store.current_cues(side, "en")
    assert [c.id for c in cues] == ["c1", "c2"] and cues[1].text == "Earth"
    assert "edited" in cues[1].flags
    _, changed_again = store.check_external(video, header(video), side)
    assert changed_again == []


def test_importing_a_legacy_encoded_srt_makes_it_the_source(video, tmp_path):
    sub = tmp_path / "fansub.srt"
    lines = ["今天天气很好，我们去公园吧。", "明天也是好天气，我们再去吧。",
             "公园里有很多人在散步和跑步。", "我们一起去吃午饭，然后回家休息。"]
    sub.write_bytes("".join(f"{i}\n00:00:0{i},000 --> 00:00:0{i},900\n{line}\n\n"
                            for i, line in enumerate(lines, 1)).encode("gbk"))
    side = store.import_subtitles(video, header(video), sub, "zh-Hans", role="source")
    assert store.source_track(side) == "zh-Hans"
    head = side.head("zh-Hans")
    assert head.by == "import"
    _, cues = store.current_cues(side, "zh-Hans")
    assert cues[0].text.startswith("今天")


def test_overlapping_imported_cues_are_clamped():
    out = store.normalise_track([Cue(id="a", start_ms=0, end_ms=2000, text="x"),
                                 Cue(id="b", start_ms=1000, end_ms=3000, text="y"),
                                 Cue(id="c", start_ms=3000, end_ms=3000, text="z")])
    assert [(c.id, c.start_ms, c.end_ms) for c in out] == [("a", 0, 1000), ("b", 1000, 3000)]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/store.py`:
```python
"""The sidecar and the subtitle files next to a video (spec §14.5, §14.6, §15).

Appends use compare-and-swap: read the sidecar, add records, re-read just before replacing, and
start over if another writer got there first; records are immutable with ids, so the merge is a
union and nothing is lost. A sidecar vid2sub can't read is never overwritten. A subtitle file is
vid2sub's only if the sidecar has a projection record for it; anyone else's (Bazarr, a fansub) is
never overwritten without `overwrite_foreign`. The projection record is written before the file.
A projected file that changed on disk is parsed and compared at its format's precision: real
changes come back as an `external` version (ids recovered by time and text), and a change that
was only bytes or styling just updates the record. An automatic version never replaces a head a
person set (spec §14.4).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

from vid2sub.media.identity import VideoIdentity
from vid2sub.security import WriteRefused, safe_write
from vid2sub.subs import naming
from vid2sub.subs.formats import (
    PRECISION_MS, ParsedCue, parse_subtitles, same_cues, sha256_hex, to_ass, to_srt,
)
from vid2sub.subs.model import Cue, new_cue_id
from vid2sub.subs.rendition import body_track_language, cues_body, cues_from_body
from vid2sub.subs.sidecar import (
    Conflict, Header, HeadRecord, ProjectionRecord, RenditionRecord, Sidecar, SidecarError,
    VideoInfo, make_rendition, parse_sidecar, serialize,
)

class StoreError(RuntimeError):
    pass


class ForeignFile(StoreError):
    def __init__(self, name: str):
        self.name = name
        super().__init__(f"{name} wasn't written by vid2sub, so it isn't overwritten "
                         "(--overwrite-foreign to replace it)")


def now() -> str:
    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")


def sidecar_path(video: Path) -> Path:
    return video.with_name(naming.sidecar_name(video.name))


def subtitle_path(video: Path, track: str, fmt: str) -> Path:
    return video.with_name(naming.subtitle_name(video.name, track, fmt))


def header_for(video: Path, identity: VideoIdentity, duration_ms: int) -> Header:
    return Header(video=VideoInfo(name=video.name, size=identity.size,
                                  quick_sha256=identity.quick_sha256, duration_ms=duration_ms))


def _decode(data: bytes, name: str) -> str:
    try:
        return data.decode("utf-8")
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        raise SidecarError(f"{name} isn't UTF-8; refusing to write over it") from None


def load(video: Path) -> tuple[Sidecar | None, list[str]]:
    path = sidecar_path(video)
    if not path.exists():
        return None, []
    return parse_sidecar(_decode(path.read_bytes(), path.name))


def append(video: Path, header: Header, records: Sequence) -> Sidecar:
    path = sidecar_path(video)
    for _ in range(5):
        before = path.read_bytes() if path.exists() else None
        current = (parse_sidecar(_decode(before, path.name))[0] if before is not None
                   else Sidecar(header))
        data = serialize(current.add(*records)).encode("utf-8")
        latest = path.read_bytes() if path.exists() else None
        if latest != before:
            continue
        safe_write(path, data, overwrite=before is not None)
        return parse_sidecar(data.decode("utf-8"))[0]
    raise StoreError("the sidecar kept changing while vid2sub saved it; try again")


def head_record(sidecar: Sidecar, track: str, rendition_id: str, by: str, *,
                replace_person: bool = False) -> HeadRecord | None:
    current = sidecar.head(track)
    if isinstance(current, Conflict):
        return None
    if current is not None:
        if current.rendition == rendition_id:
            return None
        if by == "auto" and current.by != "auto" and not replace_person:
            return None
    return HeadRecord(track=track, rendition=rendition_id,
                      replaces=current.rendition if current is not None else None,
                      by=by, at=now())


def current_cues(sidecar: Sidecar, track: str) -> tuple[RenditionRecord, list[Cue]]:
    head = sidecar.head(track)
    if head is None:
        raise StoreError(f"there are no {track} subtitles yet")
    if isinstance(head, Conflict):
        raise StoreError(f"the {track} track has two diverging versions; pick one in the editor")
    rec = sidecar.renditions()[head.rendition]
    return rec, cues_from_body(rec.body)


def source_track(sidecar: Sidecar) -> str | None:
    renditions = sidecar.renditions()
    for track in sidecar.tracks():
        head = sidecar.head(track)
        if isinstance(head, HeadRecord):
            body = renditions[head.rendition].body
            if body.get("meta", {}).get("role") == "source":
                return track
    return None


def project(video: Path, header: Header, sidecar: Sidecar, track: str, fmt: str, *,
            overwrite_foreign: bool) -> tuple[Sidecar, Path, bool]:
    rec, cues = current_cues(sidecar, track)
    path = subtitle_path(video, track, fmt)
    if path.is_symlink() or (path.exists() and not path.is_file()):
        raise WriteRefused(f"{path.name} exists and isn't a regular file")
    existing = path.read_bytes() if path.is_file() else None
    previous = sidecar.projections().get(path.name)
    if existing is not None and previous is None and not overwrite_foreign:
        raise ForeignFile(path.name)
    ours = existing if (existing is not None and previous is not None) else None
    data = to_srt(cues) if fmt == "srt" else to_ass(cues, existing=ours)
    digest = sha256_hex(data)
    if (existing is not None and previous is not None and previous.rendition == rec.id
            and sha256_hex(existing) == digest):
        return sidecar, path, False
    record = ProjectionRecord(path=path.name, format=fmt, track=track, rendition=rec.id,
                              sha256=digest, at=now())
    sidecar = append(video, header, [record])
    safe_write(path, data, overwrite=existing is not None)
    return sidecar, path, True


def normalise_track(cues: Sequence[Cue]) -> list[Cue]:
    out: list[Cue] = []
    for c in sorted(cues, key=lambda c: (c.start_ms, c.end_ms)):
        if out and c.start_ms < out[-1].end_ms:
            out[-1] = out[-1].model_copy(update={"end_ms": c.start_ms})
        out.append(c)
    return [c for c in out if c.end_ms > c.start_ms and c.text.strip()]


def align_ids(parsed: Sequence[ParsedCue], projected: Sequence[Cue], *,
              tolerance_ms: int = 1000) -> list[Cue]:
    taken = {c.id for c in projected}
    used: set[str] = set()
    out: list[Cue] = []
    for p in parsed:
        match = next((c for c in projected if c.id not in used and c.text == p.text
                      and abs(c.start_ms - p.start_ms) <= tolerance_ms), None)
        if match is None:
            match = next((c for c in projected if c.id not in used
                          and abs(c.start_ms - p.start_ms) <= 100
                          and abs(c.end_ms - p.end_ms) <= 100), None)
        if match is None:
            out.append(Cue(id=new_cue_id(taken), start_ms=p.start_ms, end_ms=p.end_ms,
                           text=p.text))
            continue
        used.add(match.id)
        flags = match.flags
        if match.text != p.text and "edited" not in flags:
            flags = flags + ("edited",)
        out.append(match.model_copy(update={"start_ms": p.start_ms, "end_ms": p.end_ms,
                                            "text": p.text, "flags": flags}))
    return normalise_track(out)


def check_external(video: Path, header: Header, sidecar: Sidecar) -> tuple[Sidecar, list[str]]:
    renditions = sidecar.renditions()
    records: list = []
    changed: list[str] = []
    for name, proj in sidecar.projections().items():
        path = video.with_name(name)
        if not path.is_file() or path.is_symlink():
            continue
        data = path.read_bytes()
        digest = sha256_hex(data)
        if digest == proj.sha256:
            continue
        projected_body = renditions[proj.rendition].body
        projected = cues_from_body(projected_body)
        parsed = parse_subtitles(data, proj.format)
        if same_cues(parsed, projected, precision_ms=PRECISION_MS[proj.format]):
            records.append(proj.model_copy(update={"sha256": digest, "at": now()}))
            continue
        language, script = body_track_language(projected_body)
        body = cues_body(align_ids(parsed, projected), language=language, script=script,
                         engine="external", model=None, version=f"external@{digest[:12]}",
                         created_at=now(), inputs={"file_sha256": digest},
                         meta={"file": name, "role": projected_body.get("meta", {}).get("role")})
        rec = make_rendition([proj.rendition], proj.track, body)
        head = sidecar.head(proj.track)
        records += [rec,
                    HeadRecord(track=proj.track, rendition=rec.id,
                               replaces=head.rendition if isinstance(head, HeadRecord) else None,
                               by="external", at=now()),
                    ProjectionRecord(path=name, format=proj.format, track=proj.track,
                                     rendition=rec.id, sha256=digest, at=now())]
        changed.append(proj.track)
    if records:
        sidecar = append(video, header, records)
    return sidecar, changed


def import_subtitles(video: Path, header: Header, sub_path: Path, track: str, *,
                     role: str) -> Sidecar:
    data = sub_path.read_bytes()
    fmt = sub_path.suffix.lower().lstrip(".")
    parsed = parse_subtitles(data, fmt if fmt in ("srt", "ass", "ssa", "vtt") else None)
    taken: set[str] = set()
    cues = normalise_track([Cue(id=new_cue_id(taken), start_ms=p.start_ms, end_ms=p.end_ms,
                                text=p.text) for p in parsed])
    info = naming.track(track)
    digest = sha256_hex(data)
    body = cues_body(cues, language=info.lang, script=info.script, engine="import", model=None,
                     version=f"import@{digest[:12]}", created_at=now(),
                     inputs={"file_sha256": digest}, meta={"file": sub_path.name, "role": role})
    existing, _ = load(video)
    sidecar = existing or Sidecar(header)
    rec = make_rendition([], track, body)
    head = sidecar.head(track)
    records: list = [rec, HeadRecord(track=track, rendition=rec.id,
                                     replaces=head.rendition if isinstance(head, HeadRecord)
                                     else None, by="import", at=now())]
    if fmt in ("srt", "ass") and sub_path.resolve() == subtitle_path(video, track, fmt).resolve():
        records.append(ProjectionRecord(path=sub_path.name, format=fmt, track=track,
                                        rendition=rec.id, sha256=digest, at=now()))
    return append(video, header, records)
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the chat responder**

Append to `tests/fakes.py`:
```python
def _schema_ids(node):
    if isinstance(node, dict):
        props = node.get("properties")
        if isinstance(props, dict) and "src" in props:
            items = props["src"].get("items", {})
            return items.get("enum") or ([items["const"]] if "const" in items else [])
        for value in node.values():
            found = _schema_ids(value)
            if found:
                return found
    elif isinstance(node, list):
        for value in node:
            found = _schema_ids(value)
            if found:
                return found
    return []


def chat_responder(req):
    """An OpenAI-compatible chat completion that fills the request's batch schema."""
    body = req.json()
    ids = _schema_ids(body["response_format"]["json_schema"]["schema"])
    content = json.dumps({"items": [{"src": [i], "text": f"EN {i}"} for i in ids]})
    return 200, {
        "id": "chatcmpl-test", "object": "chat.completion", "created": 0, "model": body["model"],
        "choices": [{"index": 0, "finish_reason": "stop",
                     "message": {"role": "assistant", "content": content}}],
        "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 1, "completion_tokens": 1, "total_tokens": 2},
    }, {}
```

`tests/test_translate.py`:
```python
import re

import pytest
from fakes import FakeServer, chat_responder

from vid2sub.config import Config, Endpoint, ModelEntry
from vid2sub.engines.clients import KeyRing, RunPolicy
from vid2sub.subs.glossary import EMPTY_GLOSSARY, parse_glossary
from vid2sub.subs.model import Cue
from vid2sub.translate.run import TranslationFailed, make_ask, system_prompt, translate_cues

SOURCE = [Cue(id=f"s{i}", start_ms=i * 2000, end_ms=i * 2000 + 1500, text=f"テキスト{i}")
          for i in range(85)]


def fake_ask(*, fail_sizes=(), empty_for=()):
    calls = []

    def ask(system, prompt, model):
        ids = re.findall(r"^\[(\S+)\] \(max", prompt, flags=re.M)
        calls.append((system, prompt, ids))
        order = list(reversed(ids)) if len(ids) in fail_sizes else ids
        items = [{"src": [i], "text": "" if i in empty_for else f"EN {i}"} for i in order]
        return model.model_validate({"items": items})

    return ask, calls


def test_a_track_is_translated_in_batches_with_timing_from_the_source():
    ask, calls = fake_ask()
    out = translate_cues(SOURCE, source_lang="ja", target_track="en", glossary=EMPTY_GLOSSARY,
                         ask=ask)
    assert len(calls) == 3 and [len(c[2]) for c in calls] == [40, 40, 5]
    assert [c.text for c in out[:2]] == ["EN s0", "EN s1"]
    assert (out[3].start_ms, out[3].end_ms, out[3].src) == (6000, 7500, ("s3",))


def test_the_prompt_shows_context_and_limits_and_the_system_prompt_the_glossary():
    ask, calls = fake_ask()
    glossary = parse_glossary('description = "An anime"\n[terms]\n"工藤新一" = "Shinichi Kudo"\n')
    translate_cues(SOURCE, source_lang="ja", target_track="en", glossary=glossary, ask=ask)
    system, second_prompt, _ = calls[1]
    assert "Japanese to English" in system and "工藤新一 → Shinichi Kudo" in system
    assert "An anime" in system
    assert "Earlier lines" in second_prompt and "(max 30 characters)" in second_prompt


def test_a_batch_the_model_gets_wrong_is_split_and_retried():
    ask, calls = fake_ask(fail_sizes={40})
    out = translate_cues(SOURCE, source_lang="ja", target_track="en", glossary=EMPTY_GLOSSARY,
                         ask=ask)
    assert len(out) == 85
    assert [len(c[2]) for c in calls] == [40, 20, 20, 40, 20, 20, 5]


def test_a_single_cue_that_cant_be_translated_fails_loudly():
    ask, _ = fake_ask(empty_for={"s1"})
    with pytest.raises(TranslationFailed, match="s1"):
        translate_cues(SOURCE[:3], source_lang="ja", target_track="en", glossary=EMPTY_GLOSSARY,
                       ask=ask)


def test_only_the_asked_cues_are_translated():
    ask, calls = fake_ask()
    out = translate_cues(SOURCE, source_lang="ja", target_track="en", glossary=EMPTY_GLOSSARY,
                         ask=ask, only=["s1", "s2"])
    assert [c.src for c in out] == [("s1",), ("s2",)]
    assert calls[0][2] == ["s1", "s2"]


def test_chinese_targets_are_told_their_script():
    assert "Traditional Chinese" in system_prompt("ja", "zh-Hant", EMPTY_GLOSSARY)


def test_translation_goes_through_chatlas_with_a_schema_and_thinking_off():
    with FakeServer(responder=chat_responder) as srv:
        cfg = Config(
            endpoints={"spark": Endpoint(base_url=srv.url, key_env="SPARK_API_KEY")},
            models={"gemma": ModelEntry(catalog="gemma-4-26b-a4b", endpoint="spark",
                                        model="gemma-4-26b-a4b", task="translate", local=True,
                                        request_extra={"chat_template_kwargs":
                                                       {"enable_thinking": False}})})
        ask = make_ask("gemma", cfg.models["gemma"], cfg, RunPolicy(),
                       KeyRing(environ={"SPARK_API_KEY": "k"}))
        out = translate_cues(SOURCE[:3], source_lang="ja", target_track="en",
                             glossary=EMPTY_GLOSSARY, ask=ask)
        req = srv.requests[0]
    assert [c.text for c in out] == ["EN s0", "EN s1", "EN s2"]
    assert req.path == "/v1/chat/completions"
    body = req.json()
    assert body["response_format"]["type"] == "json_schema"
    assert body["chat_template_kwargs"] == {"enable_thinking": False}
    assert body["messages"][0]["role"] == "system"
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/translate/__init__.py`:
```python
"""Translation through chatlas (spec §13)."""
```

`src/vid2sub/translate/run.py`:
```python
"""Translating a track through chatlas (spec §13).

Batches of about 40 source cues, with read-only context either side, go to a fresh Chat each
(chat_structured adds turns to the Chat it's called on). The schema is built per batch so the
model can only name real cue ids; an answer that doesn't cover the batch exactly once, in order,
is split in half and retried, down to one cue at a time, and a cue that still fails stops the
track loudly. Items may cover several adjacent cues (Japanese→English reorders across cues).
Thinking is switched off through the model entry's `request_extra` (spec §13.1). The prompts
carry the glossary but never file names or titles.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping, Sequence

from pydantic import BaseModel

from vid2sub.config import Config, ModelEntry
from vid2sub.engines import clients
from vid2sub.subs import langs, naming
from vid2sub.subs.glossary import Glossary
from vid2sub.subs.model import Cue
from vid2sub.subs.translate_shape import (
    Batch, Item, assemble, batch_model, item_limits, items_from, plan_batches, split_batch,
    validate_partition,
)

PROMPT_VERSION = "t1"
AskFn = Callable[[str, str, type[BaseModel]], BaseModel]


class TranslationFailed(RuntimeError):
    pass


_WRITTEN = {"zh": "Chinese"}  # Mandarin is spoken; the written target is Chinese


def _name(code: str) -> str:
    return langs.get(code).name if code in langs.LANGS else code


def system_prompt(source_lang: str, target_track: str, glossary: Glossary) -> str:
    info = naming.track(target_track)
    script = {"Hant": " in Traditional Chinese characters",
              "Hans": " in Simplified Chinese characters"}.get(info.script or "", "")
    lines = [
        f"You translate subtitles from {_name(source_lang)} to {_WRITTEN.get(info.lang, _name(info.lang))}{script}.",
        "Write natural, concise subtitle text a viewer can read quickly. Keep the meaning; drop "
        "filler if a line would be too long.",
        "Never add notes, explanations, romanisation or the original text.",
        "Answer only with the JSON the request asks for.",
    ]
    if glossary.description:
        lines.append(f"About this video: {glossary.description}")
    pairs = [(s, t) for s, t in glossary.terms if t]
    if pairs:
        lines.append("Use these translations for names and terms:")
        lines += [f"- {s} → {t}" for s, t in pairs]
    return "\n".join(lines)


def batch_prompt(batch: Batch, cues: Mapping[str, Cue], limits: Mapping[str, int]) -> str:
    def show(cid: str) -> str:
        return cues[cid].text.replace("\n", " ")

    parts: list[str] = []
    if batch.before:
        parts.append("Earlier lines, for context only (don't translate them):")
        parts += [f"[{cid}] {show(cid)}" for cid in batch.before]
    parts.append("Translate these lines. Return items in order. Each item lists the ids it "
                 "covers (one id, or several adjacent ids when a sentence runs across them) and "
                 "its translation. Cover every id exactly once. Keep each translation within the "
                 "character limit shown.")
    parts += [f"[{cid}] (max {limits[cid]} characters) {show(cid)}" for cid in batch.ids]
    if batch.after:
        parts.append("Later lines, for context only (don't translate them):")
        parts += [f"[{cid}] {show(cid)}" for cid in batch.after]
    return "\n".join(parts)


def make_ask(name: str, entry: ModelEntry, config: Config, policy: clients.RunPolicy,
             keys: clients.KeyRing) -> AskFn:
    key_env = config.endpoints[entry.endpoint].key_env
    secrets = keys.secrets(config)

    def ask(system: str, prompt: str, data_model: type[BaseModel]) -> BaseModel:
        chat = clients.chat(name, entry, config, policy, keys, system_prompt=system)
        return clients.call_structured(chat, prompt, data_model,
                                       extra_body=entry.request_extra or None,
                                       key_env=key_env, secrets=secrets)

    return ask


def _translate(batch: Batch, all_ids: Sequence[str], cues: Mapping[str, Cue],
               limits: Mapping[str, int], system: str, ask: AskFn) -> list[Item]:
    try:
        result = ask(system, batch_prompt(batch, cues, limits), batch_model(batch.ids))
        return validate_partition(items_from(result), batch.ids)
    except ValueError as err:  # a PartitionError or pydantic's ValidationError
        if len(batch.ids) == 1:
            raise TranslationFailed(
                f"the model couldn't translate cue {batch.ids[0]} in the required shape") \
                from err
        items: list[Item] = []
        for half in split_batch(batch, all_ids):
            items += _translate(half, all_ids, cues, limits, system, ask)
        return items


def translate_cues(source: Sequence[Cue], *, source_lang: str, target_track: str,
                   glossary: Glossary, ask: AskFn, only: Sequence[str] | None = None,
                   taken: set[str] | None = None, batch_size: int = 40,
                   on_batch: Callable[[int, int], None] | None = None) -> list[Cue]:
    cues = {c.id: c for c in source}
    all_ids = [c.id for c in source]
    system = system_prompt(source_lang, target_track, glossary)
    limits = item_limits(source, target_track)
    batches = plan_batches(all_ids, only, size=batch_size)
    items: list[Item] = []
    for n, batch in enumerate(batches, 1):
        items += _translate(batch, all_ids, cues, limits, system, ask)
        if on_batch is not None:
            on_batch(n, len(batches))
    return assemble(items, cues, target_track, set() if taken is None else taken)
```

- Produces: `Detection(language, confidence, votes, ambiguous_zh_yue, mixed, detector)` with
  `uncertain` and `reason()`; `sample_windows(speech, duration_ms, *, positions=(0.1, 0.5, 0.8),
  window_ms=20_000) -> list[tuple[int, int]]`; `detect(engine, samples, speech, duration_ms, *,
  encode) -> Detection`; `mlx_available() -> bool`;
  `candidates(config, keys, *, probe_endpoints=True) -> list[Candidate]`;
  `choose_detector(cands, *, allow_cloud) -> str | None`.
- Produces (tests): `fakes.FakeEngine(name="whisper.cpp", language="ja", detect=None, fail=None,
  text=None)` (an `Engine` returning a word every 500 ms; `detect` may be a dict or a list of
  dicts used in turn; `fail` a list of exceptions raised by successive `transcribe` calls;
  `.calls` records `("transcribe", index, language, prompt)` and `("detect", index)`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the fake engine**

Add to the imports at the top of `tests/fakes.py`:
```python
from vid2sub.engines.base import ChunkResult, ModelStamp
from vid2sub.parse import ParsedChunk
from vid2sub.subs.model import Segment, Word
```

and append the class:
```python
class FakeEngine:
    """A transcription engine that hears one word every 500 ms of each chunk."""

    max_chunk_s = 300.0
    supports_prompt = True

    def __init__(self, name="whisper.cpp", language="ja", detect=None, fail=None, text=None):
        self.name = name
        self.language = language
        self.detect = detect if detect is not None else {language: 0.95}
        self.fail = list(fail or [])
        self.text = text or ("字" if language in ("ja", "zh", "yue") else " word")
        self.calls: list[tuple] = []

    def transcribe(self, chunk, *, language, prompt):
        self.calls.append(("transcribe", chunk.index, language, prompt))
        if self.fail:
            raise self.fail.pop(0)
        words = tuple(Word(text=self.text, start_ms=t, end_ms=t + 300)
                      for t in range(200, max(chunk.duration_ms - 400, 201), 500))
        if not words:
            return ChunkResult(ParsedChunk((), language, {}))
        seg = Segment(text="".join(w.text for w in words), start_ms=words[0].start_ms,
                      end_ms=words[-1].end_ms, words=words)
        return ChunkResult(ParsedChunk((seg,), language or self.language, {self.language: 0.95}))

    def detect_language(self, chunk):
        self.calls.append(("detect", chunk.index))
        if isinstance(self.detect, list):
            return dict(self.detect.pop(0))
        return dict(self.detect)

    def stamp(self):
        return ModelStamp(self.name, "fake-model", "unknown")

    def close(self):
        pass
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/detect.py`:
```python
"""Detecting the language from samples of speech, and finding what can run now (spec §10).

Three ~20 s windows are taken at 10%, 50% and 80% of the speech found (skipping theme songs and
intros); each is language-identified and the votes give a language, a confidence and a "mixed"
flag. Whisper often labels Cantonese as `zh`, so a Whisper `zh` is ambiguous and needs the user
(or `--from`). The best detector is Qwen3-ASR (97.9% language-ID accuracy in Qwen's benchmark),
then a local Whisper, then a cloud one if the run allows it. Candidates say which configured
models can run right now, and why the others can't: local endpoints are asked for their model
list; cloud ones are only checked for a key, never contacted.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import platform
import sys
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np

from vid2sub.catalog import CATALOG, Candidate
from vid2sub.config import Config, is_local
from vid2sub.engines import clients
from vid2sub.engines.base import Chunk, Engine, EngineError
from vid2sub.media.decode import SAMPLES_PER_MS

WHISPER_ENGINES = frozenset({"whisper.cpp", "openai", "faster-whisper"})
CONFIDENT = 0.6


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Detection:
    language: str | None
    confidence: float
    votes: tuple[str | None, ...]
    ambiguous_zh_yue: bool
    mixed: bool
    detector: str

    @property
    def uncertain(self) -> bool:
        return (self.language is None or self.confidence < CONFIDENT or self.ambiguous_zh_yue
                or self.mixed)

    def reason(self) -> str:
        if self.language is None:
            return "no language could be detected"
        if self.ambiguous_zh_yue:
            return "Whisper heard Chinese: is it Mandarin or Cantonese?"
        if self.mixed:
            return "the samples disagree: " + ", ".join(v or "?" for v in self.votes)
        if self.confidence < CONFIDENT:
            return f"only {self.confidence:.0%} sure it's {self.language}"
        return ""


def sample_windows(speech: Sequence[tuple[int, int]], duration_ms: int, *,
                   positions: Sequence[float] = (0.1, 0.5, 0.8),
                   window_ms: int = 20_000) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
    total = sum(e - s for s, e in speech)
    if total == 0:
        return []
    windows = []
    for position in positions:
        target, acc, t = position * total, 0, speech[-1][0]
        for s, e in speech:
            if acc + (e - s) >= target:
                t = s + int(target - acc)
                break
            acc += e - s
        start = max(0, min(t, duration_ms - window_ms))
        windows.append((start, min(duration_ms, start + window_ms)))
    return windows


def detect(engine: Engine, samples: np.ndarray, speech: Sequence[tuple[int, int]],
           duration_ms: int, *, encode: Callable[[np.ndarray], bytes]) -> Detection:
    votes: list[str | None] = []
    totals: dict[str, float] = defaultdict(float)
    for i, (s, e) in enumerate(sample_windows(speech, duration_ms)):
        chunk = Chunk(9000 + i, samples[s * SAMPLES_PER_MS:e * SAMPLES_PER_MS], encode)
        probs = engine.detect_language(chunk)
        if not probs:
            votes.append(None)
            continue
        votes.append(max(probs, key=probs.__getitem__))
        for lang, p in probs.items():
            totals[lang] += p
    counted = Counter(v for v in votes if v)
    if not counted:
        return Detection(None, 0.0, tuple(votes), False, False, engine.name)
    language = counted.most_common(1)[0][0]
    return Detection(language, round(totals[language] / len(votes), 3), tuple(votes),
                     engine.name in WHISPER_ENGINES and language == "zh", len(counted) > 1,
                     engine.name)


def mlx_available() -> bool:
    return (sys.platform == "darwin" and platform.machine() == "arm64"
            and importlib.util.find_spec("mlx_qwen3_asr") is not None)


def candidates(config: Config, keys: clients.KeyRing, *,
               probe_endpoints: bool = True) -> list[Candidate]:
    served: dict[str, tuple[bool, str, set[str]]] = {}
    out: list[Candidate] = []
    for name, m in config.models.items():
        if m.engine == "mlx-qwen3-asr":
            ok = mlx_available()
            out.append(Candidate(name, m.catalog, "in-process", ok,
                                 "" if ok else "install vid2sub[mac] on an Apple Silicon Mac"))
            continue
        ep = config.endpoints[m.endpoint]
        if not is_local(m, config):
            ok = bool(keys.key_for(m.endpoint, ep)) or not ep.key_env
            out.append(Candidate(name, m.catalog, "endpoint", ok,
                                 "" if ok else f"{ep.key_env} isn't set", cloud=True))
            continue
        if not probe_endpoints:
            out.append(Candidate(name, m.catalog, "endpoint", True))
            continue
        if m.endpoint not in served:
            try:
                served[m.endpoint] = (True, "", set(clients.list_models(m.endpoint, ep, keys)))
            except EngineError as err:
                reason = f"{err} ({err.hint})" if err.hint else str(err)
                served[m.endpoint] = (False, reason, set())
        ok, reason, ids = served[m.endpoint]
        if ok and ids and m.model not in ids:
            ok, reason = False, f"the server doesn't list {m.model}"
        out.append(Candidate(name, m.catalog, "endpoint", ok, reason))
    return out


def choose_detector(cands: Sequence[Candidate], *, allow_cloud: bool) -> str | None:
    usable = [c for c in cands if c.available and (allow_cloud or not c.cloud)
              and c.catalog in CATALOG and CATALOG[c.catalog].task == "transcribe"]
    for family in ("qwen3-asr", "whisper"):
        found = sorted((c for c in usable if CATALOG[c.catalog].family == family),
                       key=lambda c: (c.cloud, 0 if c.kind == "endpoint" else 1))
        if found:
            return found[0].name
    return None
```

### Task 31: The pipeline: one input, end to end

**Files:**
- Create: `src/vid2sub/awake.py`, `src/vid2sub/pipeline.py`
- Test: `tests/test_awake.py`, `tests/test_pipeline.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything from Tasks 2–30.
- Produces (`awake.py`): `keep_awake()` context manager.
- Produces (`pipeline.py`):
  - `RunOptions(source_lang=None, targets=["en"], formats=["srt"], model=None,
    translate_model=None, script=None, glossary=None, audio_track=None, keep_video=False,
    skip_existing=False, overwrite_foreign=False, allow_cloud=False, dry_run=False,
    on_uncertain="skip", wait_on_connection_s=600.0, replace_edited=False)`;
  - `InputResult(input, status ∈ {done, skipped, failed, uncertain, planned}, title="",
    reason="", hint="", kind="", notes=[], written=[], foreign=[], kept=[], plan={})`;
    `reason`, `written` and `foreign` may name files (people only); `kind` is safe for `--json`;
  - `RunContext(config, tools, keys, options, events=…, sleep=time.sleep, engines={},
    chunk_cache={}, folder_votes={}, candidates=None)` with `policy`, `engine(name)`,
    `get_candidates()`, `close()`;
  - `Paused(error)`, `StopRun`, `InputError(message, hint="")`;
  - `run_one(source: str, ctx: RunContext) -> InputResult`;
  - `PIPELINE` (8 hex: VAD, chunker and prompt policy); `MEDIA_SUFFIXES`.
- Events are `(kind, fields)` with kinds `stage` (`stage`), `chunk` (`index`, `of`), `batch`
  (`index`, `of`), `waiting` (`kind`, `seconds`); fields never hold titles or paths.

```python
import json
import re
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from fakes import FakeEngine
from helpers import SPEECH, make_clip
from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator

from vid2sub import pipeline, store
from vid2sub.catalog import Candidate

from vid2sub.media.tools import Tools

def fake_make_ask(*args, **kwargs):
    def ask(system, prompt, model):
        ids = re.findall(r"^\[(\S+)\] \(max", prompt, flags=re.M)
        return model.model_validate({"items": [{"src": [i], "text": f"EN {i}"} for i in ids]})

@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def no_real_translation(monkeypatch):
```
