```python
def make_ctx(engine, **options):
    cfg = Config(endpoints={"spark": Endpoint(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:9/v1")},
                 models={"fake": ModelEntry(catalog="whisper-large-v3-turbo", endpoint="spark",
                                            model="fake", parser="whisper.cpp", local=True),
                         "gemma": ModelEntry(catalog="gemma-4-26b-a4b", endpoint="spark",
                                             model="gemma", task="translate", local=True)})
    defaults = dict(source_lang="ja", model="fake", translate_model="gemma")
    defaults.update(options)
    sleeps: list[float] = []
    ctx = pipeline.RunContext(config=cfg, tools=Tools.find(), keys=KeyRing(environ={}),
                              options=pipeline.RunOptions(**defaults), sleep=sleeps.append)
    ctx.engines["fake"] = engine
    ctx.sleeps = sleeps
    return ctx


@pytest.fixture
def clip(tmp_path):
    return make_clip(tmp_path, "Show - S01E01.mkv", seconds=14, speech=SPEECH)


def test_a_japanese_episode_gets_japanese_and_english_subtitles_and_a_sidecar(clip):
    result = pipeline.run_one(str(clip), make_ctx(FakeEngine()))
    assert result.status == "done", (result.reason, result.hint)
    assert sorted(result.written) == ["Show - S01E01.en.srt", "Show - S01E01.ja.srt"]
    side, _ = store.load(clip)
    [transcript] = side.transcripts()
    Draft202012Validator(json.loads((CONTRACTS / "recordings-rendition.schema.json").read_text())
                         ).validate(transcript.body)
    assert isinstance(side.head("ja"), HeadRecord) and isinstance(side.head("en"), HeadRecord)
    assert side.identity() is not None
    assert "EN " in clip.with_name("Show - S01E01.en.srt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def test_a_second_run_with_skip_existing_does_nothing(clip):
    engine = FakeEngine()
    pipeline.run_one(str(clip), make_ctx(engine))
    calls = len(engine.calls)
    again = pipeline.run_one(str(clip), make_ctx(engine, skip_existing=True))
    assert again.status == "skipped" and len(engine.calls) == calls


def test_someone_elses_english_file_is_left_alone(clip):
    theirs = clip.with_name("Show - S01E01.en.srt")
    theirs.write_text("1\n00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:01,000\nBazarr\n", encoding="utf-8")
    result = pipeline.run_one(str(clip), make_ctx(FakeEngine()))
    assert result.foreign == ["Show - S01E01.en.srt"]
    assert "Bazarr" in theirs.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "Show - S01E01.ja.srt" in result.written


def test_cues_follow_an_audio_delay(tmp_path):
    clip = make_clip(tmp_path, "Delayed.mkv", seconds=14, speech=SPEECH, audio_delay=2.0)
    pipeline.run_one(str(clip), make_ctx(FakeEngine()))
    side, _ = store.load(clip)
    _, cues = store.current_cues(side, "ja")
    assert cues[0].start_ms >= 2000


def test_a_dropped_connection_waits_and_carries_on(clip):
    ctx = make_ctx(FakeEngine(fail=[EngineError("connection", "down")]))
    assert pipeline.run_one(str(clip), ctx).status == "done"
    assert ctx.sleeps


def test_a_connection_that_stays_down_pauses_the_run(clip):
    engine = FakeEngine(fail=[EngineError("connection", "down")] * 100)
    with pytest.raises(pipeline.Paused):
        pipeline.run_one(str(clip), make_ctx(engine, wait_on_connection_s=10))


def test_the_language_is_detected_when_not_given(clip):
    ctx = make_ctx(FakeEngine(detect={"ja": 0.95}), source_lang=None, model=None)
    ctx.candidates = [Candidate("fake", "whisper-large-v3-turbo", "endpoint", True),
                      Candidate("gemma", "gemma-4-26b-a4b", "endpoint", True)]
    assert pipeline.run_one(str(clip), ctx).status == "done"


def test_whisper_hearing_chinese_is_left_for_the_user_to_decide(clip):
    ctx = make_ctx(FakeEngine(detect={"zh": 0.95}), source_lang=None, model=None)
    ctx.candidates = [Candidate("fake", "whisper-large-v3-turbo", "endpoint", True)]
    result = pipeline.run_one(str(clip), ctx)
    assert result.status == "uncertain" and "--from" in result.hint
    assert not clip.with_name("Show - S01E01.en.srt").exists()


def test_cantonese_is_written_in_traditional_characters_as_zh_hk(clip):
    ctx = make_ctx(FakeEngine(language="yue", text="头"), source_lang="yue", targets=[])
    result = pipeline.run_one(str(clip), ctx)
    assert result.written == ["Show - S01E01.zh-HK.srt"]
    assert "頭" in clip.with_name("Show - S01E01.zh-HK.srt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def test_two_videos_with_the_same_name_are_refused(clip):
    clip.with_suffix(".mp4").write_bytes(b"x")
    result = pipeline.run_one(str(clip), make_ctx(FakeEngine()))
    assert result.status == "failed" and "same name" in result.reason


def test_a_missing_file_fails_with_a_reason(tmp_path):
    result = pipeline.run_one(str(tmp_path / "nope.mkv"), make_ctx(FakeEngine()))
    assert result.status == "failed" and "no file" in result.reason


def test_a_dry_run_writes_nothing(clip):
    result = pipeline.run_one(str(clip), make_ctx(FakeEngine(), dry_run=True))
    assert result.status == "planned" and not store.sidecar_path(clip).exists()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `awake.py`**

`src/vid2sub/awake.py`:
```python
"""Keep the machine awake while vid2sub works (spec §8.10; plan decision 5).

A season run from the CLI must survive the laptop's idle sleep. macOS: caffeinate, tied to this
process. Windows: SetThreadExecutionState. Linux: systemd-inhibit, when it exists. These are
long-running helpers rather than run_tool calls, so they're started here directly, by absolute
path, with the key-free environment.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from collections.abc import Iterator

from vid2sub.media.tools import which
from vid2sub.security import child_env

_ES_CONTINUOUS = 0x80000000
_ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED = 0x00000001


@contextlib.contextmanager
def keep_awake() -> Iterator[None]:
    proc: subprocess.Popen | None = None
    windows = False
    try:
        if sys.platform == "darwin" and os.path.exists("/usr/bin/caffeinate"):
            proc = subprocess.Popen(["/usr/bin/caffeinate", "-i", "-w", str(os.getpid())],
                                    env=child_env())
        elif os.name == "nt":
            import ctypes

            ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetThreadExecutionState(_ES_CONTINUOUS | _ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED)
            windows = True
        else:
            exe = which("systemd-inhibit")
            if exe:
                proc = subprocess.Popen([exe, "--what=idle:sleep", "--who=vid2sub",
                                         "--why=making subtitles", "--mode=block", "sleep",
                                         "infinity"], env=child_env())
        yield
    finally:
        if proc is not None:
            proc.terminate()
        if windows:
            import ctypes

            ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetThreadExecutionState(_ES_CONTINUOUS)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `pipeline.py`**

`src/vid2sub/pipeline.py`:
```python
"""One input, end to end (spec §17.2).

resolve (a path, or a YouTube download) → probe → glossary → the source track: reused from the
sidecar, or made now: the language (given, inherited from earlier files in the folder, or
detected), decode, find speech, chunk, transcribe with retries, place words, add the timeline
offset, build cues, convert the Chinese script → translate each target (only what's new or stale)
→ write the files → record the full hash. Connection errors and Spark refusals wait with backoff
up to a limit, then raise Paused so the CLI exits 75 and a re-run resumes; finished chunks are
cached for the life of the process. Any other error fails only this input, with the fix. Events
carry stages and counts, never titles or paths (spec §6.8).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import tempfile
import time
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal

import platformdirs

from vid2sub import detect as detecting
from vid2sub import store
from vid2sub.catalog import suggest
from vid2sub.config import Config, ConfigError
from vid2sub.engines.base import Chunk, ChunkResult, Engine, EngineError
from vid2sub.engines.clients import KeyRing, RunPolicy
from vid2sub.engines.registry import make_engine
from vid2sub.media import youtube
from vid2sub.media.chunk import chunk_samples, place, plan_chunks
from vid2sub.media.decode import SAMPLES_PER_MS, decode_to_wav, encode_flac, read_wav, to_float
from vid2sub.media.identity import full_sha256, identify
from vid2sub.media.probe import Probe, ProbeError, pick_track, probe, timeline_offset_ms
from vid2sub.media.tools import ToolFailed, ToolMissing, Tools
from vid2sub.media.vad import VAD_ID, speech_regions
from vid2sub.security import InputRefused, WriteRefused, resolve_input
from vid2sub.subs import langs, naming, scripts
from vid2sub.subs.build import build_cues
from vid2sub.subs.formats import SubtitleError
from vid2sub.subs.glossary import (
    EMPTY_GLOSSARY, Glossary, GlossaryError, chunk_prompt, parse_glossary, prompt_sha,
    vocabulary_prompt,
)
from vid2sub.subs.model import Segment, Transcript, shift_segment
from vid2sub.subs.rendition import cues_body, transcript_body
from vid2sub.subs.rules import rules_for
from vid2sub.subs.sidecar import HeadRecord, IdentityRecord, Sidecar, SidecarError, make_rendition
from vid2sub.subs.translate_shape import stale_ids
from vid2sub.translate.run import PROMPT_VERSION, TranslationFailed, make_ask, translate_cues

MEDIA_SUFFIXES = frozenset({".mkv", ".mp4", ".m4v", ".mov", ".webm", ".avi", ".ts", ".m2ts",
                            ".wmv", ".mp3", ".m4a", ".aac", ".flac", ".wav", ".ogg", ".opus"})
CHUNKER_VERSION = "c1"
PROMPT_POLICY = "p1"
PIPELINE = hashlib.sha256(f"{VAD_ID}|{CHUNKER_VERSION}|{PROMPT_POLICY}".encode()).hexdigest()[:8]
SUSPECT_COMPRESSION = 2.4
Event = Callable[[str, dict[str, Any]], None]


class Paused(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, error: EngineError):
        super().__init__(str(error))
        self.error = error


class StopRun(RuntimeError):
    pass


class InputError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, message: str, hint: str = ""):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.hint = hint


@dataclass
class RunOptions:
    source_lang: str | None = None
    targets: list[str] = field(default_factory=lambda: ["en"])
    formats: list[str] = field(default_factory=lambda: ["srt"])
    model: str | None = None
    translate_model: str | None = None
    script: str | None = None
    glossary: Path | None = None
    audio_track: int | str | None = None
    keep_video: bool = False
    skip_existing: bool = False
    overwrite_foreign: bool = False
    allow_cloud: bool = False
    dry_run: bool = False
    on_uncertain: Literal["proceed", "skip", "stop"] = "skip"
    wait_on_connection_s: float = 600.0
    replace_edited: bool = False


@dataclass
class InputResult:
    input: str
    status: Literal["done", "skipped", "failed", "uncertain", "planned"] = "failed"
    title: str = ""
    reason: str = ""  # for people: may name a file, so never put in --json output
    hint: str = ""
    kind: str = ""  # for scripts: an error kind or exception name, safe for --json
    notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    written: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    foreign: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    kept: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    plan: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


def _no_event(kind: str, fields: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    return None


@dataclass
class RunContext:
    config: Config
    tools: Tools
    keys: KeyRing
    options: RunOptions
    events: Event = _no_event
    sleep: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep
    engines: dict[str, Engine] = field(default_factory=dict)
    chunk_cache: dict[tuple, ChunkResult] = field(default_factory=dict)
    folder_votes: dict[Path, list[str]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    candidates: list | None = None

    @property
    def policy(self) -> RunPolicy:
        return RunPolicy(self.options.allow_cloud)

    def engine(self, name: str) -> Engine:
        if name not in self.engines:
            self.engines[name] = make_engine(name, self.config, self.policy, self.keys)
        return self.engines[name]

    def get_candidates(self) -> list:
        if self.candidates is None:
            self.candidates = detecting.candidates(self.config, self.keys)
        return self.candidates

    def close(self) -> None:
        for engine in self.engines.values():
            engine.close()


def run_one(source: str, ctx: RunContext) -> InputResult:
    result = InputResult(input=source)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="vid2sub-") as tmp:
        try:
            _run(source, ctx, Path(tmp), result)
        except (Paused, StopRun):
            raise
        except (EngineError, ToolMissing, youtube.YouTubeError, InputError) as err:
            _fail(result, str(err), getattr(err, "hint", ""),
                  getattr(err, "kind", type(err).__name__))
        except ToolFailed as err:
            _fail(result, f"{err.tool} couldn't process this file",
                  "check that it plays in a media player", "ToolFailed")
        except (youtube.NotYouTube, ProbeError, InputRefused, SubtitleError, GlossaryError,
                ConfigError, SidecarError, store.StoreError, WriteRefused, TranslationFailed,
                naming.UnknownTrack, langs.UnknownLanguage) as err:
            _fail(result, str(err), kind=type(err).__name__)
    return result


def _fail(result: InputResult, reason: str, hint: str = "", kind: str = "") -> None:
    result.status, result.reason, result.hint, result.kind = "failed", reason, hint, kind


def _run(source: str, ctx: RunContext, scratch: Path, result: InputResult) -> None:
    opts = ctx.options
    video = _resolve(source, ctx, scratch, result)
    ctx.events("stage", {"stage": "probe"})
    p = probe(video, ctx.tools, scratch=scratch)
    header = store.header_for(video, identify(video), p.duration_ms)
    sidecar, _ = store.load(video)
    if sidecar is not None:
        sidecar, changed = store.check_external(video, header, sidecar)
        result.notes += [f"kept your outside edit to {t}" for t in changed]
    glossary = _glossary(video, opts)
    targets = [naming.target_track(t) for t in opts.targets]
    src_track = store.source_track(sidecar) if sidecar is not None else None
    if opts.skip_existing and _all_exist(video, targets, opts.formats, src_track):
        result.status, result.reason = "skipped", "its subtitle files already exist"
        return
    if opts.dry_run:
        result.status = "planned"
        result.plan = {
            "source": src_track or (naming.source_track(opts.source_lang, opts.script)
                                    if opts.source_lang else "detect"),
            "transcribe": "reuse the sidecar" if src_track else (opts.model or "suggested"),
            "targets": targets,
            "files": [store.subtitle_path(video, t, f).name
                      for t in ([src_track] if src_track else []) + targets
                      for f in opts.formats],
        }
        return
    if src_track is None:
        sidecar, src_track = _transcribe(video, p, header, sidecar, glossary, ctx, scratch,
                                         result)
        if src_track is None:
            return
    sidecar = _translate_targets(video, header, sidecar, src_track, targets, glossary, ctx,
                                 result)
    tracks = [src_track] + [t for t in targets if t != src_track]
    sidecar = _write(video, header, sidecar, tracks, ctx, result)
    if sidecar.identity() is None:
        ctx.events("stage", {"stage": "hash"})
        store.append(video, header, [IdentityRecord(sha256=full_sha256(video), at=store.now())])
    result.status = "done"


def _resolve(source: str, ctx: RunContext, scratch: Path, result: InputResult) -> Path:
    if youtube.is_url(source):
        configured = ctx.config.defaults.youtube_dir
        out_dir = (Path(configured).expanduser() if configured
                   else Path(platformdirs.user_videos_dir()) / "vid2sub")
        ctx.events("stage", {"stage": "download"})
        fetched = youtube.download(source, out_dir=out_dir, scratch=scratch,
                                   keep_video=ctx.options.keep_video, tools=ctx.tools)
        result.title = fetched.title
        return fetched.path
    path = resolve_input(source)
    if not path.is_file():
        raise InputError("there's no file at that path")
    result.title = path.name
    twins = [p for p in path.parent.iterdir() if p != path and p.stem == path.stem
             and p.suffix.lower() in MEDIA_SUFFIXES]
    if twins:
        raise InputError("another video in this folder has the same name, so their subtitles "
                         "would collide (spec §8.3)", "rename one of them")
    return path


def _glossary(video: Path, opts: RunOptions) -> Glossary:
    path = opts.glossary or video.parent / naming.GLOSSARY_NAME
    if not path.is_file():
        if opts.glossary is not None:
            raise InputError("that glossary file doesn't exist")
        return EMPTY_GLOSSARY
    return parse_glossary(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def _all_exist(video: Path, targets: list[str], formats: list[str],
               src_track: str | None) -> bool:
    tracks = list(targets) + ([src_track] if src_track else [])
    return bool(tracks) and all(store.subtitle_path(video, t, f).exists()
                                for t in tracks for f in formats)


def _choose(video: Path, samples, speech, encode, ctx: RunContext,
            result: InputResult) -> tuple[str | None, str | None, dict]:
    opts = ctx.options
    lang, meta = opts.source_lang, {}
    if lang is None:
        votes = ctx.folder_votes.get(video.parent, [])
        if len(votes) >= 2 and len(set(votes)) == 1:
            lang, meta = votes[0], {"inherited_from_folder": True}
        else:
            detector = detecting.choose_detector(ctx.get_candidates(),
                                                 allow_cloud=opts.allow_cloud)
            if detector is None:
                raise InputError("no model is available to detect the language",
                                 "pass --from LANG, or check `vid2sub models`")
            ctx.events("stage", {"stage": "detect"})
            found = detecting.detect(ctx.engine(detector), samples, speech,
                                     len(samples) // SAMPLES_PER_MS, encode=encode)
            meta = {"detector": found.detector, "votes": list(found.votes),
                    "confidence": found.confidence}
            if found.uncertain:
                if opts.on_uncertain == "stop":
                    raise StopRun(found.reason())
                if opts.on_uncertain == "skip" or found.language is None:
                    result.status, result.reason = "uncertain", found.reason()
                    result.hint = "run it again with --from LANG (for example --from yue)"
                    return None, None, meta
            lang = found.language
            ctx.folder_votes.setdefault(video.parent, []).append(lang)
    model = opts.model
    if model is None:
        s = suggest(lang, ctx.get_candidates(), "transcribe", allow_cloud=opts.allow_cloud)
        if s.model is None:
            raise InputError(f"no usable transcription model for {langs.get(lang).name}",
                             "; ".join(f"{n}: {r}" for n, r in s.considered)
                             or "add one to vid2sub.toml (`vid2sub config init`)")
        if s.warning:
            result.notes.append(s.warning)
        model = s.model
    return lang, model, meta


def _with_retries(engine: Engine, chunk: Chunk, *, language: str, prompt: str,
                  ctx: RunContext) -> ChunkResult:
    waited, server_tries = 0.0, 0
    while True:
        try:
            return engine.transcribe(chunk, language=language, prompt=prompt)
        except EngineError as err:
            if err.kind == "server" and server_tries < 2:
                server_tries += 1
                ctx.sleep(2.0 ** server_tries)
                continue
            if err.kind in ("connection", "refused"):
                delay = err.retry_after or min(30.0, 5.0 + waited / 4)
                if waited + delay > ctx.options.wait_on_connection_s:
                    raise Paused(err) from None
                ctx.events("waiting", {"kind": err.kind, "seconds": round(delay)})
                ctx.sleep(delay)
                waited += delay
                continue
            raise


def _transcribe(video: Path, p: Probe, header, sidecar: Sidecar | None, glossary: Glossary,
                ctx: RunContext, scratch: Path,
                result: InputResult) -> tuple[Sidecar | None, str | None]:
    opts = ctx.options
    track = pick_track(p, opts.source_lang, opts.audio_track)
    offset = timeline_offset_ms(p, track)
    ctx.events("stage", {"stage": "decode"})
    wav = scratch / "audio.wav"
    decode_to_wav(video, track, wav, ctx.tools, scratch=scratch, duration_ms=p.duration_ms)
    samples = read_wav(wav)
    ctx.events("stage", {"stage": "speech"})
    speech = speech_regions(to_float(samples))

    def encode(s):
        return encode_flac(s, ctx.tools, scratch=scratch)

    lang, model_name, detection = _choose(video, samples, speech, encode, ctx, result)
    if lang is None:
        return sidecar, None
    engine = ctx.engine(model_name)
    duration = len(samples) // SAMPLES_PER_MS
    plans = plan_chunks(speech, duration, engine.max_chunk_s)
    cjk = langs.get(lang).cjk
    segments: list[Segment] = []
    tail, answered = "", None
    for plan in plans:
        ctx.events("chunk", {"index": plan.index + 1, "of": len(plans)})
        key = (header.video.quick_sha256, track.stream_index, model_name, lang, plan.plan_hash())
        if key not in ctx.chunk_cache:
            chunk = Chunk(plan.index, chunk_samples(samples, plan, speech), encode)
            prompt = chunk_prompt(glossary, tail, cjk=cjk) if engine.supports_prompt else ""
            ctx.chunk_cache[key] = _with_retries(engine, chunk, language=lang, prompt=prompt,
                                                 ctx=ctx)
        res = ctx.chunk_cache[key]
        answered = res.answered_model or answered
        placed = place(plan, res.parsed.segments)
        segments += placed
        suspect = any((s.compression_ratio or 0) > SUSPECT_COMPRESSION for s in placed)
        tail = "" if suspect else "".join(s.text for s in placed)[-200:]
    transcript = Transcript(language=lang,
                            segments=tuple(shift_segment(s, offset) for s in segments))
    stamp = engine.stamp()
    vocab = prompt_sha(vocabulary_prompt(glossary.source_terms())) if engine.supports_prompt \
        else ""
    now = store.now()
    t_body = transcript_body(
        transcript, engine=stamp.engine, model=stamp.model,
        version=f"{stamp.label}+v{vocab or '0'}+p{PIPELINE}", created_at=now,
        inputs={"audio_sha256": full_sha256(wav), "video_quick_sha256": header.video.quick_sha256,
                "audio_track": track.index, "timeline_offset_ms": offset},
        meta={"vad": VAD_ID, "chunker": CHUNKER_VERSION, "prompt_policy": PROMPT_POLICY,
              "speech": [list(r) for r in speech], "chunks": len(plans),
              "answered_model": answered, "detection": detection})
    t_rec = make_rendition([], None, t_body)
    rules = rules_for(lang)
    cues = build_cues(transcript, rules,
                      speech=[(s + offset, e + offset) for s, e in speech])
    src_track = naming.source_track(lang, opts.script)
    info = naming.track(src_track)
    c_rec = make_rendition([t_rec.id], src_track, cues_body(
        cues, language=lang, script=info.script, engine="vid2sub", model=None,
        version=f"cues@{rules.sha()}", created_at=now, inputs={"transcript": t_rec.id},
        meta={"role": "source"}))
    records: list = [t_rec, c_rec]
    head_target = c_rec
    config = scripts.config_for(src_track)
    if config:
        head_target = make_rendition([c_rec.id], src_track, cues_body(
            scripts.convert_cues(cues, config), language=lang, script=info.script,
            engine=f"opencc:{config}", model=None, version=f"opencc@{config}", created_at=now,
            inputs={"cues": c_rec.id}, meta={"role": "source"}))
        records.append(head_target)
    base = sidecar or Sidecar(header)
    head = store.head_record(base, src_track, head_target.id, "auto",
                             replace_person=opts.replace_edited)
    if head is None and base.head(src_track) is not None:
        result.kept.append(src_track)
    sidecar = store.append(video, header, records + ([head] if head else []))
    return sidecar, src_track


def _translator(src_lang: str, ctx: RunContext) -> str:
    if ctx.options.translate_model:
        return ctx.options.translate_model
    s = suggest(src_lang, ctx.get_candidates(), "translate", allow_cloud=ctx.options.allow_cloud)
    if s.model is None:
        raise InputError("no usable translation model",
                         "; ".join(f"{n}: {r}" for n, r in s.considered)
                         or "add one to vid2sub.toml (`vid2sub config init`)")
    return s.model


def _translate_targets(video: Path, header, sidecar: Sidecar, src_track: str,
                       targets: list[str], glossary: Glossary, ctx: RunContext,
                       result: InputResult) -> Sidecar:
    opts = ctx.options
    src_rec, source = store.current_cues(sidecar, src_track)
    src_lang = naming.track(src_track).lang
    by_id = {c.id: c for c in source}
    for target in targets:
        if target == src_track:
            continue
        if opts.skip_existing and all(store.subtitle_path(video, target, f).exists()
                                      for f in opts.formats):
            continue
        model_name = _translator(src_lang, ctx)
        entry = ctx.config.models.get(model_name)
        if entry is None or entry.task != "translate":
            raise ConfigError(f"{model_name} isn't a translation model in vid2sub.toml")
        existing = sidecar.head(target)
        parents, keep, todo = [src_rec.id], [], list(by_id)
        if isinstance(existing, HeadRecord):
            old_rec, old = store.current_cues(sidecar, target)
            parents.append(old_rec.id)
            same = (old_rec.body["payload"].get("glossary_sha", "") == (glossary.sha() or "")
                    and old_rec.body.get("model") == entry.model)
            stale = stale_ids(old, by_id) if same else {c.id for c in old}
            keep = [c for c in old if c.id not in stale or "edited" in c.flags]
            covered = {i for c in keep for i in (c.src or ())}
            todo = [i for i in by_id if i not in covered]
            if not todo:
                continue
        ctx.events("stage", {"stage": "translate"})
        ask = make_ask(model_name, entry, ctx.config, ctx.policy, ctx.keys)
        new = translate_cues(source, source_lang=src_lang, target_track=target,
                             glossary=glossary, ask=ask, only=todo if keep else None,
                             taken={c.id for c in keep},
                             on_batch=lambda n, total: ctx.events("batch",
                                                                  {"index": n, "of": total}))
        info = naming.track(target)
        rec = make_rendition(parents, target, cues_body(
            store.normalise_track(keep + new), language=info.lang, script=info.script,
            engine=f"translate:{model_name}", model=entry.model,
            version=f"{entry.model}@unknown+g{glossary.sha() or '0'}+{PROMPT_VERSION}",
            created_at=store.now(), inputs={"source": src_rec.id}, meta={"role": "translation"},
            glossary_sha=glossary.sha() or None))
        head = store.head_record(sidecar, target, rec.id, "auto",
                                 replace_person=opts.replace_edited)
        if head is None and isinstance(existing, HeadRecord):
            result.kept.append(target)
        sidecar = store.append(video, header, [rec] + ([head] if head else []))
    return sidecar


def _write(video: Path, header, sidecar: Sidecar, tracks: list[str], ctx: RunContext,
           result: InputResult) -> Sidecar:
    opts = ctx.options
    for track in tracks:
        if not isinstance(sidecar.head(track), HeadRecord):
            continue
        for fmt in opts.formats:
            try:
                sidecar, path, written = store.project(video, header, sidecar, track, fmt,
                                                       overwrite_foreign=opts.overwrite_foreign)
            except store.ForeignFile as err:
                result.foreign.append(err.name)
                continue
            if written:
                result.written.append(path.name)
    return sidecar
```

```python
def fake_make_ask(*args, **kwargs):
    """Stands in for translate.run.make_ask: 'EN <id>' for every cue it's asked about."""
    def ask(system, prompt, model):
        ids = re.findall(r"^\[(\S+)\] \(max", prompt, flags=re.M)
        return model.model_validate({"items": [{"src": [i], "text": f"EN {i}"} for i in ids]})
    return ask
```

```python
from vid2sub.commands._common import duration, expand

    path.write_bytes(b"x")
    return path

                 ".DS_Store", "Show S01E01.en.srt", "notes.txt"):
```

```python
    assert result["status"] == "done" and result["written"] == 2

def test_ctrl_c_exits_75(setup):

    engine.fail = [KeyboardInterrupt()]
    assert main(["run", str(clip), "--from", "ja", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 75


def test_usage_and_config_errors_have_their_own_exit_codes(setup, tmp_path):
    cfg, _, clip = setup
    assert main(["run", str(clip), "--from", "xx", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 64
    assert main(["run", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 64
    bad = tmp_path / "bad.toml"
    bad.write_text('[endpoints.x]\nbase_url = "https://a.example/v1"\nkey_env = "sk-oops"\n')
    assert main(["run", str(clip), "--config", str(bad)]) == 78

    cfg, _, clip = setup

    [report] = [e for e in events(capsys.readouterr().out) if e["event"] == "detect"]
    assert report["language"] == "ja" and report["transcribe"] == "fake"
    assert report["translate"] == "gemma" and report["uncertain"] is False
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `_common.py`**

`src/vid2sub/commands/_common.py`:
```python
"""What the commands share: settings, inputs, output and the run context (spec §18).

Output is for people in a terminal, or JSON Lines for scripts (`--json`). Titles and paths reach
only an interactive terminal: piped plain output names inputs by number, and JSON carries
stages, counts and error kinds, never names (spec §6.8, §18.2). Folders expand to their videos in
episode order (`S01E02` before `S01E10`), others by natural name order after them.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import re
import sys
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, TextIO

from vid2sub import exitcodes
from vid2sub.config import Config, ConfigError, config_path, load_config
from vid2sub.engines.clients import KeyRing
from vid2sub.media.tools import Tools
from vid2sub.media.youtube import is_url
from vid2sub.pipeline import MEDIA_SUFFIXES, RunContext, RunOptions
from vid2sub.security import resolve_input

_EPISODE = re.compile(r"[Ss](\d{1,3})[ ._-]*[Ee](\d{1,4})")


class CommandError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, code: int, message: str, hint: str = ""):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.hint = hint


def add_common(parser: argparse.ArgumentParser) -> None:
    parser.add_argument("--config", metavar="PATH", help="the vid2sub.toml to use")
    parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true",
                        help="JSON Lines events for scripts (never titles or paths)")


def load(args: argparse.Namespace) -> Config:
    try:
        return load_config(config_path(args.config))
    except ConfigError as err:
        raise CommandError(exitcodes.CONFIG, str(err),
                           "fix vid2sub.toml (`vid2sub config init` prints a starter)") from None


def episode_key(path: Path) -> tuple:
    natural = [int(t) if t.isdigit() else t.lower() for t in re.split(r"(\d+)", path.name)]
    m = _EPISODE.search(path.name)
    return (0, int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2)), natural) if m else (1, 0, 0, natural)


def expand(inputs: Sequence[str], *, recursive: bool = False,
           from_file: str | None = None) -> list[str]:
    items = list(inputs)
    if from_file:
        text = sys.stdin.read() if from_file == "-" else Path(from_file).read_text(
            encoding="utf-8")
        items += [line.strip() for line in text.splitlines()
                  if line.strip() and not line.lstrip().startswith("#")]
    out: list[str] = []
    for item in items:
        if is_url(item):
            out.append(item)
            continue
        path = resolve_input(item)
        if path.is_dir():
            found = path.rglob("*") if recursive else path.iterdir()
            videos = [f for f in found if f.is_file() and not f.name.startswith(".")
                      and f.suffix.lower() in MEDIA_SUFFIXES]
            out += [str(f) for f in sorted(videos, key=lambda f: (str(f.parent), episode_key(f)))]
        else:
            out.append(str(path))
    return out


def duration(value: str) -> float:
    m = re.fullmatch(r"(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*([smh]?)", value.strip())
    if not m:
        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("use seconds, or a number with s, m or h (like 10m)")
    return float(m.group(1)) * {"": 1, "s": 1, "m": 60, "h": 3600}[m.group(2)]


def audio_track(value: str) -> int | str:
    return int(value) if value.isdigit() else value


class Output:
    def __init__(self, json_mode: bool, stream: TextIO | None = None, err: TextIO | None = None):
        self.json = json_mode
        self.stream = stream or sys.stdout
        self.err = err or sys.stderr
        self.tty = not json_mode and self.stream.isatty()

    def event(self, kind: str, **fields: Any) -> None:
        if self.json:
            self.stream.write(json.dumps({"event": kind, **fields}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
            self.stream.flush()

    def say(self, text: str) -> None:
        if not self.json:
            print(text, file=self.stream, flush=True)

    def warn(self, text: str) -> None:
        print(text, file=self.err, flush=True)

    def progress(self, kind: str, fields: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        if self.json:
            self.event(kind, **fields)
            return
        if not self.tty:
            return
        lines = {
            "stage": lambda f: f"  {f['stage']}…",
            "chunk": lambda f: f"  transcribing {f['index']}/{f['of']}",
            "batch": lambda f: f"  translating {f['index']}/{f['of']}",
            "waiting": lambda f: f"  waiting {f['seconds']} s ({f['kind']})…",
        }
        if kind in lines:
            self.say(lines[kind](fields))

    def name(self, title: str, n: int) -> str:
        return title if self.tty and title else f"input {n}"


def fail(output: Output, err: CommandError) -> int:
    output.warn(f"vid2sub: {err}" + (f"\n  → {err.hint}" if err.hint else ""))
    output.event("error", code=err.code)
    return err.code


def new_context(config: Config, options: RunOptions, output: Output) -> RunContext:
    return RunContext(config=config, tools=Tools.find(), keys=KeyRing(), options=options,
                      events=output.progress)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add `detect_one` to the pipeline**

Add to `src/vid2sub/pipeline.py` (import `Suggestion` alongside `suggest` from
`vid2sub.catalog`):
```python
@dataclass
class DetectReport:
    input: str
    title: str = ""
    duration_ms: int = 0
    tracks: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    detection: detecting.Detection | None = None
    transcribe: Suggestion | None = None
    translate: Suggestion | None = None
    error: str = ""
    kind: str = ""
    hint: str = ""


def detect_one(source: str, ctx: RunContext) -> DetectReport:
    """What `run` would do for one file, without transcribing it (spec §18.1)."""
    report = DetectReport(input=source)
    if youtube.is_url(source):
        report.error, report.kind = "detect works on files, not links", "NotAFile"
        report.hint = "download it first with `vid2sub run URL --to none`"
        return report
    opts = ctx.options
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="vid2sub-") as tmp:
        scratch = Path(tmp)
        try:
            path = resolve_input(source)
            if not path.is_file():
                raise InputError("there's no file at that path")
            report.title = path.name
            p = probe(path, ctx.tools, scratch=scratch)
            report.duration_ms = p.duration_ms
            report.tracks = [f"{t.index}: {t.language or t.tag or '?'} {t.codec}"
                             f"{' (default)' if t.default else ''}" for t in p.audio]
            cands = ctx.get_candidates()
            lang = opts.source_lang
            if lang is None:
                track = pick_track(p, None, opts.audio_track)
                wav = scratch / "audio.wav"
                decode_to_wav(path, track, wav, ctx.tools, scratch=scratch,
                              duration_ms=p.duration_ms)
                samples = read_wav(wav)
                detector = detecting.choose_detector(cands, allow_cloud=opts.allow_cloud)
                if detector is None:
                    raise InputError("no model is available to detect the language",
                                     "check `vid2sub models`")
                report.detection = detecting.detect(
                    ctx.engine(detector), samples, speech_regions(to_float(samples)),
                    len(samples) // SAMPLES_PER_MS,
                    encode=lambda s: encode_flac(s, ctx.tools, scratch=scratch))
                lang = report.detection.language
            if lang:
                report.transcribe = suggest(lang, cands, "transcribe",
                                            allow_cloud=opts.allow_cloud)
                report.translate = suggest(lang, cands, "translate",
                                           allow_cloud=opts.allow_cloud)
        except (EngineError, ToolMissing, InputError) as err:
            report.error, report.hint = str(err), getattr(err, "hint", "")
            report.kind = getattr(err, "kind", type(err).__name__)
        except (ToolFailed, ProbeError, InputRefused, ConfigError) as err:
            report.error, report.kind = str(err), type(err).__name__
    return report
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the commands**

`src/vid2sub/commands/run.py`:
```python
"""`vid2sub run`: subtitles for videos, folders and YouTube links (spec §18.1).

One input at a time, the machine kept awake. A dropped connection that doesn't come back within
--wait-on-connection, or Ctrl-C, exits 75: re-running the same command with --skip-existing
carries on. Inputs whose language was uncertain are listed at the end with the command that would
run them.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import shlex
from pathlib import Path

from vid2sub import exitcodes
from vid2sub.awake import keep_awake
from vid2sub.commands import _common
from vid2sub.commands._common import CommandError, Output, add_common, audio_track, duration
from vid2sub.config import Config
from vid2sub.engines.clients import quiet_libraries
from vid2sub.pipeline import InputResult, Paused, RunOptions, StopRun, run_one
from vid2sub.subs import langs, naming

SYMBOL = {"done": "✓", "skipped": "–", "failed": "✗", "uncertain": "?", "planned": "·"}


def add_parser(sub) -> None:
    p = sub.add_parser("run", help="make subtitles for videos, folders or YouTube links")
    p.add_argument("inputs", nargs="*", metavar="INPUT",
                   help="a video, a folder of videos, or a YouTube link")
    p.add_argument("--from-file", metavar="LIST",
                   help="read inputs from a file, one per line (- for stdin)")
    p.add_argument("--recursive", action="store_true", help="look inside subfolders too")
    p.add_argument("--from", dest="source_lang", default="auto", metavar="LANG",
                   help="the spoken language: en, ja, zh, yue, ko, or auto (the default)")
    p.add_argument("--to", dest="targets", nargs="+", metavar="LANG",
                   help="languages to translate into (default from vid2sub.toml; `none` for "
                        "captions in the spoken language only)")
    p.add_argument("--model", help="the transcription model, by its name in vid2sub.toml")
    p.add_argument("--translate-model", help="the translation model, by its name in vid2sub.toml")
    p.add_argument("--format", dest="formats", action="append", choices=["srt", "ass"])
    p.add_argument("--script", choices=["hant", "hans"],
                   help="Traditional or Simplified characters for Mandarin")
    p.add_argument("--glossary", type=Path, metavar="FILE")
    p.add_argument("--audio-track", type=audio_track, metavar="N|LANG")
    p.add_argument("--keep-video", action="store_true",
                   help="YouTube: keep the video too, not only the audio")
    p.add_argument("--skip-existing", action="store_true",
                   help="skip languages whose subtitle files already exist")
    p.add_argument("--overwrite-foreign", action="store_true",
                   help="replace subtitle files vid2sub didn't write")
    p.add_argument("--replace-edited", action="store_true",
                   help="let a new version replace one you edited")
    p.add_argument("--allow-cloud", action="store_true",
                   help="allow cloud models for this run (audio or text leaves this machine)")
    p.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="say what would happen, write nothing")
    p.add_argument("--on-uncertain", choices=["proceed", "skip", "stop"], default="skip",
                   help="when the language is unclear: carry on, skip that file (default), or "
                        "stop")
    p.add_argument("--wait-on-connection", type=duration, default=600.0, metavar="DURATION",
                   help="how long to wait for a dropped connection (default 10m)")
    add_common(p)
    p.set_defaults(handler=run)


def options_from(args: argparse.Namespace, config: Config) -> RunOptions:
    source = None if args.source_lang == "auto" else args.source_lang
    if source is not None:
        langs.get(source)
    if args.targets == ["none"]:
        targets: list[str] = []
    else:
        targets = [naming.target_track(t) for t in (args.targets or config.defaults.targets)]
    return RunOptions(
        source_lang=source, targets=targets, formats=args.formats or list(config.defaults.formats),
        model=args.model, translate_model=args.translate_model, script=args.script,
        glossary=args.glossary, audio_track=args.audio_track, keep_video=args.keep_video,
        skip_existing=args.skip_existing, overwrite_foreign=args.overwrite_foreign,
        allow_cloud=args.allow_cloud, dry_run=args.dry_run, on_uncertain=args.on_uncertain,
        wait_on_connection_s=args.wait_on_connection, replace_edited=args.replace_edited)


def report(out: Output, n: int, total: int, r: InputResult) -> None:
    out.event("result", index=n, status=r.status, kind=r.kind, written=len(r.written),
              foreign=len(r.foreign), kept=len(r.kept))
    line = f"{SYMBOL[r.status]} {out.name(r.title, n)} ({n}/{total}): {r.status}"
    if r.written:
        line += f", wrote {len(r.written)} file{'s' if len(r.written) != 1 else ''}"
    out.say(line)
    if out.tty:
        for name in r.written:
            out.say(f"    {name}")
        for key, value in r.plan.items():
            out.say(f"    {key}: {value}")
    if r.reason:
        out.say(f"    {r.reason if out.tty else (r.kind or r.status)}")
    if r.hint:
        out.say(f"    → {r.hint}")
    if r.foreign:
        out.say(f"    left {len(r.foreign)} file(s) vid2sub didn't write "
                "(--overwrite-foreign replaces them)")
    if r.kept:
        out.say(f"    kept your edited {', '.join(r.kept)} "
                "(--replace-edited switches to the new version)")
    for note in r.notes:
        out.say(f"    {note}")


def summary(out: Output, results: list[InputResult]) -> None:
    counts = {status: sum(r.status == status for r in results) for status in SYMBOL}
    out.event("summary", **counts)
    out.say(" · ".join(f"{v} {k}" for k, v in counts.items() if v) or "nothing ran")
    again = [r for r in results if r.status in ("uncertain", "failed")]
    if again and out.tty:
        out.say("To run these again:")
        for r in again:
            extra = " --from LANG" if r.status == "uncertain" else ""
            out.say(f"  vid2sub run {shlex.quote(r.input)}{extra}")


def run(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    out = Output(args.json)
    quiet_libraries()
    try:
        config = _common.load(args)
        try:
            options = options_from(args, config)
        except (langs.UnknownLanguage, naming.UnknownTrack) as err:
            raise CommandError(exitcodes.USAGE, str(err)) from None
        inputs = _common.expand(args.inputs, recursive=args.recursive, from_file=args.from_file)
    except CommandError as err:
        return _common.fail(out, err)
    if not inputs:
        return _common.fail(out, CommandError(
            exitcodes.USAGE, "nothing to do",
            "give a video, a folder, a YouTube link, or --from-file"))
    ctx = _common.new_context(config, options, out)
    results: list[InputResult] = []
    code = exitcodes.OK
    try:
        with keep_awake():
            for n, item in enumerate(inputs, 1):
                out.event("input", index=n, of=len(inputs))
                result = run_one(item, ctx)
                results.append(result)
                report(out, n, len(inputs), result)
    except Paused as err:
        out.warn(f"vid2sub: paused: {err}" + (f" ({err.error.hint})" if err.error.hint else ""))
        out.warn("  → when it's back, run the same command with --skip-existing to carry on")
        out.event("paused", kind=err.error.kind)
        code = exitcodes.TEMPFAIL
    except StopRun as err:
        out.warn(f"vid2sub: stopped: {err}")
        out.event("stopped")
        code = exitcodes.FAILED
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        out.warn("vid2sub: stopped. Run the same command with --skip-existing to carry on.")
        out.event("interrupted")
        code = exitcodes.TEMPFAIL
    finally:
        ctx.close()
    summary(out, results)
    if code == exitcodes.OK and any(r.status == "failed" for r in results):
        code = exitcodes.FAILED
    return code
```

`src/vid2sub/commands/detect.py`:
```python
"""`vid2sub detect`: the Review table without running anything (spec §10, §18.1)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse

from vid2sub import exitcodes
from vid2sub.commands import _common
from vid2sub.commands._common import CommandError, Output, add_common, audio_track
from vid2sub.engines.clients import quiet_libraries
from vid2sub.pipeline import RunOptions, detect_one
from vid2sub.subs import langs


def add_parser(sub) -> None:
    p = sub.add_parser("detect", help="detect each file's language and the models run would use")
    p.add_argument("inputs", nargs="*", metavar="INPUT")
    p.add_argument("--from-file", metavar="LIST")
    p.add_argument("--recursive", action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("--from", dest="source_lang", default="auto", metavar="LANG")
    p.add_argument("--audio-track", type=audio_track, metavar="N|LANG")
    p.add_argument("--allow-cloud", action="store_true")
    add_common(p)
    p.set_defaults(handler=run)


def _clock(ms: int) -> str:
    s = ms // 1000
    return f"{s // 3600}:{s % 3600 // 60:02d}:{s % 60:02d}"


def run(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    out = Output(args.json)
    quiet_libraries()
    try:
        config = _common.load(args)
        source = None if args.source_lang == "auto" else args.source_lang
        if source is not None:
            try:
                langs.get(source)
            except langs.UnknownLanguage as err:
                raise CommandError(exitcodes.USAGE, str(err)) from None
        inputs = _common.expand(args.inputs, recursive=args.recursive, from_file=args.from_file)
    except CommandError as err:
        return _common.fail(out, err)
    options = RunOptions(source_lang=source, audio_track=args.audio_track,
                         allow_cloud=args.allow_cloud)
    ctx = _common.new_context(config, options, out)
    failed = False
    try:
        for n, item in enumerate(inputs, 1):
            r = detect_one(item, ctx)
            d = r.detection
            out.event("detect", index=n, duration_ms=r.duration_ms, tracks=len(r.tracks),
                      language=(d.language if d else source), confidence=d.confidence if d else None,
                      uncertain=d.uncertain if d else False,
                      transcribe=r.transcribe.model if r.transcribe else None,
                      translate=r.translate.model if r.translate else None, kind=r.kind)
            out.say(f"{out.name(r.title, n)}  {_clock(r.duration_ms)}  audio: "
                    f"{'; '.join(r.tracks) or '?'}")
            if r.error:
                failed = True
                out.say(f"  ✗ {r.error if out.tty else r.kind}" + (f" → {r.hint}" if r.hint
                                                                     else ""))
                continue
            if d:
                out.say(f"  language: {d.language or '?'} ({d.confidence:.0%}, {d.detector})")
                if d.uncertain:
                    out.say(f"  ? {d.reason()} → run with --from LANG")
            for label, s in (("transcribe", r.transcribe), ("translate", r.translate)):
                if s is not None:
                    out.say(f"  {label}: {s.model or 'nothing usable'}"
                            + (f" ({s.warning})" if s.warning else ""))
    finally:
        ctx.close()
    return exitcodes.FAILED if failed else exitcodes.OK
```

```python
pytestmark = pytest.mark.media


@pytest.fixture
def setup(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    cfg = tmp_path / "vid2sub.toml"
    cfg.write_text(CONFIG)
    calls = []
    real = _common.new_context

    def with_fakes(config, options, output):

def test_translate_adds_a_language_to_an_existing_video(setup):
    cfg, clip, _ = setup
    assert main(["run", str(clip), "--from", "ja", "--to", "none", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 0
    assert main(["translate", str(clip), "--to", "zh-Hant", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 0
    assert clip.with_name("Show - S01E01.zh-TW.srt").exists()


def test_translate_stale_only_redoes_only_what_changed(setup):
    cfg, clip, calls = setup
    main(["run", str(clip), "--from", "ja", "--config", str(cfg)])
    ja = clip.with_name("Show - S01E01.ja.srt")
    text = ja.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    first_line = text.split("\n")[2]
    ja.write_text(text.replace(first_line, "直した", 1), encoding="utf-8")
    calls.clear()
    assert main(["translate", str(clip), "--to", "en", "--stale-only", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 0
    assert len(calls) == 1 and calls[0].count("(max") == 1


def test_translate_needs_a_sidecar_first(setup, capsys):
    cfg, clip, _ = setup
    assert main(["translate", str(clip), "--to", "en", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 1
    assert "vid2sub run" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_import_makes_an_existing_srt_the_source_then_translate_uses_it(setup, tmp_path):

    sub.write_text("1\n00:00:01,000 --> 00:00:02,000\nこんにちは\n\n"

    assert main(["import", str(sub), "--video", str(clip), "--lang", "ja",
                 "--config", str(cfg)]) == 0
    assert main(["translate", str(clip), "--to", "en", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 0
    assert "EN " in clip.with_name("Show - S01E01.en.srt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def test_write_puts_back_a_deleted_subtitle_file(setup):
    cfg, clip, _ = setup
    main(["run", str(clip), "--from", "ja", "--config", str(cfg)])
    en = clip.with_name("Show - S01E01.en.srt")
    en.unlink()
    assert main(["write", str(clip), "--config", str(cfg)]) == 0

def test_write_can_add_ass_files(setup):

    main(["run", str(clip), "--from", "ja", "--config", str(cfg)])
    assert main(["write", str(clip), "--format", "ass", "--config", str(cfg)]) == 0

def test_models_lists_what_is_configured_and_usable(setup, capsys, monkeypatch):
    cfg, _, _ = setup

        Candidate("gemma", "gemma-4-26b-a4b", "endpoint", False, "Spark not reachable")])
    assert main(["models", "--config", str(cfg), "--json"]) == 0
    rows = {e["name"]: e for e in map(json.loads, capsys.readouterr().out.splitlines())
            if e.get("event") == "model"}
    assert rows["fake"]["available"] and not rows["gemma"]["available"]
    assert rows["gemma"]["reason"] == "Spark not reachable"


def test_models_markdown_is_the_readme_table(capsys):
    assert main(["models", "--markdown"]) == 0
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == readme_table()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Refactor `run_one` around a shared guard, and add `fresh`**

In `src/vid2sub/pipeline.py`, replace `run_one` with a shared guard so every entry point
handles errors the same way:

```python
def _guarded(result: InputResult, work: Callable[[Path], None]) -> InputResult:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="vid2sub-") as tmp:
        try:
            work(Path(tmp))
        except (Paused, StopRun):
            raise
        except (EngineError, ToolMissing, youtube.YouTubeError, InputError) as err:
            _fail(result, str(err), getattr(err, "hint", ""),
                  getattr(err, "kind", type(err).__name__))
        except ToolFailed as err:
            _fail(result, f"{err.tool} couldn't process this file",
                  "check that it plays in a media player", "ToolFailed")
        except (youtube.NotYouTube, ProbeError, InputRefused, SubtitleError, GlossaryError,
                ConfigError, SidecarError, store.StoreError, WriteRefused, TranslationFailed,
                naming.UnknownTrack, langs.UnknownLanguage) as err:
            _fail(result, str(err), kind=type(err).__name__)
    return result


def run_one(source: str, ctx: RunContext) -> InputResult:
    result = InputResult(input=source)
    return _guarded(result, lambda scratch: _run(source, ctx, scratch, result))
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the other entry points**

Append to `src/vid2sub/pipeline.py`:
```python
def _existing(source: str, ctx: RunContext, scratch: Path, result: InputResult):
    video = resolve_input(source)
    if not video.is_file():
        raise InputError("there's no file at that path")
    result.title = video.name
    p = probe(video, ctx.tools, scratch=scratch)
    header = store.header_for(video, identify(video), p.duration_ms)
    sidecar, _ = store.load(video)
    if sidecar is None:
        raise InputError("this video has no vid2sub sidecar yet",
                         "run `vid2sub run` (or `vid2sub import`) on it first")
    sidecar, changed = store.check_external(video, header, sidecar)
    result.notes += [f"kept your outside edit to {t}" for t in changed]
    return video, header, sidecar


def translate_one(source: str, ctx: RunContext, *, fresh: bool) -> InputResult:
    result = InputResult(input=source)

    def work(scratch: Path) -> None:
        video, header, sidecar = _existing(source, ctx, scratch, result)
        src_track = store.source_track(sidecar)
        if src_track is None:
            raise InputError("the sidecar has no source subtitles",
                             "run `vid2sub run` or `vid2sub import` first")
        targets = [naming.target_track(t) for t in ctx.options.targets]
        sidecar = _translate_targets(video, header, sidecar, src_track, targets,
                                     _glossary(video, ctx.options), ctx, result, fresh=fresh)
        _write(video, header, sidecar, targets, ctx, result)
        result.status = "done"

    return _guarded(result, work)


def write_one(source: str, ctx: RunContext) -> InputResult:
    result = InputResult(input=source)

    def work(scratch: Path) -> None:
        video, header, sidecar = _existing(source, ctx, scratch, result)
        _write(video, header, sidecar, sidecar.tracks(), ctx, result)
        result.status = "done"

    return _guarded(result, work)


def import_one(sub_path: str, video_path: str, track: str | None,
               ctx: RunContext) -> InputResult:
    result = InputResult(input=sub_path)

    def work(scratch: Path) -> None:
        sub = resolve_input(sub_path)
        video = resolve_input(video_path)
        if not sub.is_file() or not video.is_file():
            raise InputError("both the subtitle file and the video must exist")
        result.title = video.name
        key = naming.target_track(track) if track else naming.track_from_subtitle_name(
            video.name, sub.name)
        if key is None:
            raise InputError("can't tell the subtitle's language from its name",
                             "pass --lang (for example --lang ja)")
        p = probe(video, ctx.tools, scratch=scratch)
        header = store.header_for(video, identify(video), p.duration_ms)
        existing, _ = store.load(video)
        current = store.source_track(existing) if existing else None
        role = "source" if current in (None, key) else "translation"
        sidecar = store.import_subtitles(video, header, sub, key, role=role)
        _, cues = store.current_cues(sidecar, key)
        result.notes.append(f"imported {len(cues)} cues as the {key} {role} track")
        result.status = "done"

    return _guarded(result, work)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the commands**

`src/vid2sub/commands/translate.py`:
```python
"""`vid2sub translate`: translate videos that already have a sidecar (spec §18.1)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from pathlib import Path

from vid2sub import exitcodes
from vid2sub.commands import _common
from vid2sub.commands._common import CommandError, Output, add_common
from vid2sub.commands.run import report, summary
from vid2sub.engines.clients import quiet_libraries
from vid2sub.pipeline import Paused, RunOptions, translate_one
from vid2sub.subs import naming


def add_parser(sub) -> None:
    p = sub.add_parser("translate", help="translate videos that already have subtitles")
    p.add_argument("inputs", nargs="*", metavar="INPUT")
    p.add_argument("--from-file", metavar="LIST")
    p.add_argument("--recursive", action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("--to", dest="targets", nargs="+", required=True, metavar="LANG")
    p.add_argument("--stale-only", action="store_true",
                   help="only cues whose source changed, and new ones")
    p.add_argument("--translate-model")
    p.add_argument("--glossary", type=Path, metavar="FILE")
    p.add_argument("--format", dest="formats", action="append", choices=["srt", "ass"])
    p.add_argument("--overwrite-foreign", action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("--replace-edited", action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("--allow-cloud", action="store_true")
    add_common(p)
    p.set_defaults(handler=run)


def run(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    out = Output(args.json)
    quiet_libraries()
    try:
        config = _common.load(args)
        try:
            targets = [naming.target_track(t) for t in args.targets]
        except naming.UnknownTrack as err:
            raise CommandError(exitcodes.USAGE, str(err)) from None
        inputs = _common.expand(args.inputs, recursive=args.recursive, from_file=args.from_file)
    except CommandError as err:
        return _common.fail(out, err)
    options = RunOptions(targets=targets, formats=args.formats or list(config.defaults.formats),
                         translate_model=args.translate_model, glossary=args.glossary,
                         overwrite_foreign=args.overwrite_foreign,
                         replace_edited=args.replace_edited, allow_cloud=args.allow_cloud)
    ctx = _common.new_context(config, options, out)
    results = []
    try:
        for n, item in enumerate(inputs, 1):
            result = translate_one(item, ctx, fresh=not args.stale_only)
            results.append(result)
            report(out, n, len(inputs), result)
    except Paused as err:
        out.warn(f"vid2sub: paused: {err}")
        return exitcodes.TEMPFAIL
    finally:
        ctx.close()
    summary(out, results)
    return exitcodes.FAILED if any(r.status == "failed" for r in results) else exitcodes.OK
```

`src/vid2sub/commands/write.py`:
```python
"""`vid2sub write`: re-write subtitle files from the sidecar's current versions (spec §18.1)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse

from vid2sub import exitcodes
from vid2sub.commands import _common
from vid2sub.commands._common import CommandError, Output, add_common
from vid2sub.commands.run import report, summary
from vid2sub.pipeline import RunOptions, write_one


def add_parser(sub) -> None:
    p = sub.add_parser("write", help="write subtitle files again from the sidecar")
    p.add_argument("inputs", nargs="*", metavar="INPUT")
    p.add_argument("--recursive", action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("--format", dest="formats", action="append", choices=["srt", "ass"])
    p.add_argument("--overwrite-foreign", action="store_true")
    add_common(p)
    p.set_defaults(handler=run)


def run(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    out = Output(args.json)
    try:
        config = _common.load(args)
        inputs = _common.expand(args.inputs, recursive=args.recursive)
    except CommandError as err:
        return _common.fail(out, err)
    options = RunOptions(formats=args.formats or list(config.defaults.formats),
                         overwrite_foreign=args.overwrite_foreign)
    ctx = _common.new_context(config, options, out)
    results = [write_one(item, ctx) for item in inputs]
    for n, result in enumerate(results, 1):
        report(out, n, len(results), result)
    summary(out, results)
    return exitcodes.FAILED if any(r.status == "failed" for r in results) else exitcodes.OK
```

`src/vid2sub/commands/import_.py`:
```python
"""`vid2sub import`: make an existing SRT or ASS a track of a video (spec §15.4, §18.1)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse

from vid2sub import exitcodes
from vid2sub.commands import _common
from vid2sub.commands._common import CommandError, Output, add_common
from vid2sub.commands.run import report
from vid2sub.pipeline import RunOptions, import_one


def add_parser(sub) -> None:
    p = sub.add_parser("import", help="use an existing SRT or ASS file as a video's subtitles")
    p.add_argument("subfile", metavar="SUBFILE")
    p.add_argument("--video", required=True, metavar="VIDEO")
    p.add_argument("--lang", metavar="LANG",
                   help="the subtitle's language (default: from its file name)")
    add_common(p)
    p.set_defaults(handler=run)


def run(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    out = Output(args.json)
    try:
        config = _common.load(args)
    except CommandError as err:
        return _common.fail(out, err)
    ctx = _common.new_context(config, RunOptions(), out)
    result = import_one(args.subfile, args.video, args.lang, ctx)
    report(out, 1, 1, result)
    return exitcodes.FAILED if result.status == "failed" else exitcodes.OK
```

`src/vid2sub/commands/models.py`:
```python
"""`vid2sub models`: the catalog, and what's usable on this machine now (spec §11, §18.1)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse

from vid2sub import detect as detecting
from vid2sub import exitcodes
from vid2sub.catalog import CATALOG, readme_table
from vid2sub.commands import _common
from vid2sub.commands._common import CommandError, Output, add_common
from vid2sub.engines.clients import KeyRing, quiet_libraries


def add_parser(sub) -> None:
    p = sub.add_parser("models", help="list models and whether each can run now")
    p.add_argument("--markdown", action="store_true", help="print the README's model table")
    p.add_argument("--offline", action="store_true", help="don't contact any server")
    add_common(p)
    p.set_defaults(handler=run)


def run(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    if args.markdown:
        print(readme_table(), end="")
        return exitcodes.OK
    out = Output(args.json)
    quiet_libraries()
    try:
        config = _common.load(args)
    except CommandError as err:
        return _common.fail(out, err)
    cands = detecting.candidates(config, KeyRing(), probe_endpoints=not args.offline)
    configured = set()
    for c in cands:
        entry = config.models[c.name]
        configured.add(c.catalog)
        title = CATALOG[c.catalog].title if c.catalog in CATALOG else "uncatalogued"
        out.event("model", name=c.name, catalog=c.catalog, task=entry.task,
                  available=c.available, reason=c.reason, cloud=c.cloud)
        mark = "✓" if c.available else "✗"
        out.say(f"{mark} {c.name}: {title} ({entry.task}{', cloud' if c.cloud else ''})"
                + (f" — {c.reason}" if c.reason else ""))
    for cid, entry in CATALOG.items():
        if cid not in configured:
            out.event("model", name=None, catalog=cid, task=entry.task, available=False,
                      reason="not in vid2sub.toml", cloud=entry.cloud)
            out.say(f"· {entry.title} ({entry.task}): not in vid2sub.toml")
    return exitcodes.OK
```

In `src/vid2sub/cli.py`, set
`COMMAND_MODULES: list[str] = ["run", "detect", "translate", "import_", "write", "models"]`.

```python
import json

from vid2sub import config as c
from vid2sub.cli import main
from vid2sub.commands import doctor
from vid2sub.media.tools import Tools


def test_config_init_prints_the_starter_and_writes_nothing(capsys, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    assert main(["config", "init"]) == 0
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == c.STARTER
    assert list(tmp_path.iterdir()) == []


def test_config_path_prints_where_vid2sub_looks(capsys, tmp_path):
    assert main(["config", "path", "--config", str(tmp_path / "x.toml")]) == 0
    assert capsys.readouterr().out.strip() == str(tmp_path / "x.toml")


def test_doctor_reports_keys_by_presence_only(tmp_path, monkeypatch, capsys):
    cfg = tmp_path / "vid2sub.toml"
    cfg.write_text(c.STARTER)
    monkeypatch.setenv("SPARK_API_KEY", "supersecretvalue")
    monkeypatch.delenv("OPENAI_API_KEY", raising=False)
    main(["doctor", "--config", str(cfg), "--json", "--offline"])
    out = capsys.readouterr().out
    assert "supersecretvalue" not in out
    rows = {e["check"]: e for e in map(json.loads, out.splitlines())}
    assert rows["key SPARK_API_KEY"]["ok"] and not rows["key OPENAI_API_KEY"]["ok"]


def test_doctor_exits_69_without_ffmpeg(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(doctor.Tools, "find", classmethod(
        lambda cls: Tools(ffmpeg=None, ffprobe=None, deno=None)))

def test_doctor_exits_78_for_a_broken_config(tmp_path):
    cfg = tmp_path / "vid2sub.toml"
    cfg.write_text("[server]\ncolour = 1\n")
    assert main(["doctor", "--config", str(cfg), "--offline"]) == 78
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/vid2sub/commands/config.py`:
```python
"""`vid2sub config`: print a starter vid2sub.toml, or where vid2sub looks for it (spec §5).

vid2sub never writes the settings file: `config init` prints it for you to save.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys

from vid2sub import exitcodes
from vid2sub.config import STARTER, config_path


def add_parser(sub) -> None:
    p = sub.add_parser("config", help="print a starter vid2sub.toml, or where it's read from")
    actions = p.add_subparsers(dest="action", metavar="ACTION", required=True,
                               parser_class=type(p))
    init = actions.add_parser("init", help="print a starter vid2sub.toml (save it yourself)")
    init.set_defaults(handler=_init)
    where = actions.add_parser("path", help="print where vid2sub looks for vid2sub.toml")
    where.add_argument("--config", metavar="PATH")
    where.set_defaults(handler=_path)


def _init(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    sys.stdout.write(STARTER)
    return exitcodes.OK


def _path(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    print(config_path(args.config))
    return exitcodes.OK
```

`src/vid2sub/commands/doctor.py`:
```python
"""`vid2sub doctor`: is everything vid2sub needs here? (spec §18.1)

Python, ffmpeg and ffprobe are essential; deno (YouTube), yt-dlp's age and the Mac extra are
warnings. Endpoints serving local models are asked for their model list (not with --offline);
cloud endpoints are never contacted. Keys are reported by presence only, never their values.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime as dt
import os
import sys
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass

from vid2sub import __version__, exitcodes
from vid2sub import detect as detecting
from vid2sub.commands._common import Output
from vid2sub.config import Config, ConfigError, config_path, is_local, load_config
from vid2sub.engines import clients
from vid2sub.engines.base import EngineError
from vid2sub.media.tools import Tools, version


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Check:
    name: str
    ok: bool
    detail: str
    hint: str = ""
    essential: bool = True


def add_parser(sub) -> None:
    p = sub.add_parser("doctor", help="check that everything vid2sub needs is here")
    p.add_argument("--config", metavar="PATH")
    p.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("--offline", action="store_true", help="don't contact any server")
    p.set_defaults(handler=run)


def _tool(tools: Tools, name: str, minimum: tuple[int, ...], essential: bool, hint: str) -> Check:
    exe = getattr(tools, name)
    if not exe:
        return Check(name, False, "not found", hint, essential)
    found = version(exe)
    ok = found is not None and found >= minimum
    shown = ".".join(map(str, found)) if found else "unknown version"
    return Check(name, ok, shown, "" if ok else hint, essential)


def _ytdlp() -> Check:
    try:
        from yt_dlp.version import __version__ as v
    except ImportError:
        return Check("yt-dlp", False, "not installed", "reinstall vid2sub", essential=False)
    try:
        age = (dt.date.today() - dt.date(*map(int, v.split(".")[:3]))).days
    except ValueError:
        return Check("yt-dlp", True, v, essential=False)
    ok = age <= 90
    return Check("yt-dlp", ok, f"{v} ({age} days old)",
                 "" if ok else "uv tool upgrade vid2sub --upgrade-package yt-dlp",
                 essential=False)


def checks(config_arg: str | None, environ: Mapping[str, str] = os.environ,
           tools: Tools | None = None, *, offline: bool = False) -> tuple[list[Check], bool]:
    """All checks, and whether the config loaded."""
    tools = tools or Tools.find()
    out = [Check("python", sys.version_info >= (3, 12), sys.version.split()[0],
                 "vid2sub needs Python 3.12 or later"),
           Check("vid2sub", True, __version__),
           _tool(tools, "ffmpeg", (6,), True, "install ffmpeg 6 or later"),
           _tool(tools, "ffprobe", (6,), True, "ffprobe comes with ffmpeg 6 or later"),
           _tool(tools, "deno", (2, 3), False, "install deno 2.3+ for YouTube (https://deno.com)"),
           _ytdlp()]
    if sys.platform == "darwin":
        ok = detecting.mlx_available()
        out.append(Check("mlx-qwen3-asr", ok, "installed" if ok else "not installed",
                         "" if ok else "for Qwen3-ASR on this Mac: install vid2sub[mac]",
                         essential=False))
    path = config_path(config_arg, environ)
    try:
        config = load_config(path)
    except ConfigError as err:
        out.append(Check("vid2sub.toml", False, str(err), "fix it, or start from "
                         "`vid2sub config init`"))
        return out, False
    out.append(Check("vid2sub.toml", True, "found" if path.exists() else "not found: defaults",
                     "" if path.exists() else "`vid2sub config init` prints a starter",
                     essential=False))
    out += _endpoints(config, environ, offline)
    return out, True


def _endpoints(config: Config, environ: Mapping[str, str], offline: bool) -> list[Check]:
    keys = clients.KeyRing(environ=environ)
    out = []
    for name, ep in config.endpoints.items():
        if ep.key_env:
            present = bool(environ.get(ep.key_env))
            out.append(Check(f"key {ep.key_env}", present, "set" if present else "not set",
                             "" if present else f"export {ep.key_env} in your shell",
                             essential=False))
        local = any(m.endpoint == name and is_local(m, config) for m in config.models.values())
        if not local or offline:
            reason = "cloud: not contacted" if not local else "not contacted (--offline)"
            out.append(Check(f"endpoint {name}", True, reason, essential=False))
            continue
        try:
            served = clients.list_models(name, ep, keys)
            out.append(Check(f"endpoint {name}", True, f"serves {len(served)} model(s)",
                             essential=False))
        except EngineError as err:
            out.append(Check(f"endpoint {name}", False, str(err), err.hint, essential=False))
    return out


def run(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    out = Output(args.json)
    clients.quiet_libraries()
    results, config_ok = checks(args.config, offline=args.offline)
    for c in results:
        out.event("check", check=c.name, ok=c.ok, essential=c.essential,
                  detail=c.detail if c.name != "vid2sub.toml" else ("ok" if c.ok else "invalid"))
        if out.json:
            continue
        mark = "✓" if c.ok else ("✗" if c.essential else "!")
        out.say(f"{mark} {c.name}: {c.detail}" + (f" → {c.hint}" if c.hint else ""))
    if not config_ok:
        return exitcodes.CONFIG
    if any(c.essential and not c.ok for c in results):
        return exitcodes.UNAVAILABLE
    return exitcodes.OK
```

Note `Check("key …")` JSON rows key on the name `key SPARK_API_KEY`, which is the variable's
*name*: fine to show. Because the event uses `check=c.name`, the test reads
`rows["key SPARK_API_KEY"]`; the `json.loads` rows come one per line (the doctor emits nothing
else in `--json` mode).

In `src/vid2sub/cli.py`, set
`COMMAND_MODULES: list[str] = ["run", "detect", "translate", "import_", "write", "models", "doctor", "config"]`.

Expected: all pass.

Run: `uv run pytest tests/test_readme.py` → FAIL (no markers).

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the README**

`README.md` (generate the table block with `uv run vid2sub models --markdown`):
````markdown
# vid2sub

Subtitles for videos and YouTube, made on your own machine. vid2sub extracts the audio,
transcribes it with timestamps in the video's language, translates it, and writes SRT (and
optionally ASS) next to the video, named the way Plex and Jellyfin expect. It runs models on
your computer, on a machine you run (such as a DGX Spark), or, only when you allow it for a run,
on OpenAI.

Status: milestone M1, the CLI. The web editor arrives with M2. Design:
[`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-vid2sub-design.md`](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-vid2sub-design.md).

## Install

You need [uv](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/), ffmpeg 6 or later, and, for YouTube, deno 2.3 or later.

| | macOS (Apple Silicon, 14+) | Windows | Linux |
|---|---|---|---|
| uv | `brew install uv` | `winget install astral-sh.uv` | `curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh \| sh` |
| ffmpeg | `brew install ffmpeg` | `winget install Gyan.FFmpeg` | your package manager |
| deno | `brew install deno` | `winget install DenoLand.Deno` | `curl -fsSL https://deno.land/install.sh \| sh` |

Then install the CLI from a clone (release wheels arrive with M4). None of this needs Node:

```bash
git clone https://github.com/chendaniely/vid2sub && cd vid2sub
uv tool install --editable '.[mac]'   # on a Mac: Qwen3-ASR in-process
uv tool install --editable .          # elsewhere
vid2sub doctor
```

`--editable` follows your clone, so `git pull` updates it. To work inside the project's own
environment instead: `uv sync --extra mac` and `uv run vid2sub …`.

## Configure

```bash
vid2sub config init > "$(vid2sub config path)"   # then edit it
```

`vid2sub.toml` names endpoints and models; it never holds a key, only the *names* of the
environment variables that do. Put keys in your shell's environment (on macOS and Linux, a file
your shell sources; on Windows, `setx NAME value` and a new terminal).

- **The DGX Spark:** start local-ai's tunnel (`make tunnel`, see local-ai's
  `website/how-to/pi.md`) and export `SPARK_API_KEY`. The starter config points at
  `http://127.0.0.1:9100/v1`.
- **OpenAI:** export `OPENAI_API_KEY`. Cloud models are used only on runs with `--allow-cloud`.
- **LM Studio or Ollama** (for translation): add an endpoint with its OpenAI-compatible URL.

## Use

```bash
vid2sub run "Show - S01E02.mkv" --to en              # detect the language, transcribe, translate
vid2sub run ~/TV/Show/Season\ 1 --from yue --to en   # a whole season, Cantonese
vid2sub run ~/TV/Show --recursive --skip-existing     # carry on where a run stopped
vid2sub run https://youtu.be/VIDEO_ID --to none       # English captions for an English video
vid2sub detect ~/TV/Show/Season\ 1                    # what run would do, without running
vid2sub import fansub.ja.srt --video ep1.mkv          # use an existing SRT as the source
vid2sub translate ep1.mkv --to zh-Hant --stale-only   # translate only what changed
vid2sub write ep1.mkv --format ass                     # add ASS files from the sidecar
vid2sub models                                         # what can run on this machine now
```

A `vid2sub.glossary.toml` in a show's folder helps with names:

```toml
description = "Detective Conan, a Japanese anime"
[terms]
"工藤新一" = "Shinichi Kudo"
```

Files written next to `Show - S01E02.mkv`: `Show - S01E02.ja.srt`, `Show - S01E02.en.srt`
(Cantonese is `.zh-HK`, Mandarin `.zh-CN` or `.zh-TW`), and `Show - S01E02.vid2sub.jsonl`, the
sidecar that keeps every version. vid2sub never overwrites a subtitle file it didn't write.

Exit codes: 0 done; 1 an input failed; 64 usage; 69 a tool or model is missing; 75 interrupted or
a connection dropped (run the same command with `--skip-existing`); 78 a settings problem.

## Models

<!-- models:start -->
(paste the output of `uv run vid2sub models --markdown` here)
<!-- models:end -->

Upgrade yt-dlp when YouTube changes: `uv tool upgrade vid2sub --upgrade-package yt-dlp`.

## Develop

```bash
make setup    # uv sync --group dev
make test     # pytest (media tests need ffmpeg)
make lint
make live     # opt-in tests against real models; see tests/README.md
```
````

