In `CLAUDE.md`, replace the Commands section with:
```markdown
## Commands

- `make setup` · `make test` · `make lint` · `make live` (opt-in, real models) ·
  `make readme` (refresh the README's model table from the catalog).
- The CLI: `uv run vid2sub --help`. Media tests need ffmpeg; set `VID2SUB_REQUIRE_FFMPEG=1` to
  make them fail instead of skip.

## Layout

- `src/vid2sub/subs/`, `parse/`, `catalog.py`: pure (they run in the browser from M4).
- `media/`: ffmpeg, probing, VAD (vendored Silero in `media/_vendor/`), chunking, YouTube.
- `engines/`: the Engine protocol; `clients.py` is the only network client factory.
- `translate/`, `detect.py`, `store.py`, `pipeline.py`, `commands/`.
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write CI**

`.github/workflows/ci.yml`:
```yaml
name: ci
on:
  push:
  pull_request:
  workflow_dispatch:
concurrency:
  group: ci-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true
permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  python:
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        os: [ubuntu-24.04, macos-14, windows-2022]
        python: ["3.12", "3.14"]
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    timeout-minutes: 30
    env:
      UV_FROZEN: "1"
      VID2SUB_REQUIRE_FFMPEG: "1"
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with: { persist-credentials: false }
      - uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@c18668ad3cf93ea998bef934396af7bb5c839dc7 # v10.2.0
        with: { version: "0.13.1" }
      - name: ffmpeg (Linux)
        if: runner.os == 'Linux'
        run: sudo apt-get update && sudo apt-get install -y ffmpeg
      - name: ffmpeg (macOS)
        if: runner.os == 'macOS'
        run: brew install ffmpeg
      - name: ffmpeg (Windows)
        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
        run: choco install ffmpeg -y --no-progress
      - run: uv sync --group dev --python ${{ matrix.python }}
      - run: uv run --python ${{ matrix.python }} pytest
      - run: uv run ruff check src tests

  floor:
    # The pure packages run in Pyodide later (Python 3.12, pydantic 2.10.5): test them there.
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 15
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with: { persist-credentials: false }
      - uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@c18668ad3cf93ea998bef934396af7bb5c839dc7 # v10.2.0
        with: { version: "0.13.1" }
      - run: uv sync --frozen --group dev --python 3.12
      - run: uv pip install "pydantic==2.10.5"
      - run: >-
          uv run --no-sync pytest tests/test_purity.py tests/test_langs.py tests/test_text.py
          tests/test_model.py tests/test_rules_lint.py tests/test_build.py tests/test_formats.py
          tests/test_naming.py tests/test_sidecar.py tests/test_rendition.py
          tests/test_glossary.py tests/test_scripts.py tests/test_translate_shape.py
          tests/test_parse_words.py tests/test_parse_whispercpp.py
          tests/test_parse_openai_qwen.py tests/test_catalog.py

  leaks:
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 10
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
        with: { fetch-depth: 0, persist-credentials: false }
      - name: download gitleaks
        run: curl -fsSL --retry 3 --max-time 120 -o gitleaks.tar.gz "https://github.com/gitleaks/gitleaks/releases/download/v8.30.1/gitleaks_8.30.1_linux_x64.tar.gz"
      - name: check gitleaks against its pinned checksum, then unpack it
        run: |
          echo "551f6fc83ea457d62a0d98237cbad105af8d557003051f41f3e7ca7b3f2470eb  gitleaks.tar.gz" | sha256sum -c -
          tar -xzf gitleaks.tar.gz gitleaks
      - name: gitleaks over the whole history
        run: ./gitleaks git --redact --no-banner -v .
```

The action SHAs and the gitleaks checksum are `recordings`' (its `ci.yml` at
`64b75513876207f6e4d0ddfd7191497551c6e200`); `uv` is pinned to the version on Dan's Mac.

`.github/dependabot.yml`:
```yaml
version: 2
updates:
  - package-ecosystem: github-actions
    directory: /
    schedule: { interval: weekly, day: friday }
  - package-ecosystem: uv
    directory: /
    schedule: { interval: weekly, day: friday }
```

> as Phase 2's first task, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-23-vid2sub-phase-2.md` (Dan,
> [Q&A item 7](../specs/2026-10-14-vid2sub-qa.md)).

This task needs Dan: his Mac, the Spark, Plex and Jellyfin. The implementer prepares and records;
Dan runs the parts that touch his media and servers.

**Files:**

- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-vid2sub-design.md` (§29 results)

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the opt-in Spark test**

`tests/test_live_spark.py`:
```python
"""The real Spark (opt-in): VID2SUB_LIVE_SPARK=1 with `make tunnel` running and SPARK_API_KEY
exported. Checks whisper.cpp's verbose_json and Gemma's structured output against vid2sub's
parsers and schemas (spec §29)."""

import os

import pytest
from helpers import SPEECH

from vid2sub.config import Config, Endpoint, ModelEntry
from vid2sub.engines.base import Chunk
from vid2sub.engines.clients import KeyRing, RunPolicy
from vid2sub.engines.endpoint import EndpointEngine
from vid2sub.media.decode import encode_flac, read_wav
from vid2sub.media.tools import Tools
from vid2sub.subs.glossary import EMPTY_GLOSSARY
from vid2sub.subs.model import Cue
from vid2sub.translate.run import make_ask, translate_cues

pytestmark = [pytest.mark.live, pytest.mark.skipif(os.environ.get("VID2SUB_LIVE_SPARK") != "1",
                                                   reason="needs VID2SUB_LIVE_SPARK=1")]
CONFIG = Config(
    endpoints={"spark": Endpoint(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:9100/v1", key_env="SPARK_API_KEY")},
    models={"turbo": ModelEntry(catalog="whisper-large-v3-turbo", endpoint="spark",
                                model="whisper-large-v3-turbo", parser="whisper.cpp", local=True),
            "gemma": ModelEntry(catalog="gemma-4-26b-a4b", endpoint="spark",
                                model="gemma-4-26b-a4b", task="translate", local=True,
                                request_extra={"chat_template_kwargs":
                                               {"enable_thinking": False}})})


def test_the_sparks_whisper_returns_timed_words(tmp_path):
    samples = read_wav(SPEECH)
    engine = EndpointEngine("turbo", CONFIG.models["turbo"], CONFIG, RunPolicy(), KeyRing())
    chunk = Chunk(0, samples, lambda s: encode_flac(s, Tools.find(), scratch=tmp_path))
    words = [w for s in engine.transcribe(chunk, language="en", prompt="").parsed.segments
             for w in s.words]
    assert len(words) > 10 and all(w.end_ms >= w.start_ms for w in words)


def test_gemma_translates_a_batch_in_the_required_shape():
    cues = [Cue(id=f"s{i}", start_ms=i * 2000, end_ms=i * 2000 + 1500, text=t)
            for i, t in enumerate(["おはようございます。", "今日はいい天気ですね。", "また明日。"])]
    ask = make_ask("gemma", CONFIG.models["gemma"], CONFIG, RunPolicy(), KeyRing())
    out = translate_cues(cues, source_lang="ja", target_track="en", glossary=EMPTY_GLOSSARY,
                         ask=ask)
    assert [c.src for c in out] == [("s0",), ("s1",), ("s2",)]
```

Run (Dan, with the tunnel open): `VID2SUB_LIVE_SPARK=1 uv run pytest -m live tests/test_live_spark.py -v`.

2. A Japanese episode: `vid2sub run "<episode>" --to en` (detection on). Watch the `.en.srt`.
3. Naming: copy one `.srt` to `.zh-CN`, `.zh-TW`, `.zh-HK` and `.ja` names next to a test video;
   note what Plex and Jellyfin each display.

   download in the YouTube folder.
5. A season: `vid2sub run "<season folder>" --from ja --to en`; then the same command with
   `--skip-existing` does nothing and exits 0.
